*Elize Herrewijnen,Benedetta Muscato,Gizem Gezici,Fosca Giannotti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇观点论文，针对大语言模型（LLM）自我解释（self-explanations）的可信度问题展开论述。作者提出核心论点：自我解释可能高度合理（plausible）、可信度存疑（questionably faithful）、但高度可操作（actionable）。论文从传统可解释人工智能（XAI）视角指出标准评估协议在评估LLM自我解释时的局限，提出评估其合理性与忠实性的实用准则，并主张评估应超越这两项标准扩展到可操作性（actionability），以支持各类利益相关者做出知情决策并采取恰当行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能够生成解释其自身决策的自然语言自我解释，这成为可解释人工智能（XAI）领域一个很有前景的方向。然而，自我解释虽然往往表面上看似合理，但其是否真实反映模型的底层推理过程仍是一个悬而未决的问题。作者旨在揭示这些自我解释可能"看似合理但内在不忠实"的矛盾特性，并改进现有评估框架。

Method: 本文为观点性质论文：1）从传统XAI视角出发，识别当前LLM自我解释标准评估协议的局限性；2）提出评估自我解释合理性（plausibility）与忠实性（faithfulness）的实用准则；3）论证评估标准应进一步扩展至可操作性（actionability），关注LLM理性化（rationalization）能力在支持知情决策与恰当行动方面的应用价值。

Result: 论文提出了一个三重视角的核心判断：自我解释"高度合理、忠实性存疑、高度可操作"，并系统梳理了标准评估协议的不足，同时提出了聚焦合理性、忠实性与可操作性的扩展评估准则框架。

Conclusion: 作者认为自我解释的评估不应仅局限于传统XAI的合理性与忠实性标准，而应整合可操作性维度，使评估能够覆盖LLM理性化能力在跨利益相关者知情决策与恰当行动中的应用价值，从而更全面地衡量自我解释的实际效用。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate natural language explanations that rationalize their own decisions, a phenomenon commonly referred to as self-explanations. Such explanations have emerged as a promising direction for explainable artificial intelligence (XAI), particularly for interpreting LLM behavior. However, while self-explanations often appear plausible, whether they faithfully reflect a model's underlying reasoning process remains an open question. In this opinion paper, we argue that self-explanations can be highly plausible, questionably faithful, and yet highly actionable. From a traditional XAI perspective, we identify the limitations of standard evaluation protocols for LLM-generated self-explanations and propose practical guidelines for assessing their plausibility and faithfulness.Moreover, we argue that evaluation should extend beyond these criteria to actionability, highlighting applications of LLM rationalization capabilities that support informed decision-making and appropriate action across diverse stakeholders.

</details>


### [133] [$Φ$-Bench: Can Large Language Models Engineer the Infrastructure That Powers Them?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10226)
*Leilei Ding,Shumin Wang,Yuting Huang,Fanqi Wan,Yinmin Zhang,Qi Han,Yiming Xu,Feiyuan Zhang,Xiaomeng Chu,Guoliang You,Wuyang Zhang,Daxin Jiang,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Φ-Bench，一个用于系统评估大语言模型（LLM）在LLM基础设施工程中能力的基准，覆盖从内核级函数补全到端到端系统优化的多种任务，并通过实验揭示前沿LLM的能力与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要聚焦于孤立内核、预定义运算符或预指定优化目标，无法评估LLM进行开放式、长周期LLM基础设施工程的能力。

Method: 基于前沿研究中优化问题和真实代码仓库，构建Φ-Bench基准，系统覆盖LLM基础设施栈，任务从局部内核级函数补全到长周期实现和端到端系统优化，复杂度各异。

Result: 对前沿LLM的大量实验揭示了它们在复杂LLM基础设施工程中的当前能力与局限性。

Conclusion: 研究提供了关于未来AI基础设施自主优化所面临挑战的洞察。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in reasoning and code generation, raising the prospect that they could assist in developing and optimizing the very infrastructure that powers them. However, existing benchmarks mainly focus on isolated kernels, predefined operators, or pre-specified optimization targets, and therefore fail to evaluate the ability of LLMs to perform open-ended, long-horizon LLM infrastructure engineering. To address this gap, we present $Φ$-Bench, a benchmark for systematically evaluating LLMs on engineering the LLM infrastructure stack. Derived from optimization problems studied in frontier research and grounded in real-world code repositories, $Φ$-Bench provides broad coverage of the LLM infrastructure stack and spans tasks of varying complexity, ranging from localized kernel-level function completion to long-horizon implementation and end-to-end system optimization. Extensive experiments on frontier LLMs reveal their current capabilities and limitations in engineering complex LLM infrastructure, offering insights into the challenges that remain on the path toward autonomous optimization of future AI infrastructure.

</details>


### [134] [The Answer Path and the Grounding Instruction in LLM Question Answering over Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10237)
*Arquimedes Canedo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统地分析了图检索增强生成（Graph RAG）流水线中的四个设计选择——三元组选择、语法、排序和接地指令——在六个大语言模型和两个知识图谱问答基准上的影响。研究发现只有两个选择真正影响答案质量：答案路径是否出现在上下文中（移除路径会损失图的大部分价值），以及接地指令（在没有事实时会导致 F1 降低 8.63 倍）。语法、三元组排序和子图规模对多跳深度无显著影响。研究还指出了比较中的方法论缺陷，并撤回了自己发现的一个虚假结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Graph RAG 流水线的性能受多种设计决策影响，但此前缺乏对这些决策（三元组选取、语法、排序、接地指令）进行系统、受控的消融研究。作者希望通过隔离变量（保持三元组数量固定、使用黄金 SPARQL 提取子图以排除检索器干扰）来精确衡量每个组件对多跳问答准确率的独立贡献，并批判性地审视自身评估设计中的方法论陷阱。

Method: 作者构建了一个可控的 Graph RAG 流水线，对四个设计维度进行逐项消融：(1) 答案路径是否包含在提示中——保持三元组总数固定，用无关实体的三元组替换非链条三元组；移除链条 vs. 保留链条；(2) 接地指令——告知模型仅使用提供的事实作答，对比无上下文基线；(3) 语法格式；(4) 三元组排序。子图来自黄金 SPARQL（无检索器），因此精度仅描述所构建的上下文。实验覆盖六个 LLM 与两个 KGQA 基准，采用 F1 指标评估。作者还通过专门的对照实验（对上下文臂和无上下文基线同时应用/不应用指令）来甄别虚假结论。

Result: 两项决策显著影响结果：答案路径存在性——用无关实体替换非链条三元组仅改变 F1 +0.003，而移除链条则损失图中大部分价值（即检索预算应投入召回而非精度）；接地指令——无事实时若不加指令 F1 为 0.299，加指令降至 0.035（下降 8.63 倍），但该数字来自空上下文臂而非实际工作流水线。语法、三元组排序和子图规模在多跳深度无可测量影响。同时发现了一个由不均衡应用指令造成的"图上下文在多跳深度有损"的虚假结果，并予以撤稿。

Conclusion: Graph RAG 中真正影响多跳问答质量的是"答案路径是否被检索到"（召回决定价值，精度无关）以及"接地指令"的合理使用；语法、排序和子图规模意义不大。研究强调：将接地指令应用到上下文臂但不应用到无上下文基线会产生虚假结论，需要对称的实验设计。指令（决定输出格式）与正确上下文之间无法用格式敏感的评分器公平比较，故将这一对比报告为开放问题而非数值。

Abstract: A graph retrieval-augmented generation pipeline chooses which triples to put in the prompt, a syntax to write them in, an order to write them in, and a sentence telling the model what to do with them. We vary all four over six large language models and two knowledge-graph question answering benchmarks. Two of the four choices move the answer and the other two are flat. The first is whether the answer path, the triples needed to reach the answer, is in the prompt at all. Holding the number of triples fixed and replacing every triple that is not on the chain with material from an unrelated entity changes answer accuracy by +0.003 F1, while removing the chain costs most of what the graph was worth. Retrieval budget belongs on recall, and precision in the range we can test buys nothing. There is no retriever here: subgraphs come from gold SPARQL, so precision describes the context we build, not a system setting. The second is the grounding instruction. With no facts in the prompt, telling a model to answer using only the provided facts drops F1 from 0.299 to 0.035, a factor of 8.63. That figure describes an evaluation with an empty context arm rather than a working pipeline, and an experiment that applies the instruction to its context arm but not to its no-context baseline manufactures a spurious finding that graph context hurts at depth. We found one in our own results and retract it. Syntax, triple order and subgraph size produce no effect we can measure at multi-hop depth. The comparison that would price the grounding instruction against correct context is not measurable with a format-sensitive scorer, because the instruction determines the response format; we report it as an open contrast rather than a number.

</details>


### [135] [Two-Token Features and Small-Large Ensembles for VLM Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10244)
*Eli Schwartz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SHROOM-Visions 2026共享任务（字符级VLM幻觉检测）的系统方案。系统将一个小型4B参数VLM微调为逐token分类器，读取其自身隐藏状态中的双token特征，并在推理时与约400B的零样本VLM裁判进行集成。两个组件均使用现成的OCR识别可见图像文本。采用大型模型生成的合成幻觉数据作为集成多样性来源，并通过验证集选择特征层、训练数据和OCR锚定。官方提交在隐藏测试集上达到平均Cor 0.487 / Cor-lbl 0.387，在主要Cor-lbl指标上分别位列EN第6/28、FR第6/21、IT第8/21、ZH第7/22名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多模态大语言模型（VLM）中的幻觉问题，特别是字符级（character-level）幻觉检测任务，作者希望构建一个高效的检测系统。核心动机是利用小型模型的高效性与超大规模零样本VLM的强判别能力进行互补集成，结合OCR对图像中可见文本进行锚定，从而在多项语言上取得稳定表现。

Method: 方法包含两个核心组件：(1) 将一个4B参数的小型VLM微调为逐token分类器，通过其自身隐藏状态的双token特征进行预测；(2) 在推理时将约400B参数的零样本VLM裁判与前者集成。两个组件均采用现成的OCR技术对图像中的可见文本进行识别与锚定。此外，利用大型模型生成的合成幻觉数据作为集成多样性来源，并通过验证集对特征层、训练数据选择和OCR锚定策略进行超参数选择。

Result: 官方提交在隐藏测试集上取得平均Cor 0.487 / Cor-lbl 0.387。在任务的主要Cor-lbl指标上，系统在EN语种排名第6/28，FR语种第6/21，IT语种第8/21，ZH语种第7/22。

Conclusion: 该混合架构（小型微调分类器+超大零样本VLM裁判+OCR锚定+合成数据集成）在多个语种上均取得稳定的中上排名，证明了小模型与大模型集成、以及OCR锚定策略在字符级VLM幻觉检测中的有效性。

Abstract: We present our system for the SHROOM-Visions 2026 shared task on character-level VLM hallucination detection. A small ($4$B-parameter) VLM is fine-tuned as a per-token classifier reading a two-token feature from its own hidden states, and is ensembled with a $\sim$400B zero-shot VLM judge at prediction time. Both components see off-the-shelf OCR of any visible in-image text. We use synthetic hallucination data generated by the large model as a source of ensemble diversity, and use validation to select feature layer, training data and OCR grounding. Our official entry reaches mean Cor $0.487$ / Cor-lbl $0.387$ on the hidden test set, placing $6$th/$28$ (EN), $6$th/$21$ (FR), $8$th/$21$ (IT) and $7$th/$22$ (ZH) on the task's primary Cor-lbl metric.

</details>


### [136] [DiSCo: A Distribution-First Steering and Cultural Prior Evaluation Framework for Measuring Cultural Preference Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10253)
*Bhuvan Arora,Devesh Saraogi,Sravya Varada,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DiSCo框架，一种以分布为先的强制选择评估框架，用于隔离LLM的默认文化先验并通过四级上下文梯度(C0-C3)测试可引导性。基于304个跨12文化的条目评估6个指令微调LLM，发现默认文化偏好高度集中（英美占据约35%选择），且提示词引导反而扩大高低资源文化间选择差距，事实注入近乎无效，证明文化偏好偏差无法单靠提示词个性化解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文化基准仅评估单一"正确答案"上的准确率，难以刻画LLM在多种文化答案均有效时的默认文化偏好先验，且混淆了默认偏好与上下文驱动的适应。这阻碍了对全球部署LLM文化公平性的理解与改进。

Method: 设计DiSCo分布优先强制选择评估框架：通过强制选择隔离默认文化先验，并用四级上下文梯度(C0-C3)测试可引导性。基于BLEnD构建DiSCo-Bench（304项，覆盖12种文化），对6个不同指令微调LLM进行评估，包括默认选择分析、提示词引导和显式文化事实注入实验。

Result: 默认文化先验高度集中，英美仅占12种文化中的2种却吸收约35%的全部选择；提示词引导持续扩大高资源与低资源文化的选择差距；注入显式文化事实几乎不产生分布扰动。

Conclusion: 文化偏好偏差存在固有的默认先验集中问题，且无法通过提示词个性化或事实注入解决，需要从根本上改变训练策略或架构，而非依赖推理时的提示词工程。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in globally used assistants, yet their default choices in culturally grounded everyday situations can systematically favour some cultures over others, affecting localisation, user trust, and equitable behaviour. Existing cultural benchmarks evaluate accuracy against a single "correct" answer, making it difficult to characterise an LLM's cultural preference prior when multiple culturally grounded responses are all valid; they also conflate default preferences with context-driven adaptation. We propose DiSCo, a distribution-first forced-choice evaluation framework that isolates default cultural priors and tests steerability via a four-level context gradient (C0--C3). Using DiSCo-Bench (304 items) derived from BLEnD spanning 12 cultures, we evaluate six diverse instruction-tuned LLMs. Default priors are heavily concentrated, with UK and US together absorbing approximately 35\% of all selections despite representing only 2 of 12 cultures. Most critically, prompt-based steering consistently widens the selection gap between high- and low-resource cultures, and injecting explicit cultural facts produces negligible distributional disruption, confirming that cultural preference bias cannot be resolved through prompt-based personalisation alone.

</details>


### [137] [KVShareArena: KV-Cache Reuse Across Contexts and Model Checkpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10266)
*Xi Shi,Qian Lou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了KVShareArena基准，用于系统评测跨提示上下文和模型检查点的KV缓存复用。研究发现：仅修正位置（无需重计算）在大多数情况下足够，但当问题需要同时整合多个来源时，需要付出重编码或训练代价的方法才能恢复一半到三分之二的性能差距；未修复的缓存甚至可能比无缓存更差。跨检查点的场景下，免训练方法几乎不受影响，而特定检查点训练的自适应器会损失质量。该基准以pip包形式发布，附带自动化提交流程和公开排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统仅在复用文本位于提示开头时才能复用KV缓存，但两种增长中的工作负载（检索增强生成和智能体协调器）破坏了这一条件。修复方法分散在三个不同社区各自独立评估，且现有基准只测试精确前缀复用（无任何损失），缺乏统一、公平的评测框架。

Method: KVShareArena在检索块和智能体报告上评测跨提示上下文与模型检查点的KV缓存复用。打分方式为每个方法恢复"无缓存"与"完全重算"之间差距的比例，并计入计算、内存和每请求延迟成本（缓存就绪时），同时单独报告构建缓存的一次性成本。方法分类涵盖位置修正（免重算）、部分重编码、训练类方法以及缓存压缩方法，并在三个模型版本上进行验证。

Result: 位置修正（无需重计算）在大部分场景下足够；但当问题需要多个来源并行时，只有通过部分重编码或训练的方法能恢复一半到三分之二的差距，未修复缓存甚至可能比无缓存更差。单提示下无害的缓存压缩方法在新鲜智能体报告上明显落后于位置修正。此规律在三个模型版本上一致。对不同检查点写入的缓存，免训练方法几乎不受影响，而基于单检查点缓存训练的自适应器会损失质量。

Conclusion: KV缓存复用必须考虑跨上下文和跨检查点的场景，简单的修正位置是高效基线，但复杂多源场景需要更大代价的方法；训练类方法存在跨检查点泛化脆弱性。该基准工具链（harness、冻结查询集、成本核算）以pip包发布，配合自动化提交工作流和公开排行榜，为社区提供统一评测标准。

Abstract: LLM serving systems already reuse KV caches, but only when the reused text sits at the very start of the prompt. Two growing workloads break this condition: a retrieval-augmented generation server assembles a different set of retrieved chunks for every query, and a multi-agent coordinator reads reports written by other agents. Reused inside a new prompt, a cache carries the wrong positions and never attended to the other sources. The cache may also have been written by a different checkpoint of the same model family, which changes the stored values. Repair methods for such caches have appeared in three separate communities, each measured on its own terms, and existing benchmarks test only exact-prefix reuse, where nothing is lost. KVShareArena benchmarks KV-cache reuse across prompt contexts and model checkpoints on retrieved chunks and agent reports. It scores every method by the fraction of the gap it recovers between no cache and full recomputation, and charges compute, memory, and per-request latency with the cache in hand, reporting the one-time cost of building a cache separately. We find that correcting positions, which needs no recomputation, is enough until a question needs several sources at once. There, only methods that pay, by re-encoding part of the cache or by training, recover half to two thirds of the gap; unrepaired caches can be worse than no cache. Cache-compression methods that are harmless on a single prompt fall significantly behind position correction on freshly written agent reports. These patterns hold across three model boards. When a different checkpoint wrote the cache, training-free methods are barely affected, while an adapter trained on one checkpoint's caches loses quality. Harness, frozen querysets, and cost accounting ship as a pip package with an automated submission workflow and a public leaderboard.

</details>


### [138] [GANDR: Claim Auditing for Verifiable Legal Answer Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10293)
*Chen Qian,Yimeng Wang,Yu Chen,Lingfei Wu,Andreas Stathopoulos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 GANDR，一个面向高利害法律场景的双智能体落地生成系统：Drafter 按结构化法律推理格式撰写答案，独立 Critic 以人类核验者视角对每项主张逐条审计其引用来源，并生成逐条审计迹。配套采用严格正确性标准（每条引用须解析到检索器返回的段落）。在 185 项法律基准上，六套系统共享同一主干、检索面和引用指令，GANDR 在所有主要指标上排名第一，严格准确率达 70.8%，领先最强基线 11.3 个百分点（p<0.01）。撤销协议锚定提交规则会使严格准确率下降 22.7 个百分点；在另外三个主干上严格领先持续为正（+3.2 至 +6.5 个百分点）。作为二分类检测器，其审计对证据不足主张的 F1 为 0.84，但对四分类判定标签的一致性较弱，仅为参考性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高利害领域（如法律实务）中，语言模型答案的价值取决于读者能否对照系统引用的来源逐条核验每项主张。现有落地生成流水线对答案整体打分，导致结论正确但引用虚假或松散匹配时仍能获得高分。要弥合这一缺口，既需要为逐条核验而设计的系统，也需要能够度量该能力的评估方法。

Method: 提出 GANDR 双智能体系统：Drafter 以结构化法律推理格式撰写答案；独立的 Critic 与人类核验者视角一致，对每项主张对照其引用来源进行审计，并在每轮输出逐条审计迹。配套定义严格正确性标准，要求每条引用都必须解析到检索器实际返回的段落；并采用协议锚定的提交规则决定何时定稿。

Result: 在 185 项法律基准上（六套系统共享同一主干、检索面与引用指令），GANDR 在所有主要指标排名第一，严格准确率达 70.8%，领先最强基线 11.3 个百分点（p<0.01）。撤销协议锚定提交规则会使严格准确率下降 22.7 个百分点；该领先优势在另外三个主干上保持为正（+3.2 至 +6.5 个百分点）。领先可归因于 Drafter 配置与协议锚定提交，而非重写。针对两位受过法律训练的标注者，审计作为二分类检测器对证据不足主张的 F1 为 0.84；其四分类判定标签一致性较弱，仅为参考性质。

Conclusion: 本文证明了逐条核验系统与相应评估在落地生成中的必要性。GANDR 通过"撰写者-审计者"双智能体架构和严格引用正确性标准，在可控共享设置下显著优于整体打分式基线；其优势主要来自 Drafter 配置与协议锚定的提交机制。审计可作为可靠的二分类证据不足检测器，但细化分类标签还需进一步校准。

Abstract: In high-stakes domains such as legal practice, a language-model answer is only useful to the extent that a reader can verify each claim against the source the system cites. Current grounded-generation pipelines score the answer as a whole, so a correct conclusion can rest on fabricated or loosely matched citations and still score well. Closing this gap requires both a system built for per-claim verification and an evaluation that measures it. We introduce GANDR (Grounded ANswer DRafter), a two-agent system in which a Drafter writes an answer in a structured legal-reasoning format and a separate Critic, with the same view as a human verifier, audits each claim against its cited source and emits a per-claim audit trace on every round. We pair it with a strict correctness criterion requiring every citation to resolve to a passage the retriever returned. On a 185-item legal benchmark where all six systems share one backbone, one retrieval surface, and one citation instruction, GANDR ranks first on every primary metric, reaching 70.8% strict accuracy and leading the strongest baseline by 11.3 points (p<0.01). Reverting the protocol-anchored commit rule lowers strict accuracy by 22.7 points, and the strict lead stays positive on three further backbones, at +3.2 to +6.5 points. This lead traces to the Drafter configuration and the protocol-anchored commit, not to rewriting. Against two law-trained annotators the audit flags under-supported claims at F1 0.84 as a binary detector, while its four-way verdict labels agree only weakly and are advisory. Code is available upon request.

</details>


### [139] [The Semantic Bottleneck: Leveraging Semantic Representations for Non-Invasive Speech Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10296)
*Gilad D. Landau,Dulhan Jayalath,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Brain2Semantics2Text 方法，通过中间语义嵌入空间将非侵入性 MEG 脑电信号映射为语义表征，再将其反转为自然语言，从而绕过低信噪比下难以进行细粒度音素/词汇解码的难题，并在句子级重构上优于既往非侵入式 Brain2Text 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非侵入式语音解码长期受限于神经记录的低信噪比，难以实现音素或单词级别的精细重构。受神经科学证据启发（高层语义表征广泛分布于皮层区域且时间尺度较慢），作者假设语义内容比低频声学或词汇特征更适合作为非侵入式解码的目标。

Method: 提出 Brain2Semantics2Text 管线：将句子级 MEG 响应映射到语义流形（神经到语义映射），再将预测的语义嵌入反转为自然语言。语义瓶颈机制允许在无需词级对齐的情况下恢复高层语义，并采用策略缓解可靠神经-语义映射学习的挑战，最后与非侵入式 Brain2Text 基线对比。

Result: 通过语义瓶颈实现高层语义恢复，在句子级重构结果上优于既往的非侵入式 Brain2Text 方法。

Conclusion: 语义层面可作为非侵入式脑解码的更合适目标，Brain2Semantics2Text 展示了通过语义嵌入瓶颈改善句子级文本重构的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Non-invasive speech decoding remains constrained by the low signal-to-noise ratio of neural recordings, which makes fine-grained reconstruction of phonemes or individual words difficult. Motivated by neuroscientific evidence that high-level semantic representations are distributed across cortical regions and evolve over slower temporal scales, we hypothesize that semantic content may provide a more suitable target for non-invasive decoding than low-level acoustic or lexical features. We introduce Brain2Semantics2Text, a method that reconstructs text through an intermediate semantic embedding space. Our model maps sentence-level MEG responses into a semantic manifold and then inverts the predicted embeddings into natural language. This semantic bottleneck enables recovery of high-level meaning without word-level alignment. We describe the core principles of the approach, its implementation, and the strategies used to mitigate the challenges of learning a reliable neural-to-semantic mapping. Finally, we compare against prior non-invasive Brain2Text methods and show improved sentence-level results.

</details>


### [140] [RiLM: Parameter-Efficient Language Modeling via Geodesic Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10305)
*Fang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出黎曼语言模型（RiLM），通过完全移除输出矩阵 W_out，将上下文演化建模为黎曼流形上的轨迹，并利用状态与词嵌入之间的测地线距离平方直接产生下一词的概率分布。在 d=128、|V|=2000 的规模下，双曲版本（HypRiLM，基于庞加莱球）在 WikiText-2 上达到 54.2±0.2 的验证困惑度，而平坦版本（Flat RiLM）为 87.6±0.6，远优于绑定的 LSTM/Transformer/SSM 基线（113–147 PPL），领先约 2 倍。作者还揭示朴素双曲循环中的边界坍缩问题，并证明 Möbius 稳定化可恢复可训练性；实验在 PTB 与 10k 词表上验证了测地线解码的跨语料转移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在百万参数以下的语言模型中（面向边缘部署、领域适配与可复现研究），输出矩阵 W_out 占据约三分之一容量（d=128 的两层 LSTM/Transformer 中），造成严重的参数浪费。作者希望消除该巨大输出层，转而利用几何（测地线距离）作为解码机制，从而将容量释放给更有效的表征。

Method: 提出 RiLM 框架：完全去除输出矩阵，上下文作为黎曼流形上的轨迹演化，下一词概率由当前状态与词嵌入之间的平方测地线距离决定；同一嵌入映射同时充当输入与输出（解码即几何）。在平坦欧氏空间 R^d 上实例化 Flat RiLM，并在庞加莱球 H^d 上实例化 HypRiLM，两者共用 MLP 组合映射 φ（约 290k 参数，d=128，|V|=2000）。同时分析双曲循环中的边界坍缩现象，并引入 Möbius 稳定化以恢复可训练性。

Result: 在 WikiText-2 上（五种随机种子），HypRiLM 达到 54.2±0.2 验证困惑度，Flat RiLM 为 87.6±0.6；绑定的 LSTM、Transformer、SSM 基线均为 113–147 PPL，HypRiLM 比最强绑定循环基线（SSM，113.0±3.8）领先约 2 倍。Penn Treebank 与 10k 词表压力测试证实测地线解码可跨语料与更大词表迁移，且双曲曲率仅在部分场景有选择性益处。

Conclusion: RiLM 证明在小模型场景下可以从根本上告别庞大的输出投影层，以几何测地线距离作为解码依据，显著优于传统绑定基线；双曲几何带来进一步增益，但需通过 Möbius 稳定化克服边界坍缩。作者明确将声称范围限定于受控的小模型对比，而非全词表的最先进水平。

Abstract: Language models under one million parameters matter for edge deployment, domain adaptation, and reproducible research, yet a two-layer LSTM or Transformer at embedding width d = 128 still spends roughly one third of its capacity on the output matrix W_out in R^(d x |V|). We propose Riemannian Language Models (RiLM), which remove that layer entirely: context unfolds as a trajectory on a Riemannian manifold, and next-token probabilities arise from squared geodesic distance between the current state and vocabulary embeddings. The same embedding map serves input and output -- decoding is geometry. We instantiate the framework on flat R^d (Flat RiLM) and the Poincare ball H^d (HypRiLM) with a shared MLP composition map phi (~290k parameters, d = 128, |V| = 2000). Across five seeds on WikiText-2, HypRiLM reaches 54.2 +/- 0.2 validation perplexity versus 87.6 +/- 0.6 for Flat RiLM; tied and matched LSTM, Transformer, and SSM controls remain at 113-147 PPL on WT-2 -- HypRiLM leads by roughly 2x over the strongest tied recurrent baseline (SSM, 113.0 +/- 3.8). Penn Treebank and a 10k-vocabulary stress test confirm that geodesic decoding transfers across corpora and larger |V|, while hyperbolic curvature helps selectively. We also characterize boundary collapse in naive hyperbolic recurrence and show how Mobius stabilization restores trainability. Claims are scoped to controlled small-model comparisons, not full-vocabulary state of the art.

</details>


### [141] [On-Policy Distillation for Vision-Language Model Adaptation, an Effective Paradigm on Low-Quality Multimodal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10321)
*Hongyuan Zhang,Xianda Guo,Yanlun Peng,Qianlong Yang,Yubin Guo,Pinhan Fu,Mulin Chen,Xiaozhen Qiao,Ping Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出OnPoKD，首个将在线策略蒸馏应用于视觉语言模型适应的框架，通过轻量控制器动态构建样本自适应的蒸馏目标，提升基类到新类泛化和跨数据集迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言蒸馏方法通常将蒸馏目标固定为教师预测并统一应用于所有样本，在类别和域偏移下不可靠。作者认为蒸馏目标构建应作为动态训练决策而非固定配方。

Method: OnPoKD学习一个轻量控制器，利用教师模型、学生模型和零样本先验的可靠性与分歧线索，通过有界策略动作动态平衡教师监督、零样本先验引导和硬标签锚定，构建样本自适应目标；控制器通过验证反馈更新，以优化可迁移性而非仅拟合训练分布。

Result: 在Base-to-novel泛化和跨数据集迁移基准上，OnPoKD一致优于强视觉语言蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: OnPoKD可无缝集成到现有视觉语言蒸馏流程中，保持原始推理架构和测试时成本不变，证明了动态目标构建的有效性。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation offers an efficient route to transfer a task-adapted vision-language teacher to a compact student. The training target in current vision-language distillation methods is typically constructed from the teacher prediction and applied uniformly to all training samples, making it unreliable under class and domain shifts. In this paper, we argue that distillation target construction should be treated as a dynamic training decision rather than a fixed recipe. To this end, we propose OnPoKD, an on-policy distillation framework for vision-language model adaptation. To the best of our knowledge, OnPoKD is the first framework that applies on-policy distillation to vision-language model adaptation by learning target construction as a policy decision. OnPoKD learns a lightweight controller that constructs sample-wise adaptive targets using reliability and disagreement cues from the teacher model, student model, and zero-shot prior. Instead of relying on a fixed teacher prediction, the controller dynamically balances teacher supervision, zero-shot prior guidance, and hard-label anchoring through bounded policy actions, allowing the distillation target to adapt to varying sample reliability and training stages. The policy controller is updated with validation feedback, encouraging target construction to optimize transferability rather than merely fitting the training distribution. Since the controller is only used during training, OnPoKD can be seamlessly integrated into existing vision-language distillation pipelines while preserving the original inference architecture and test-time cost. Extensive experiments on Base-to-novel generalization and Cross-dataset transfer benchmarks show that OnPoKD consistently improves over strong vision-language distillation baselines.

</details>


### [142] [Rosetta at AlexandriaX-2026: LoRA-Adapted NileChat for Context-Aware Dialectal Arabic Dialogue Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10395)
*Nada Esmaeil,Fathima Rena,Sibi Subhash,Osama Elgendy,Mina Naguib,Salma Omar,Muhammad Arif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍Rosetta系统在AlexandriaX共享任务子任务1（上下文感知的英语到方言阿拉伯语对话翻译）中的方案，对NileChat-3B模型应用LoRA适配器微调，并通过结构化系统/用户提示词将方言与对话上下文作为生成条件。在限制与非限制赛道中分别获得第4名（spBLEU 26.10）和第5名（spBLEU 25.09），实验发现外部预训练仅对13种方言中的2种有增益，整体性能轻微下降，表明存在负迁移现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决英语到多方言阿拉伯语对话翻译中方言多样性与上下文依赖带来的挑战，参与AlexandriaX共享任务的约束与非约束两种赛道，探究外部方言数据预训练能否提升跨方言翻译性能。

Method: 采用NileChat-3B作为基础模型，通过LoRA适配器进行参数高效微调；构建结构化系统/用户提示词，将目标方言和对话历史上下文作为条件注入生成过程。非约束赛道额外在MADAR和PADIC语料上进行适配器预训练。

Result: 约束赛道排名第4，spBLEU为26.10；非约束赛道排名第5，spBLEU为25.09。外部预训练仅对13种方言中2种产生正向帮助，总体性能反而略有下降。

Conclusion: 外部方言数据的额外预训练在整体上造成负迁移，表明跨方言预训练数据的选取与适配策略需谨慎设计，单纯增加外部语料并非总能提升多方言翻译效果。

Abstract: This paper describes the Rosetta system for Subtask 1 (Context-Aware English-to-Dialectal Arabic Dialogue Translation) of the AlexandriaX shared task, participating in both constrained and unconstrained tracks. The approach fine-tunes a LoRA adapter on NileChat-3B using structured system/user prompts that condition generation on dialect and dialogue context. For the unconstrained track, the adapter is additionally pretrained on MADAR and PADIC. Rosetta ranked 4th in the constrained track (spBLEU 26.10) and 5th in the unconstrained track (spBLEU 25.09). The experimental results demonstrate that external pretraining helps only two of thirteen dialects while slightly hurting overall performance, suggesting negative transfer.

</details>


### [143] [Can Foundation Models Moderate Online Content? Evaluating Instruction- vs. Example-Driven Policy Operationalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10410)
*Ayan Majumdar,Shounak Paul,Pushpdeep Singh,Ines Abdelaziz,Sayeh Jarollahi,Seungeon Lee,Krishna P. Gummadi,Ingmar Weber,Abhisek Dash*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了两种用于视觉-语言模型（VLM）内容审核范式的指令驱动（基于政策原则推理）与示例驱动（基于先例泛化），并构建了包含4,000条Bluesky平台真实标注帖子的新基准ModerationBench。实验表明，基础模型能显著超越Bluesky现有审核系统，F1得分几乎提升三倍（0.60对0.22），且两种范式均能达到相近的峰值效果，为大规模可靠、可适配的政策落地指明了路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内容审核政策日益复杂，给其一致性落地带来严峻挑战。虽然基础模型具备应对这一挑战的基本能力，但其能否可靠地审核在线内容仍是未解之谜，由此激发了本研究的开展。

Method: 作者系统对比了两种相互竞争的VLM引导范式：指令驱动方法（模型依据政策条文进行推理）与示例驱动方法（模型从既有先例中泛化）。为支撑该研究，作者构建了ModerationBench基准，包含来自Bluesky平台的4,000条人工标注的真实网络帖子。

Result: 实验发现，基础模型在基准的Random Posts上能以0.60对0.22的F1得分大幅超越Bluesky已部署的审核系统（几乎提升三倍），且指令驱动与示例驱动两种范式均能达到相近的峰值效果。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明，基础模型能够为内容审核政策提供可靠且可适配的落地途径，为大规模政策可操作化奠定了基础。

Abstract: The growing complexity of content moderation policies presents a critical challenge for their consistent operationalization. While foundation models possess the basic capabilities needed to confront this challenge, whether they can reliably moderate online content remains an unanswered question. In this paper, we systematically compare two competing paradigms for Vision-Language Model (VLM) guidance: an instruction-driven approach where models reason from policy precepts, and an example-driven approach where they generalize from prior precedents. We ground this investigation in ModerationBench, a new benchmark of 4,000 manually annotated, in-the-wild posts from the Bluesky platform. Our experiments reveal that foundation models can substantially outperform Bluesky's deployed moderation system, nearly tripling its $F_1$ score (0.60 vs. 0.22) on Random Posts in the benchmark, with both instruction- and example-driven paradigms achieving comparable peak effectiveness. Our findings thus chart a path toward reliable and adaptable policy operationalization at scale.

</details>


### [144] [Do speech foundation models really learn words?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10434)
*Robin Huo,Ewan Dunbar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了自监督语音基础模型（如HuBERT和wav2vec 2.0）是否真正学习到了独立的词表征，而非仅仅编码了词形（音素）信息。通过使用残差化（residualization）方法剔除音素信息，作者发现在深层网络中这些模型确实能以较高保真度编码与局部语音内容无关的词表征，并且这种简单的解耦方法能增强词发现任务中的更高阶语言学信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常通过探针来考察语音模型表征对音素和词的判别能力，但词的判别能力并不必然意味着模型具有专门的词表征——好的词判别可能仅仅得益于对词形（音素）的良好编码，而非编码了词的身份或句法/语义属性。作者旨在区分这两种可能性，即模型是否真正学习到独立于音素内容的词级表征。

Method: 作者采用残差化（residualization）方法，从词表征中剔除（partialling out）音素信息，从而分离出词的身份、句法/语义等与局部语音内容无关的信息。他们对HuBERT和wav2vec 2.0的不同深度层进行系统性分析，并在词发现任务中验证这种解耦方法的效果。

Result: 实证表明，在较深的网络层中，HuBERT和wav2vec 2.0确实学习到了与局部音素内容无关、具有合理保真度的词表征。采用残差化这一简单的解耦方法能够显著增强词发现任务中更高阶的语言学信息。

Conclusion: 自监督语音基础模型在深层确实形成了独立的词级表征，而非仅依赖音素编码。残差化作为一种简洁有效的解耦手段，可以提升下游任务（如词发现）中高阶语言学信息的利用，为理解和改进语音基础模型的表征能力提供了新思路。

Abstract: Self-supervised speech foundation models are now used in a wide array of downstream applications, including traditional speech recognition and as the basis for tokens in speech-aware language models. Attempts to understand their usefulness have largely focused on probing their representations' ability to discriminate phonemes and words. However, discriminative ability for words need not imply specialized representation of words per se. Good discrimination of words may be explained by good encoding of word form (phonemes) rather than form-independent word representations encoding identity or syntactic/semantic properties. By partialling out phoneme information using residualization, we show that, in later layers, HuBERT and wav2vec 2.0 do in general learn representations which encode words with reasonable fidelity independently of local phonetic content. We show that this simple approach to disentanglement can enhance higher-order linguistic information in word discovery tasks.

</details>


### [145] [Building Multilingual Bridges: Data Mixing as the Pillar of Generalization for In-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10445)
*Mehrnaz Mofakhami,Ananya Sahu,Alejandro R. Salamanca,Daniel D'souza,Alexandre Berard,Thomas Euyang,Marzieh Fadaee,Julia Kreutzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对推理语言模型"以英语为中心"的缺陷，提出L2推理概念——让模型用用户提示语言进行推理。作者从数据角度切入，通过优化SFT中的数据构成与调度，构建了3.35B规模的Tiny Aya L2-Thinker模型，在60种语言的6个基准上实现超93%的L2推理率，同时保持较强性能。研究表明推理是语言无关行为，可通过数据混合在类型多样的语言间迁移，无需每种目标语言都有推理监督数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理语言模型在复杂任务上取得了显著进展，但其能力高度以英语为中心——无论用户用何种语言提示，模型都主要用英语推理。这对非英语用户不友好，会丢失原始问题的语义意图，且放弃了目标语言中更易于表达的知识。因此作者希望提升L2推理能力，即在用户提示语言中保持推理一致性，建立提示与答案之间的"语言内桥梁"。

Method: 采用数据中心的视角，重点研究在监督微调（SFT）中如何优化数据构成（composition）与调度（scheduling）以促进推理泛化。据此构建了3.35B规模的Tiny Aya L2-Thinker模型。

Result: 在60种语言的6个基准（涵盖数学、常识推理、指令遵循、开放式生成和文化推理）上，L2推理率超过93%，且保持较强性能。作者指出将L2推理泛化到未见语言的关键路径包括：更广的语言覆盖、现成的多语言非推理数据，以及足够的英语推理骨干。

Conclusion: 推理是一种语言无关的行为，可以通过仔细的数据混合在类型多样的语言之间迁移，而无需在每种目标语言中提供推理监督。作者开源了模型权重和多语言推理数据，以支持更多关于可访问、语言内推理的研究。

Abstract: Reasoning language models have made substantial advances on a variety of complex tasks, yet their capabilities remain overwhelmingly English-centric: models primarily reason in English regardless of the language they are prompted in. This is inaccessible for non-English-speaking users, risks losing the intent of the original question, and forgoes knowledge more readily expressed in the target language. In this work, we advance L2 reasoning, the ability of a model to reason consistently in the language of the user's prompt, thus building an in-language bridge between the prompt and the answer. We approach this problem from a data-centric angle, investigating how to optimize data composition and scheduling in SFT for reasoning generalization. Building Tiny Aya L2-Thinker at 3.35B scale, we achieve an L2 reasoning rate above 93% across 60 languages on 6 benchmarks spanning math, commonsense reasoning, instruction following, open-ended generation, and cultural reasoning while keeping performance strong. We show the path to generalizing L2 reasoning to held-out languages goes through broader language coverage, readily available multilingual non-reasoning data, and a sufficient English reasoning backbone. These findings indicate that reasoning is a language-agnostic behavior that can be transferred across typologically diverse languages through careful data mixing and without requiring reasoning supervision in every target language. We release our model weights and multilingual reasoning data to support further research on accessible, in-language reasoning.

</details>


### [146] [IBIB: A Protocol for Measuring Enterprise AI Systems by Serving Route, Not Model Identifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10494)
*Blake Stenstrom,Charangan Vasantharajan,Brian Sathianathan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IB2协议，将企业实际部署的系统能力（而非模型标识符）视为可审计的测量对象。IB2包含三个部分：金标准盲的能力绑定预检、包含可靠性的首轮评分规则、结构上评分盲的裁决机制。参考实现含128个锁定任务和987条断言，覆盖文档、表格、图表、工具和数据库工作。在11个系统上的4项结果为：能力可用性可测量且可失败；判别力不均（四之七套件在六系统带内饱和），故以区间支持的解析分组代替排名；服务臂选择可显著改变精度（如77.38至82.54）；排除失败响应的分母会改变结论本身而非措辞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业部署的是系统而非检查点，实际可用能力取决于权重、服务路径、精度、输出契约和框架等多个因素，但18个被审计的基准却都把广告中的模型标识符作为评分对象，这属于测量误差，需要协议使其可报告。

Method: 提出IB2协议，包含三部分：(1)金标准盲的能力绑定预检，在任务到达前验证路由能执行评估契约；(2)可靠性包含的首轮评分规则，把失败计入分数同时排除不支持的能仜能力；(3)结构上评分盲的裁决机制。发布算法、分类表、请求契约和清单模式，参考实现为128个锁定任务和987条断言（过程是产物而非语料库）。

Result: 在11个系统上得到四项结果：能力可用性可测量（两套完整单路径运行在相同权重下后失败于绑定门的特定谓词，第三个先通过绑定门再在新运行中失败，而广告标识符均未暴露限制）；判别力不均（六系统带宽下四之七套件饱和，差异几乎全部来自受治理的数据库工作和多标签页联表，故报告区间支持的解析分组而非排名；五个名义标签输出的四个切分未通过多重性调整）；服务臂选择可改变修订声明和精度（77.38至82.54，配对区间[0.11,10.60]）；从分母中排除失败响应会改变点排序，即可靠性包含改变的是结论而非措辞。

Conclusion: 基准应测量部署的系统能力而非广告标识符；IB2提供了一套可复现、可报告的能力评估协议，将测量误差转化为可报告量，并证明可靠性、服务路径等部署因素足以改变结论，而非仅改变得分措辞。

Abstract: Enterprises deploy systems, not checkpoints. Usable capability depends jointly on weights, serving route, precision, output contract, and harness, yet all 18 audited benchmarks score advertised model identifiers. We treat this as measurement error and give a protocol that makes it reportable. It has three parts. A gold-blind capability-binding preflight verifies that a route can execute the evaluation contract before any task reaches it; a reliability-inclusive first-pass scoring rule keeps failure in the score while keeping unsupported capability out; and adjudication is structurally score-blind. We call the protocol IB2 and release its algorithms, classification tables, request contract, and manifest schemas. Its reference instantiation, 128 locked tasks and 987 assertions over document, spreadsheet, chart, tool and database work, stays sealed: the procedure is the artifact, not the corpus. Across eleven systems, four results. Capability availability is measurable: two complete single-route runs on identical weights later failed distinct predicates of the finalized binding gate, while a third passed that gate before a fresh run. The advertised identifier exposed neither limit. Discrimination is not uniform: four of seven suites saturate under a six-system band, with the spread almost entirely from governed database work and multi-tab joins, so we report interval-backed resolution groups, not ranks; two of the nominal five-label output's four cuts fail multiplicity adjustment. Serving-arm choice moved one declared revision and precision from 77.38 to 82.54, paired interval [0.11,10.60], though the arms differ in access mode, harness generation, and the serving tool-call parser, and harness generation is a property of our evaluator, not any endpoint. Excluding failed responses from denominators changes the point ordering, so reliability inclusion changes a conclusion, not its wording.

</details>


### [147] [IdeaAMBIG: Benchmarking Implementation-Critical Gaps in Research-Idea Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10539)
*Yiling Ma,Yilun Zhao,Sihong Wu,Manasi Patwardhan,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究研究方法的"编码就绪度"（codification readiness）问题，即一个研究想法的方法描述是否足够详细，能让有能力的实现者或编码代理在不做无依据假设的情况下忠实构建出目标方法。作者构建了基于证据的规格与解决方案，并提出了IdeaAMBIG基准，包含660个实例（163个真实缺陷和497个受控合成缺陷）。基准评估三项能力：就绪度评估、缺陷定位、澄清行动生成。实验表明缺陷定位是主要瓶颈，定位能力弱（最佳9.6%）但给定缺陷后澄清能力强（80.6%），提供金标准解决方案可将下游就绪率从14%提升至98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注想法的新颖性、连贯性和科学合理性，但忽略了一个关键维度——提议的方法是否被充分指定、足以让实现者或编码代理忠实实现。方法规格不足会导致实现偏差或失败，而现有工作缺乏对这类"编码就绪度"的系统评估工具。

Method: 作者从论文、代码库、issue线程和复现产物中构建证据支撑的规格及其解决方案，提出IdeaAMBIG基准：660个实例，包括163个来自复现报告和GitHub issue的真实缺陷，以及497个注入到就绪参考中的受控合成缺陷。基准评估三项能力：编码就绪度评估、缺陷定位（仅输入规格）、澄清行动生成（额外输入标注的缺陷）。在13个LLM上评测，并进行oracle研究（提供金标准解决方案）。

Result: 最佳模型在真实实例上达到9.6%的Macro缺陷恢复率，但给定缺陷后澄清行动成功率达80.6%。Oracle研究显示，提供金标准解决方案可使下游编码就绪率从14%提升到98%。在所有模型上，缺陷定位是主要瓶颈，而给定缺陷后澄清能力明显更强。

Conclusion: 研究方法的编码就绪度是一个可以被系统和量化评估的重要维度。IdeaAMBIG基准揭示了当前LLM在自动定位方法规格缺陷方面的显著不足（这是主要瓶颈），但在明确缺陷位置后具备较强的澄清能力。该工作为提升研究方法的可复现性与可实现性提供了评测基础和方向。

Abstract: A research idea may be novel, coherent, and scientifically plausible, yet its proposed method may remain insufficiently specified for faithful implementation. We study the codification readiness of implementation-facing research-method specifications, defined by whether they provide sufficient methodological information for a competent implementer or coding agent to construct the intended method without unsupported assumptions. We construct evidence-grounded specifications and their supported resolutions from papers, codebases, issue threads, and reproduction artifacts. We introduce IdeaAMBIG, a benchmark of 660 evidence-grounded instances: 163 real-world gaps from reproducibility reports and GitHub issues, and 497 controlled synthetic gaps injected into codification-ready references. IdeaAMBIG evaluates three capabilities: codification-readiness assessment, defect localization, and clarification action generation. Defect localization receives only the specification, whereas clarification additionally receives the annotated defect. Across 13 LLMs, the best model achieves 9.6% Macro Defect Recovery Rate on real-world instances but 80.6% Macro Clarification Action Success Rate when given the defect. In an oracle study, supplying the gold resolution raises the downstream codification-ready rate from 14% to 98%. Across all evaluated models, defect localization is the main bottleneck, with stronger clarification given the defect.

</details>


### [148] [Every Activation Boosted: Scaling General Reasoner to 1 Trillion Open Language Foundation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22115)
*Ling Team,Ang Li,Ben Liu,Binbin Hu,Bing Li,Bingwei Zeng,Borui Ye,Caizhi Tang,Changxin Tian,Chao Huang,Chao Zhang,Chen Qian,Chenchen Ju,Chenchen Li,Chengfu Tang,Chilin Fu,Chunshao Ren,Chunwei Wu,Cong Zhang,Cunyin Peng,Dafeng Xu,Daixin Wang,Dalong Zhang,Dingnan Jin,Dingyuan Zhu,Dongke Hu,Fangzheng Zhao,Feifan Wu,Feng Zhu,Gangshan Wang,Haitao Zhang,Hailin Zhao,Hanxiao Zhang,Hanzi Wang,Hao Qian,Haoyi Yu,Heng Zhang,Hongliang Zhang,Hongzhi Luan,Huirong Dong,Huizhong Li,Jia Li,Jia Liu,Jialong Zhu,Jian Sha,Jianping Wei,Jiaolong Yang,Jieyue Ma,Jiewei Wu,Jinjing Huang,Jingyun Tian,Jingyuan Zhang,Jinquan Sun,Juanhui Tu,Jun Liu,Jun Xu,Jun Zhou,Junjie Ou,Junpeng Fang,Kaihong Zhang,Kaiqin Hu,Ke Shi,Kun Tang,Kunlong Chen,Lanyin Mei,Lei Liang,Lei Xu,Libo Zhang,Lin Ju,Lin Yuan,Ling Zhong,Lintao Ma,Lu Liu,Lu Yu,Lun Cai,Meiqi Zhu,Mengying Li,Min Chen,Minghao Xue,Minghong Cai,Mingming Yin,Peijie Jiang,Peilong Zhao,Pingping Liu,Qian Zhao,Qing Cui,Qingxiang Huang,Qingyuan Yang,Quankun Yu,Shaowei Wei,Shijie Lian,Shoujian Zheng,Shun Song,Shungen Zhang,Shuo Zhang,Siyuan Li,Song Liu,Ting Guo,Tong Zhao,Wanli Gu,Weichang Wu,Weiguang Han,Wenjing Fang,Wubin Wang,Xiang Shu,Xiao Shi,Xiaoshun Lan,Xiaolu Zhang,Xiaqing Sun,Xin Zhao,Xingyu Lu,Xiong Xu,Xudong Wang,Xudong Wang,Xuemin Yang,Yajie Yang,Yang Xiang,Yanzhe Li,Yi Zhang,Yilong Wang,Yingxue Li,Yongzhen Guo,Yuzhuo Fu,Yuanyuan Wang,Yue Yang,Yue Yu,Yufeng Deng,Yun Zhang,Yunfei Yu,Yuqi Zhang,Yuxiao He,Zengke Gui,Zhaoxin Huan,Zhaoyang Wang,Zhibo Zhu,Zhihao Wang,Zhiqiang Zhang,Zhoufei Wang,Zihang Zeng,Ziqi Liu,Zitao Xuan,Zuoli Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Ling 2.0是一个面向推理的语言基础模型系列，基于“每次激活都提升推理能力”的原则，采用统一MoE架构，参数规模从数百亿到一万亿，强调高稀疏性、跨尺度一致性和经验缩放定律指导的效率。包括三个非思考（指令）模型，总参数16B至1T，相比密集模型实现最高7倍激活计算效率。集成架构、预训练、后训练和基础设施的协同创新，Ling-1T在万亿规模上建立推理准确率与计算效率的新帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个规模从数十亿到一万亿参数、统一MoE范式下、以推理能力为核心的语言基础模型系列，通过高稀疏性和跨尺度一致性实现高效可扩展的智能。

Method: 采用高稀疏MoE架构，结合MTP（多令牌预测）提升推理效率；推理导向的数据与中训练CoT激活；基于强化学习的微调（DFT、Evo-CoT）；全规模FP8训练与细粒度异构流水线。三个模型：Ling-mini-2.0、Ling-flash-2.0、Ling-1T，总参数16B-1T，激活计算效率最高达密集模型的7倍。

Result: Ling-1T在万亿规模上建立了推理准确率与计算效率的新帕累托前沿，证明稀疏激活与推理目标正确对齐时可实现可扩展且高效的智能。

Conclusion: Ling 2.0提供了一个连贯、开放且高效的基础，用于推进未来推理与思考模型，包括基于同一基座的Ring系列。

Abstract: We introduce Ling 2.0, a series reasoning-oriented language foundation built upon the principle that every activation boosts reasoning capability. Designed to scale from tens of billions to one trillion parameters under a unified Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) paradigm, Ling 2.0 emphasizes high sparsity, cross-scale consistency, and efficiency guided by empirical scaling laws. The series includes three non-thinking (instruct) models - Ling-mini-2.0, Ling-flash-2.0, and Ling-1T - ranging from 16B to 1T total parameters and achieving up to 7-fold active-compute efficiency compared with dense counterparts. Ling 2.0 integrates coordinated innovations across model architecture, pre-training, post-training, and infrastructure: a high-sparsity MoE with MTP for efficient reasoning, reasoning-oriented data and mid-training CoT activation, reinforcement-based fine-tuning (DFT, Evo-CoT), and full-scale FP8 training with fine-grained heterogeneous pipelines. At the trillion scale, Ling-1T establishes a new Pareto frontier of reasoning accuracy versus computational efficiency, demonstrating that sparse activation, when properly aligned with reasoning objectives, enables scalable and efficient intelligence. Collectively, Ling 2.0 provides a coherent, open, and efficient foundation for advancing future reasoning and thinking models, including the Ring series built upon the same base.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [EEGBind: Detecting Source-Level Interictal Epileptiform Discharges via EEG-Centric Multimodal Binding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09728)
*Muchen Li,Anglin Liu,Xuetian Gao,Ruijian Xu,Jintai Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EEGBind，一种以EEG为中心的多模态绑定框架，用于五分类的源级发作间期癫痫样放电（IED）分类。EEGBind将EEG作为主模态，并将同步的视频上下文特征绑定到EEG中心表示上，通过视图一致性修复阶段提升隐藏集鲁棒性。在NeuroMM 2026挑战赛Track 3基准上达到0.8395的加权F1，优于强基线，验证了EEG中心多模态绑定策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源级IED分析对癫痫术前评估和治疗规划至关重要，其目标不仅是检测IED是否存在，还需将IED阳性活动归类到有临床意义的脑区类别。然而短EEG窗口中的源区证据可能细微、部分、受被试差异、类别不平衡和模态上下文不完善影响，给分类带来挑战。

Method: EEGBind采用EEG为中心的多模态绑定框架：将EEG作为主模态，将同步视频上下文特征绑定到EEG中心表示周围；不采用早期或过强的多模态融合，以免扰动源敏感EEG表示，而是将视频上下文作为辅助证据；并通过视图一致性修复阶段提高隐藏集鲁棒性，同时保持已学习的源类别边界。

Result: 在NeuroMM 2026挑战赛Track 3 NMM-Source-IED基准上，EEGBind取得0.8395的加权F1分数，优于强竞争对手。

Conclusion: 结果支持EEG中心多模态绑定作为源级IED分类的实用策略，代码已开源。

Abstract: Source-level analysis of interictal epileptiform discharges (IEDs) is relevant to presurgical evaluation and treatment planning because it helps characterize where epileptiform activity is likely to arise. Beyond detecting whether an IED is present, this setting requires assigning IED-positive activity to clinically meaningful brain-region categories. This setting is challenging because source-region evidence in short electroencephalography (EEG) windows can be subtle, partial, and affected by subject variability, class imbalance, and imperfect multimodal context. We present EEGBind, an EEG-centric multimodal binding framework for five-class source-level IED classification. EEGBind treats EEG as the primary modality and binds synchronized video-context features around an EEG-centric representation. Instead of relying on early or overly strong multimodal fusion, which may perturb the source-sensitive EEG representation, EEGBind uses video context as auxiliary evidence for robust classification. A view-consistent repair stage is further used to improve hidden-set robustness while preserving the learned source-class boundary. On the NeuroMM 2026 Grand Challenge Track 3 NMM-Source-IED benchmark, EEGBind achieves 0.8395 on weighted-F1 and outperforms strong competitors. These results support EEG-centric multimodal binding as a practical strategy for source-level IED classification. The open-source code is available at https://github.com/HKUSTGZ-ML4Health-Lab/NeuroMM2026_IED_Detection.

</details>


### [150] [Spectral origin of the topological gap exponent d + η: mechanism, kernel, decomposition, and scope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09159)
*Matthew Loftus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从解析和数值两方面研究临界点云拓扑间隙Δ的标度行为，证明Δ∼L^{d+η}来源于谱积分I(α)=∑_{k≠0}S_conn(k)|k|^α在红外主导下的标度L^{2-α-η}，并分解为体积因子I_0与反常维度因子I_shape；同时指出该机制仅适用于d<2+η，因而物理系统限于二维，三维需密度归一化。对Potts q=4的α扫描给出α_opt∈[-0.75,-0.5]，与Ising的-2η一致但与q=4的-1不一致（暂定为初步结果）。文中还分析了⟨m²·I(-2η)⟩超标度积主要由协方差修正主导、裸PH核平坦性、以及单构型Δ与I(-0.5)的强相关（R²=0.91）在控制|M|后消失等结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解临界点云中拓扑间隙Δ=L^{d+η}标度的起源，并厘清体积效应、反常维度、磁化涨落等因素对Δ及其协方差修正的贡献，同时检验谱积分方法在二维与三维中的适用性。

Method: 采用解析推导（谱积分I(α)的IR/UV主导分析、I_0与I_shape分解、Divol–Polonik启发式推广）结合数值模拟：Potts q=4在L=32–256上的α扫描，以及逐构型Pearson相关与偏相关分析（控制|M|）来检验Parseval恒等式和形因子|k|^{-2η}。

Result: 导出I(α)∼L^{2-α-η}（IR主导），从而I(-2η)∼L^{d+η}；体积因子I_0∝N(1-m²)∼L^d，反常维度因子I_shape∼L^η。证明机制要求d<2+η，故仅d=2成立；d=3为UV主导。α扫描得α_opt∈[-0.75,-0.5]，符合-2η_Ising而非-2η_{q=4}=-1（待L≥1024确认）。⟨m²·I(-2η)⟩由r(m²,I)≈-0.98的强相关主导，故按协方差修正处理。逐构型Δ与I(-0.5)的R²=0.91在偏掉|M|后降至约0，说明相关主要源于磁化；逐构型数据支持I_0的Parseval恒等式，但不支持|k|^{-2η}形因子。

Conclusion: 拓扑间隙Δ∼L^{d+η}的解析机制是IR主导的谱积分加上体积/反常维度分解；该机制在d=2成立，在d=3失效需用密度归一化。数值结果在二维Potts模型中与Ising型-2η一致，但需要更大尺寸确认；最终强调单构型证据只支持体积分量，形因子需依靠系综L标度建立。

Abstract: The topological gap $Δ$ -- the excess $H_1$ total persistence of a critical point cloud over a density-matched null -- scales as $Δ\sim L^{d+η}$. We derive this analytically: the spectral integral $I(α) = \sum_{k\neq 0} S_{\mathrm{conn}}(k)\,|k|^α$ scales as $L^{2-α-η}$ when IR-dominated, giving $I(-2η) \sim L^{d+η}$. The decomposition $I(-2η) = I_0 \cdot I_{\mathrm{shape}}$ separates volume ($I_0 \propto N(1-m^2) \sim L^d$) from anomalous dimension ($I_{\mathrm{shape}} \sim L^η$); the volume factor accounts for the magnetization-driven per-configuration variance of $Δ$. We prove the mechanism requires $d < 2 + η$ (IR dominance), confining it to $d = 2$ for physical systems; in $d = 3$ the spectral integral is UV-dominated, explaining why density normalization is needed. An $α$-sweep for Potts $q = 4$ at $L = 32$--$256$ finds $α_{\mathrm{opt}}$ in $[-0.75, -0.5]$, consistent with $-2η_{\mathrm{Ising}}$ and inconsistent with $-2η_{q=4} = -1$; we flag this as tentative pending $L \geq 1024$ confirmation. The $\langle m^2 \cdot I(-2η)\rangle$ hyperscaling product is dominated by the correlation $r(m^2, I) \approx -0.98$ via the shared $I_0$ amplitude, so we report it as a covariance-correction analysis. Under a heuristic argument extending Divol--Polonik to inhomogeneous Poisson intensities, the bare PH kernel is flat; the effective kernel acquires $k$-dependence only at criticality. The per-configuration agreement between $Δ$ and $I(-0.5)$ is primarily a magnetization correlation: $R^2 = 0.91$ at $L = 256$ collapses to $R^2 \approx 0$ once $|M|$ is partialed out. Per-configuration evidence corroborates the $I_0$ Parseval identity but not the $|k|^{-2η}$ shape factor; the latter is established by ensemble $L$-scaling.

</details>


### [151] [Physics-informed neural networks by Gradient-Guided Gaussian Adaptive Sampling (3GAS-PINNs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09162)
*Yousen Wang,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出梯度引导高斯自适应采样物理信息神经网络(3GAS-PINNs)，通过结合均匀概率分布与基于解空间梯度的Gaussian平滑概率分布来分配配点，在保持全局约束的同时将配点集中在高梯度区域，从而精确捕捉激波、孤子等间歇性结构。在三个非线性基准问题（一维受迫Burgers方程、KdV方程和非线性薛定谔方程）上验证，相比基线PINNs，数值模拟精度最高提升14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINNs在求解非线性问题时存在收敛缓慢、梯度失衡、分辨率不足等问题，难以捕捉激波等局部间歇性结构。其根源在于使用固定损失权重和均匀配点分布，无法适应训练过程中解场不断演化的复杂性。

Method: 提出3GAS-PINNs方法：将均匀概率分布与由解的空间梯度推导出的Gaussian平滑概率分布相结合，进行自适应配点采样。该策略既维持了全局约束满足，又将计算资源集中于高梯度区域，从而准确捕捉激波和孤子等间歇性结构。

Result: 方法在三个非线性基准问题（一维受迫Burgers方程、KdV方程和非线性薛定谔方程）上进行了评估，这些方程均具有陡峭梯度或强非线性特征。与基线PINNs相比，3GAS-PINNs有效提升了间歇区域的物理一致性，数值模拟精度最高提升达14倍。

Conclusion: 3GAS-PINNs通过梯度引导的高斯自适应采样策略，有效解决了传统PINNs在非线性问题中难以捕捉局部间歇性结构的缺陷，显著提升了求解精度和物理一致性。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) provide a mesh-free framework for solving partial differential equations, yet their performance in nonlinear problems is often limited by slow convergence, gradient imbalance, and insufficient resolution to capture localized intermittent structures such as shock waves[1]. These issues arise primarily from the use of fixed weights of loss and uniform collocation point distributions, which cannot adapt to the evolving complexity of the solution field during training. To address these challenges, Gradient-Guided Gaussian Adaptive Sampling Physics-Informed Neural Networks (3GAS-PINNs) is proposed in this paper, which combines uniform probability distribution and Gaussian-smoothed probability distribution derived from the spatial gradients of solution, to maintain global constraint satisfaction as well as concentrating collocation points in regions of high gradient. Thus, intermittency structures like shock wave and solitons can be accurately captured. The method is evaluated on three benchmark nonlinear problems, including one-dimensional forced Burgers equation, Korteweg-de Vries (KdV) equation and nonlinear Schrodinger equation, all of which exhibit steep gradients or strong nonlinearity. In comparison with baseline PINNs, 3GAS-PINNs can effectively promote the physical consistency in intermittent regions. The accuracy of the numerical simulation can be improved by a factor of up to 14.

</details>


### [152] [World-Time Compute with Verified Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09163)
*James Schwoebel,Ingrida Semenec,Jenia Rousseva,Marcos Ortiz,Collin Overbay,Christopher Klaus,Anderson Edmond,Manish Bhatt,Rome Thorstenson,Jessica Tsai,Martin G. Frasch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"世界时间计算"（world-time compute）：当某个领域的动态可写成代码时，用一个模板可实例化出大量可执行、可验证的世界模型，每个模型都能生成无限且带精确标签的轨迹。用这类轨迹微调LLM（小到0.5B模型可提升+29个点），可显著增强对未见世界（合成世界族）的泛化能力，且标签精确性（而非任务多样性）是增益的关键驱动因素。该增益是一种饱和性规律而非普适定律：在少步推理、小/弱模型上最强，对长链推理、感知型任务和已饱和任务上减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数领域缺乏大量真实标注样本，导致LLM难以在其上泛化。本文受测试时间计算（test-time compute）启发的对称类比，旨在研究能否在训练阶段廉价地"制造"标注数据：当领域动态可被写成代码时，借助可验证的合成世界产生精确标注轨迹，从而提升跨世界泛化能力。

Method: 核心方法是将领域动态写成代码模板，模板实例化出大量可执行且可验证的"世界"（基于符号状态的程序），每个世界都是精确标注轨迹的无穷来源。通过对这些轨迹微调LLM（即"世界时间计算"），用合成并经校验的动态在20步rollout上精确，并100%回答10倍分布外探测。与域随机化（domain randomization）不同，每个世界独立撰写和验证；通过损坏标签对照实验区分"标签精确性"与"任务多样性"的贡献。该方法在ARC-AGI网格、List Functions、CLRS等真实基准上应用，并测试了跨世界形式（如用128个不相交世界训练一个适配器）。框架由OpenWorld提供实现。

Result: 增益在能力最稀缺处最大：0.5B模型提升+29个点，而最大模型的提升在噪声范围内（趋于饱和）。在List Functions上，跨世界形式用128个不相交世界训练一个适配器，在未见世界上达到40%，而损坏标签对照仅6%（+34点，95% CI [29,39]）。合成-校验的动态在20步rollout上精确，并能100%精确回答10倍OOD探测，而逐步LLM和MLP预测器会复合误差并崩溃。该增益是饱和性规律：少步推理和小/弱模型上最大，在长链、感知诱导任务和饱和任务上减弱，跨任务迁移若无共享技能则较弱。

Conclusion: 作者将该增益界定为"饱和性规律"而非普适定律：对少步推理和小/弱模型最有效，对长链推理、感知型（pixel-native）任务和饱和任务作用有限；跨任务迁移需要共享技能。适用范围限定于符号状态领域，像素原生领域仍属于学习模型的领地。所有代码、配方和手稿均可从一个仓库再生，OpenWorld为零依赖框架。

Abstract: LLMs generalize across a domain only after seeing many real, labeled examples, which most domains lack. We study a way to manufacture it cheaply. When a domain's dynamics can be written as code, one template instantiates into many world models: executable, verifiable programs over symbolic state, each an inexhaustible source of exactly-labeled trajectories. Fine-tuning an LLM on trajectories through many such worlds, which we call world-time compute, a training-time analogue of test-time compute, lifts generalization to held-out worlds it never trained on (synthesized world families). Gains are largest where capability is scarcest: +29 points at 0.5B; the largest model's lift is within noise, consistent with saturation. Labels can be trusted because the worlds are verified code: synthesized-then-checked dynamics are exact over 20-step rollouts and answer 10x out-of-distribution probes exactly (100%), whereas per-step LLM and MLP predictors compound error and collapse. Unlike domain randomization, each world is independently authored and verified; a corrupted-label control shows label exactness, not task variety, drives the gains. On real benchmarks (ARC-AGI grids, List Functions, CLRS) the same lever holds as per-world test-time training. On List Functions the harder cross-world form holds: one adapter trained on 128 disjoint worlds reaches 40% on held-out worlds versus 6% for a corrupted-label control (+34 points, CI [29, 39]). The gain is a saturating regularity, not a law: largest for few-step reasoning and small/weak models, fading for long chains, perception-induced tasks, and saturated tasks; cross-task transfer is weak without shared skill. Worlds are authored and served by OpenWorld, a zero-dependency framework (companion paper). Scope: symbolic state; pixel-native domains remain territory of learned models. All code, recipes, and this manuscript regenerate from one repository.

</details>


### [153] [Scaling Post-Training Ternarisation to Qwen3-8B Capability Retention, Reproduction, Lossless Packing, and Packed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09240)
*Anirudh Malik,M Sparsh Mehra,Poojith Devan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将超低比特（"1.58-bit"级）后训练量化/三元化转换流程从Qwen3-4B规模化扩展至Qwen3-8B，采用KOTMS旋转、E2M-ATQ自适应三元化和GPTQ式误差补偿（A16权重仅量化配置），系统刻画了端到端规模化特性。8B模型在三个语料库上取得1.361x的困惑度比率，八项零样本任务中达到FP16的78.5%机会校正保留率；打包检查点为8.24 GiB并支持直接打包执行（15.52 tokens/s），验证了规模化基线，即更大模型尺寸提升对激进后训练离散化的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 名义上的"1.58-bit"标签并未指明实际部署的表示形式及其执行代价。超低比特语言模型承诺减少存储和内存流量，但缺乏对其端到端规模化执行成本与能力损失的严谨刻画。作者旨在通过将聚合式后训练转换流程从4B扩展至8B，提供一个经过外部复现验证的规模化基线，从而回答"更大模型是否更能承受激进后训练离散化"以及"实际序列化与直接执行是否可行"等核心问题。

Method: 方法为聚合式后训练转换管线的规模化扩展：采用KOTMS旋转、E2M-ATQ自适应三元化以及GPTQ式误差补偿，在权重仅A16配置下运行。作者不声称这些算法是新的，其贡献在于端到端规模化特征刻画，具体包括：外部复现门控、匹配的4B/8B能力对比分析、跨语料库困惑度评估、有效比特数核算、无损格点感知打包以及直接打包执行。

Result: 8B模型三语料库（WikiText-2、C4、PTB）困惑度比率分别为1.318x、1.393x和1.371x，总体1.361x。在n=500的八项零样本任务中，平均准确率64.6%（对比FP16的72.4%），对应78.5%的机会校正保留率和7.8个百分点的绝对代价；匹配的4B运行保留69.6%，显示8B有8.9个百分点的优势。打包检查点为8.24 GiB，困惑度与原始测量精度一致；直接打包执行在7.35 GiB内存下达到15.52 tokens/s，而初步打包GEMV仍慢于FP16 cuBLAS。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个经过验证的规模化基线：模型尺寸提升增强了对激进后训练离散化的鲁棒性，实际序列化难题在测量工件上得到解决，直接执行具备可行性。然而，更广泛的数据种子、校准分布选择以及内核优化仍属开放问题。

Abstract: Ultra-low-bit language models promise reductions in storage and memory traffic, but a nominal "1.58-bit" label does not specify the deployed representation or its execution cost. We study a scale-up of an aggressive post-training conversion pipeline from Qwen3-4B to Qwen3-8B.
  The conversion uses KOTMS rotation, E2M-ATQ adaptive ternarisation, and GPTQ-style error compensation in a weight-only A16 configuration. We do not claim these algorithms as new. Our contribution is the end-to-end scale-up characterisation: an external reproduction gate, matched 4B/8B capability analysis, cross-corpus perplexity, effective-bit accounting, lossless lattice-aware packing, and direct packed execution.
  The 8B model reaches a three-corpus perplexity ratio of 1.361x, with WikiText-2, C4, and PTB ratios of 1.318x, 1.393x, and 1.371x. On eight zero-shot tasks at n = 500, mean accuracy is 64.6% versus 72.4% for FP16, corresponding to 78.5% chance-corrected retention and a 7.8-point absolute cost. The matched 4B run retains 69.6%, yielding an 8.9-point 8B advantage.
  The packed checkpoint is 8.24 GiB and preserves the recorded perplexity to measurement precision. Direct packed execution reaches 15.52 tokens/s in 7.35 GiB, while a preliminary packed GEMV remains slower than FP16 cuBLAS. The result is a validated scale-up baseline: model size improves robustness to aggressive post-training discretisation, actual serialisation is solved for the measured artefact, and direct execution is feasible, while broader seeds, calibration distributions, and kernel optimisation remain open.

</details>


### [154] [Distribution-Consistent Inference for Dynamic Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09241)
*Dohyeon Kim,Bedionita Soro,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出推理期动态减少top-k专家路由会引发SMoE输出的分布偏移（RMS尺度和方差增大），从而加剧性能退化；作者提出轻量级的层间分布对齐（LDA）校正方法，利用逐层校准统计量将降路由表示对齐回默认配置，在多个SMoE大模型和基准上恢复了大部分因分布偏移损失的性能，且保持稀疏推理的高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽能扩展模型容量并保持高效推理，但多数模型采用固定top-k专家选择策略，对所有token分配相同专家预算。推理期动态路由虽可减少计算量，但现有方法忽视了偏离训练期路由配置所引发的分布偏移问题，导致性能下降。

Method: 作者首先实证发现减少激活专家数量会一致性地增大SMoE输出的RMS尺度与方差，造成表征失配；据此提出Layer-wise Distribution Alignment (LDA)，一种基于逐层校准统计量的轻量级推理期校正方法，将降路由表征与默认配置对齐。

Result: 在多种SMoE大语言模型、基准与路由策略上，LDA在降路由条件下恢复了因分布偏移损失的大部分性能，同时几乎不引入额外开销，保持了稀疏推理的效率。

Conclusion: 分布偏移是降路由性能退化中可校正的重要组成部分，LDA以极低的推理成本有效补偿该偏移，为动态路由在MoE模型中的实际部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have emerged as a powerful paradigm for scaling model capacity while preserving efficient inference in large foundation models. However, most MoE models use a fixed top-$k$ expert selection policy, assigning the same expert budget to every token even when fewer experts may be sufficient. Inference-time dynamic top-$k$ routing can reduce computation without retraining, but existing methods often overlook the distributional shift caused by deviating from the training-time routing configuration. We show that reducing the number of activated experts consistently increases the RMS scale and variance of SMoE outputs, inducing a representation mismatch that contributes to downstream performance degradation in addition to the loss of expert capacity. To address this correctable component, we propose Layer-wise Distribution Alignment (LDA), a lightweight inference-time correction that uses layer-wise calibration statistics to align reduced-routing representations with the default configuration. Across multiple SMoE LLMs, benchmarks, and routing strategies, LDA recovers much of the performance lost induced by the distributional shift under reduced routing while preserving sparse-inference efficiency with negligible overhead.

</details>


### [155] [DiffLUT-Net: Differentiable Training of FPGA LUT Networks with Learnable Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09254)
*Jiaqi Ye,Xinrui Gong,Jingcun Wang,Olga Kondrateva,Bing Li,Grace Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiffLUT-Net 提出一种 FPGA 原生的神经网络架构，其网络由六输入查找表（LUT）直接连接而成，并从头端到端训练。方法通过可微的 LUT 函数松弛与硬件源选择，联合学习每个 LUT 的 64 项真值表条目及六个输入端口的数据来源；训练后对真值表和连接进行离散化、剪除无用逻辑，并直接导出为可综合的 Verilog。在五个基准上取得了良好的精度-资源权衡，证明联合学习 LUT 函数与稀疏连接对紧凑型 FPGA 原生推理的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 FPGA 神经网络部署流程要么加速乘累加（MAC）运算，要么将预训练量化模型转换成查找表（LUT），两种方式都未将网络本身设计为 FPGA 原生结构，导致不能充分利用 LUT 资源并受限与预训练模型。作者旨在直接从 FPGA 硬件结构出发，训练一种由 LUT 直接连接构成的网络。

Method: DiffLUT-Net 将网络定义为由六输入 LUT 组成的连接拓扑。训练时采用可微的 LUT 函数松弛（以连续方式表示 64 项真值表）和可微的硬件源选择（学习每个输入端口连接自哪个上游节点），从而实现联合端到端学习。训练完成后将真值表和连接离散化，剪除未使用的逻辑，并直接导出为可综合的 Verilog 代码。

Result: 在五个基准任务上，DiffLUT-Net 相比现有方法取得了更优的精度-资源权衡（favorable accuracy-resource trade-offs），展现了在紧凑资源占用下保持较好精度的能力。

Conclusion: 联合学习 LUT 函数与稀疏连接是构建紧凑 FPGA 原生推理网络的有效途径，可显著减少硬件资源开销并直接生成可综合硬件描述，代码已开源。

Abstract: Field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) enable efficient neural-network inference, but most deployment flows either accelerate multiply-accumulate operations or convert pretrained quantized models into lookup tables (LUTs). We present DiffLUT-Net, an FPGA-native network connected by six-input LUTs that are trained from scratch. We jointly learn the 64 truth-table entries of a LUT and the source to each of its six input ports using a differentiable LUT function relaxation and hardware source selection. After training, the truth tables and connections are discretized, unused logic can be pruned, and the network is exported directly as synthesizable Verilog. Across five benchmarks, DiffLUT-Net achieves favorable accuracy-resource trade-offs. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of jointly learning LUT functions and sparse connectivity for compact FPGA-native inference. The code is available at https://github.com/TUDa-HWAI/DiffLUT-Network.

</details>


### [156] [Accountable and uncertainty-aware evaluation of sensor-based AI under distribution shift: devices, subjects, and nearly three years underground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09257)
*Benny Platte,Rico Thomanek,Christian Roschke,Marc Ritter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种分阶段、可问责的评估协议，用于真实部署条件下（设备、人员、记录时间漂移）的传感器AI系统。协议包含四个累积泛化阶段（留出设备、受试者、时间），以重复训练的分位数对比具有正确类别数的机会基准来判定，并通过"超出当前范围率"暴露模型对部署中已不存在类别的隐性误导，同时用5%分位数（而非均值）作为显式决策规则。在真实地下矿井的无基础设施地磁定位任务中验证：在训练后34个月、未知设备代际及留出调查员条件下，5%分位数精度为0.39（299次重复训练），为机会水平的16.5倍；42个可达位置类别间的波动为±0.08，远超重复运行间的差异。双峰配置在均值检验下显著通过而其5%分位数低于机会水平一个数量级以上，证明均值会产生误导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器AI系统很少在训练条件下的环境运行：设备、人员和记录历元不断变化，每次变化都会以随机训练-测试划分无法揭示的方式降低性能。因此需要一种能够甄别这些分布漂移对部署模型真实影响的严谨评估方法。

Method: 提出一个分阶段、可问责的评估协议，将已部署模型的评估视为一种测量，声明参考水平并量化不确定性。具体包含：(1) 四个累积泛化阶段，分别留出设备、受试者和时间；(2) 每阶段以重复训练的分位数对具有正确类别数的机会基准进行判定；(3) 引入超出现场范围率（out-of-present-scope rate），暴露模型向训练中存在但部署中已不再存在类别的隐性误导；(4) 以5%分位数而非均值作为显式决策规则。在真实地下矿井的智能手机循环神经网络无基础设施地磁定位上验证，并在第二现场复制训练阶段。

Result: 在训练后34个月、训练时未知的设备代际、留出调查员条件下，未更改模型在299次重复训练中现时条件精度的5%分位数为0.39，为机会水平的16.5倍；在42个可达位置类别间该值波动±0.08，为重复运行间差异的数倍。双峰配置示例表明：其均值检验显著通过，但5%分位数低于机会水平一个数量级以上，揭示均值为何误导。

Conclusion: 均值指标在评估部署模型时具有误导性，应以5%分位数等分位数指标作为决策依据。所提出的协议通过分阶段留出设备、受试者与时间，结合机会基准和超出现场范围率，可对长期部署的传感器AI系统进行严谨、可问责的评估，其有效性已通过真实地下矿井34个月跨度数据的实证验证。

Abstract: Sensor-based AI systems are rarely operated under the conditions under which they were trained: devices, personnel and recording epochs change, and each change degrades performance in ways a random train-test split cannot reveal. We propose a staged, accountable evaluation protocol that treats the evaluation of a deployed model as a measurement with declared reference levels and a quantified uncertainty. Four cumulative generalisation stages hold out devices, subjects and time. Each stage is judged on quantiles of repeated trainings against chance references with the correct class count, an out-of-present-scope rate exposes silent misdirection towards classes that are no longer present in deployment relative to training, and an explicit decision rule ties roll-out decisions not to means but to 5% quantiles. We demonstrate the protocol on infrastructure-free geomagnetic localisation with smartphone-based recurrent classifiers in two real underground mines, including a replication of the scheme's training stages at the second site. Unchanged models are re-evaluated on data recorded 34 months after the training campaign, on a device generation unknown at training time and with a held-out surveyor. The 5% quantile of their present-conditioned precision there is 0.39 over 299 repeated trainings, 16.5 times the chance level; across the composition of the 42 reachable location classes the figure varies by +/-0.08, several times the spread between repeated runs. Repeated trainings of a single configuration show why means mislead: a bimodal configuration passes a mean-based test decisively while its 5% quantile lies more than an order of magnitude below chance.

</details>


### [157] [Literati: Towards Anytime Optimal Shape Generalized Trees via AO*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09299)
*Nakul Upadhya,Eldan Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Literati，首个用于最优形状广义决策树（SGT）归纳的算法。通过新颖的 AND/OR 图建模，同时优化树结构与形状函数复杂度，并基于改进的 AO* 算法求解，在 24 个真实数据集上优于现有顶尖树方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贪心决策树归纳算法在可解释性方面表现出色，但会产生次优且不必要的复杂结构。最优决策树方法通过全局优化克服了这一问题，但仍局限于轴对齐的阈值分裂，限制了节点表达力，迫使树变得更深更复杂才能捕捉非线性特征效应。SGT 通过可学习的单变量形状函数泛化阈值分裂，提升表达力并生成更紧凑的树，但现有 SGT 归纳算法是贪心的，缺乏最优性保证。

Method: 本文提出 Literati，首个最优 SGT 归纳算法。核心贡献包括：(1) 提出一种新颖的 AND/OR 图公式化，联合优化树结构和形状函数复杂度；(2) 开发基于 AO* 的求解算法，并引入两个增强机制以在保持最优性的同时提升 anytime 性能——用于 OR 节点选择的次级启发式，以及用于 AND 节点探索的轮询（round-robin）策略。

Result: 在 24 个真实数据集上，Literati 在训练准确率和测试准确率上均高于现有最先进的树方法。

Conclusion: Literati 通过最优方式归纳 SGT，兼顾了表达力、紧凑性和最优性保证，在准确率与树复杂度之间取得了更优的平衡，为可解释机器学习提供了更强的方案。

Abstract: Decision trees are prized for their interpretability and strong performance on tabular data, but popular greedy top-down induction algorithms can yield suboptimal and unnecessarily complex structures. Optimal decision tree methods address this through global optimization, yet remain restricted to axis-aligned threshold splits, which limit the expressivity of each node and often force deep, complex trees to capture non-linear feature effects. Shape Generalized Trees (SGTs) generalize threshold splits to learnable univariate shape functions, improving expressivity and enabling more compact trees. However, existing SGT induction algorithms are greedy and offer no optimality guarantees. In this work, we introduce Literati, the first algorithm for optimal SGT induction. We propose a novel AND/OR graph formulation of the problem that jointly optimizes tree structure and shape function complexity. To solve this AND/OR graph, we develop an AO*-based algorithm with two enhancements that improve anytime performance while preserving optimality: a secondary heuristic for OR-node selection and a round-robin policy for AND-node exploration. Across 24 real-world datasets, Literati achieves higher training and test accuracy than state-of-the-art tree approaches.

</details>


### [158] [Explaining f-Divergence-Based Regularization via Local Curvature and Sharpness-Aware Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09367)
*Nour Jamoussi,Marios Kountouris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过经典f-散度的二阶展开，揭示了散度正则化与锐度感知最小化（SAM）在局部一致性上的深层联系：两者在参数空间扰动下均诱导曲率敏感惩罚，散度正则化产生Fisher加权二次型，而SAM惩罚主导Hessian特征值所对应的锐度。对于指数族输出分布的负对数似然目标，Fisher与Gauss-Newton矩阵重合，使对应关系尤为清晰。研究进一步将该几何视角拓展至输入空间扰动，其中散度正则化通过输入变换诱导拉回二次型，比标准SAM提供更通用的扰动框架。实验部分采用非对称α-偏斜Jensen-Shannon散度族作为受控试验平台，其局部曲率系数按α(1-α)缩放，在对称点α=1/2处达到最大并恢复标准JSD。损失景观可视化表明更强的诱导曲率惩罚与更平坦的局部极小值相关联；四个基准数据集的实验证实精确度和负对数似然在最大曲率惩罚区域附近持续达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散度正则化和SAM都是深度学习泛化改进的著名方法，均根植于对扰动的鲁棒性，但二者关系此前尚未被充分探索。作者旨在建立这两种方法的理论联系，并提供一个统一的几何框架来理解它们。

Method: 作者利用f-散度的经典二阶展开，在数学上证明散度正则化与SAM在参数空间扰动下局部一致，二者均诱导曲率敏感惩罚。具体而言，散度正则化产生Fisher加权的二次型，SAM则通过主导Hessian特征值惩罚锐度。在指数族输出分布的负对数似然目标下，Fisher和Gauss-Newton矩阵一致，简化了对应关系。研究还将此视角推广到输入空间扰动场景。实验验证采用α-偏斜Jensen-Shannon散度族作为可控测试平台，辅以损失景观可视化和四个基准数据集的实证评估。

Result: 理论结果显示两种正则化方法在局部共享一致的几何解释，散度正则化在输入空间诱导的拉回二次型比标准SAM更具一般性。实验表明局部曲率系数α(1-α)在α=1/2（标准JSD）处最大，且更强的诱导曲率惩罚与更平坦的局部最小值相关。四个基准数据集的实验证实，在最大曲率惩罚区域附近，精确度和负对数似然均表现最佳。

Conclusion: 本文为散度正则化与SAM建立了统一的理论桥梁，证明二者在局部几何意义上等价，并提供了统一的扰动框架。实证结果验证了理论预测，即最大化曲率惩罚（对应标准对称JSD）能获得更好的泛化性能和损失表现，为深度学习中正则化方法的选择与设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Divergence-based regularization and Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) are two prominent approaches for improving generalization in deep learning, both motivated by robustness to perturbations. However, their relationship has remained largely unexplored. Building on classical second-order expansions of $f$-divergences, we show that the two methods are locally consistent under parameter-space perturbations: both induce curvature-sensitive penalties, with divergence regularization yielding a Fisher-weighted quadratic form and SAM penalizing sharpness through the dominant Hessian eigenvalue. For negative log-likelihood objectives with exponential-family output distributions, this correspondence becomes especially transparent, since the Fisher and Gauss-Newton matrices coincide. We further show that the same local geometric perspective extends to input-space perturbations, where divergence-based regularization is defined through transformations of the input. In this setting, the regularizer induces a pullback quadratic form on the input space, providing a more general perturbation framework than standard SAM while preserving the same local sensitivity interpretation. To validate the analysis empirically, we use the asymmetric $α$-skew Jensen-Shannon divergence (JSD) family as a controlled testbed. Its local curvature coefficient scales as $α(1-α)$ and is maximized at the symmetric point $α=\tfrac12$, which recovers the standard JSD. Loss-landscape visualizations in the input-perturbation regime show that stronger induced curvature penalization is associated with flatter local minima. Experiments on four benchmark datasets further demonstrate that both accuracy and negative log-likelihood are consistently best near this regime of maximal curvature penalization.

</details>


### [159] [Constraint-Aware Discrete Black-Box Optimization Using Tensor Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09370)
*Keisuke Onoue,Ryosuke Kojima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于张量分解的代理模型方法，用于离散黑箱优化问题。该方法将可行性逻辑约束直接集成到代理模型训练中，通过将训练形式化为带约束的多项式优化问题，并使用基于T-范数的可微惩罚项求解松弛形式，从而有效引导搜索远离不可行区域，提高样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散黑箱优化常通过序贯模型优化（SMBO）方法解决，利用代理模型逼近昂贵的目标函数。然而在许多实际问题中，可行输入集合由已知的逻辑约束定义，现有的代理建模技术未能捕获离散输入空间中支配可行性的符号规则，导致样本效率低下。

Method: 提出一种基于张量分解的代理建模方法，能够捕获离散搜索空间的结构，同时直接整合可行性信息。具体实现将代理模型训练形式化为一个带约束的多项式优化问题，并利用基于T-范数（三角范数）推导的微分惩罚项求解该问题的松弛形式。

Result: 在合成基准和真实世界基准（包括压力容器设计任务）上的实验表明，所提方法通过有效引导搜索远离不可行区域，显著提高了样本效率。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地将逻辑可行性约束融入基于张量分解的代理模型，为解决具有符号约束的离散优化问题提供了一种新颖且有效的方案，在样本效率方面优于现有方法。

Abstract: Discrete black-box optimization is often addressed using approaches such as Sequential Model-Based Optimization (SMBO), which aims to improve sample efficiency by fitting surrogate models that approximate a costly objective function over a discrete search space. In many real-world problems, the set of feasible inputs is often given by logical constraints known in advance. However, existing surrogate modeling techniques generally fail to capture the symbolic rules governing feasibility in discrete input spaces. In this paper, we propose a surrogate modeling approach based on tensor decomposition that captures the structure of discrete search spaces while directly integrating feasibility information. To implement this approach, we formulate surrogate model training as a constrained polynomial optimization problem and solve a relaxed formulation using a differentiable penalty term derived from T-norms. Our experiments on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks, including a pressure vessel design task, demonstrate that the proposed method improves sample efficiency by effectively guiding the search away from infeasible regions.

</details>


### [160] [XAI-Refine: An Automated Explanation-Knowledge Loop for Brain-Age Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09388)
*Yang Qiao,Junjie Wu,Deqiang Qiu,James J. Lah,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出XAI-Refine框架，将后验解释方法与神经科学文献知识相结合，形成一个自动化"解释-知识"循环，用于大脑年龄预测模型的证据引导式精炼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑年龄预测模型通常仅以预测准确率评估，但准确率不能证明模型依赖可复现或神经生物学支持的机制。现有后验解释工作流往往止步于诊断，或需要在模型分析前预先指定纠偏目标，缺乏自动化、知识驱动的模型精炼途径。

Method: XAI-Refine采用迭代循环：在每次迭代中，（1）将多次训练运行中互补的后验分析整合为可靠的结构化模型解释；（2）将每个可靠解释转化为中性的神经生物学问题；（3）检索并验证相关文献，将经过验证的证据编译为与解释同类型空间的"可接受集合"；（4）以当前模型解释在该可接受集合上的最小投影作为精炼目标；（5）在保留原始模型变量、测量算子与适用范围的条件下，将修正后的解释翻译为可微约束；（6）仅当多种子验证确认目标导向的"解释移动"、预测性能保持在预设护栏内、且非目标解释漂移有界时，才采纳候选更新。

Result: 在基于静息态功能连接的大脑年龄预测实验中，论文评估了预测性能、解释可靠性、文献对齐度与目标特异性模型修正，展示了从后验分析到证据引导模型精炼的结构化路径。

Conclusion: XAI-Refine提供了一条从后验解释到证据引导模型修订的自动化、可验证的路线，兼顾预测性能护栏与解释目标导向性，为大脑年龄预测模型的神经生物学可信度提供了保障机制。

Abstract: Brain-age prediction models are commonly evaluated by predictive accuracy, yet accurate predictions alone do not establish that a model relies on reproducible or neurobiologically supported mechanisms. Post-hoc explanation methods can expose these mechanisms, but existing workflows typically stop at diagnosis or require correction targets to be specified before model analysis. We propose XAI-Refine, an automated explanation-knowledge loop for brain-age prediction from resting-state functional connectivity. At each iteration, XAI-Refine consolidates complementary post-hoc analyses across repeated training runs into reliable, structured model explanations. It converts each reliable explanation into a neutral neurobiological question, retrieves and verifies relevant literature, and compiles the verified evidence into an admissible set in the same typed explanation space. The target for refinement is defined as the minimal projection of the current model explanation onto the admissible set induced by applicable verified knowledge. This revised explanation is then translated into a differentiable constraint while preserving the originating model variable, measurement operator, and applicable scope. Candidate updates are promoted only when multi-seed validation confirms target-directed explanatory movement, predictive performance remains within a prespecified guardrail, and non-target explanatory drift remains bounded. Experiments on functional-connectivity-based brain-age prediction evaluate predictive performance, explanation reliability, literature alignment, and target-specific model revision, illustrating a structured route from post-hoc analysis to evidence-guided model refinement.

</details>


### [161] [Applying foundation model embeddings towards urban livability evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09429)
*Ayush Khot,Wen Zhou,Shaowen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个系统性框架，利用基础模型嵌入（如AlphaEarth、AnySat、TerraMind）从高分辨率地理空间数据中提取物理特征，以预测数据稀缺地区的城市宜居性等社会经济指标，并帮助研究者识别最具预测力的地理空间指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据稀缺地区，社会经济指标的准确测量仍然困难，限制了政策干预和资源分配；而高分辨率地理空间数据广泛可得且包含宜居性相关信息，因此需要探索如何从这些数据中提取可操作的信息。

Method: 论文提出了一个系统性框架，用于：(1) 分析不同地理空间数据类型对城市宜居性预测的影响；(2) 识别基础模型嵌入（AlphaEarth、AnySat、TerraMind）中编码的物理特征；(3) 确定最具预测力的地理空间指标；(4) 利用基础模型嵌入提升预测性能，同时考虑复杂的空间依赖性。

Result: 该工作提供了一种从卫星影像中提取可操作信息的原理性方法，能够增强数据稀缺地区城市宜居性相关结果的预测表现，并帮助研究者根据具体应用优先选择最有信息量的特征。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一套用于数据稀缺区域城市宜居性预测的原则性方法论，通过基础模型嵌入从地理空间数据中提取有效信息，为政策制定和资源分配提供支持。

Abstract: While accurate measurement of socioeconomic indicators remains challenging in data-scarce regions, which limits policy interventions and resource allocation, high-resolution geospatial data is widely available and can contain information on various livability statistics. We investigate which physical features are encoded within foundation model embeddings, such as AlphaEarth, AnySat, and TerraMind, and provide a systematic framework for identifying the most predictive geospatial indicators. By analyzing how different types of geospatial data influence urban livability predictions, our approach enables researchers to prioritize the most informative features for their specific applications. Additionally, we demonstrate how to leverage foundation model embeddings to enhance prediction performance for these outcomes. This work contributes a principled methodology for extracting actionable information from satellite imagery while accounting for complex spatial dependencies, with applications in predicting urban livability in regions with limited observation data.

</details>


### [162] [SCCM : Stream Cruise Control Method for Automated Drift Detection and Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09432)
*Mohammad Abu-Shaira,Weishi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了流巡航控制方法（SCCM），一个用于在线回归的漂移检测与自适应的综合框架。SCCM 通过早期响应、更新前漂移检测、漂移幅度量化、基于KPI窗口的自适应阈值、动态超参数调优和模型重校准实现自动化自适应；采用内存内设计以支持实时适应性，并对数据分布保持无关性。作者将SCCM集成到四种在线回归模型中，在18个合成数据集（涵盖突变、增量、交替渐变漂移）和8个真实数据集上，使用R² 和MSE指标，与8个检测-自适应基线对比，结果显示预测性能提升且漂移处理更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界数据集常出现概念漂移（分布随时间演化），忽略漂移会导致预测性能下降，而固定超参数进一步限制了模型在变化条件下的适应性。现有方法多为纯反应式，往往在性能退化后才触发自适应。因此需要一个支持自动自适应、实时性、对数据分布无关且可扩展的综合漂移检测与适应框架。

Method: SCCM框架包含多个核心组件：早期响应式、更新前的漂移检测；漂移幅度量化；基于KPI窗口的阈值设定以缓解局部误报；动态超参数调优；模型重校准。同时采用内存内设计实现实时适应性，使用动态阈值保持对数据分布无关（支持KPI监控，可处理高维和大规模数据流）。该框架可与不同在线回归模型集成。

Result: SCCM集成到四种在线回归模型，在18个合成数据集（含突变、增量、交替渐变漂移）和8个真实数据集上，采用R² 与MSE指标，与8个检测—自适应基线对比，结果表明SCCM在在线回归场景下获得了更好的预测性能，并能有效处理各类漂移。

Conclusion: SCCM作为面向在线回归的漂移检测与自适应综合框架，通过提前响应和内存内设计克服了纯反应式方法的滞后性，在多种漂移类型、高维和大规模数据流上均展现出改进的预测性能与有效的漂移处理能力。

Abstract: Real-world datasets often exhibit evolving distributions, known as concept drift. Ignoring drift degrades predictive performance, while reliance on fixed hyperparameters further limits model adaptability under changing conditions. Adaptive learning addresses this challenge by continuously updating models online, allowing them to incrementally adjust and remain effective as data distributions evolve. This paper presents the Stream Cruise Control Method (SCCM), a comprehensive framework for drift detection and adaptation in online regression. SCCM enables automated adaptation through early-response, pre-update drift detection, drift magnitude quantification, KPI-window-based thresholding for local false-alarm mitigation, dynamic hyperparameter tuning, and model recalibration. SCCM also adopts an in-memory design for real-time adaptability, unlike purely reactive methods that typically activate adaptation only after performance degradation is observed. By using dynamic thresholding and remaining agnostic to data distributions, SCCM supports KPI-based monitoring across varying data streams, including high-dimensional and large-scale settings. SCCM is integrated with four online regression models and evaluated on 18 synthetic datasets covering abrupt, incremental, and alternating gradual drift, together with eight real-world datasets. The evaluation uses both R2 and MSE and compares against eight detector--adaptation baselines. Results show improved predictive performance and effective drift handling across the evaluated online regression settings.

</details>


### [163] [Efficient Leakage-Free Neural Architecture Search under Leave-One-Subject-Out Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09433)
*Heinke Hihn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无泄漏的基于块的神经架构搜索（NAS）方法，用于解决完全嵌套的留一受试者（LOSO）评估计算开销大的问题，在BioVid Heat Pain数据集上提升了平均准确率并大幅减少了参数数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LOSO评估虽能估计基于受试者分类的泛化性能，但完全嵌套实现需要N次独立架构搜索，计算成本近似O(N^2)，使得NAS在计算上非常昂贵。

Method: 提出一种无泄漏的基于块的方法，在不同受试者之间共享NAS运行，从而避免为每个受试者单独执行完整搜索，同时保持评估有效性。

Result: 在BioVid Heat Pain数据集上，该方法将平均准确率从82.79%提升到83.39%，同时将参数数量最多减少了99.2%。

Conclusion: 所提出的分块共享方法在降低计算开销的同时，提升了模型性能并显著压缩了模型规模，是一种高效的LOSO评估替代方案。

Abstract: Leave-One-Subject-Out (LOSO) evaluation estimates generalisation performance for subject-based classification but makes Neural Architecture Search (NAS) computationally expensive because a fully nested implementation requires N independent architecture searches and, assuming approximately linear training cost, scales as O(N^2). We propose a leakage-free, block-based approach that shares NAS runs across subjects. On the BioVid Heat Pain dataset, our approach increased the mean accuracy from 82.79% to 83.39% while reducing the number of parameters by up to 99.2%.

</details>


### [164] [Tensor-Train Weak SINDy: Identifying High-Dimensional Nonlinear Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09434)
*Will Houser,Vanja Dukic,David M. Bortz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TT-WSINDy方法，将MANDy与WSINDy技术结合，并在张量列（TT）格式中实现弱形式变换、回归和稀疏化等计算，能够搜索指数增长的候选函数空间而不受维数灾难影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱形式方法在数据驱动的动力系统发现方面取得了显著进展，但在高维场景下，现有技术在计算量和内存消耗上均非常昂贵，亟需一种能克服维数灾难的高效方法。

Method: 将MANDy与WSINDy两种方法的技术相结合，把所有必要计算（弱形式变换、回归、稀疏化）在张量列（TT）格式中实现，从而高效处理指数级增长的候选函数空间。

Result: 该方法能够搜索指数增长的候选函数空间，并完成弱形式变换、回归与稀疏化全过程，同时不会遭受维数灾难，在高维设置下实现计算与内存上的显著优化。

Conclusion: TT-WSINDy成功结合了弱形式识别与张量网络压缩的优势，为高维动力系统稀疏辨识提供了一种可扩展的解决方案，克服了以往方法在高维下的计算与内存瓶颈。

Abstract: In recent years, weak-form methods have made significant advances in data-driven discovery of dynamical systems. However, in high-dimensional settings, current techniques can prove expensive in both computation and memory. In this work, we introduce TT-WSINDy, which combines techniques of the Multidimensional Approximation of Nonlinear Dynamics (MANDy) and Weak Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (WSINDy) methods, implementing requisite computations in the tensor-train (TT) format. We demonstrate that this method is able to search an exponentially-growing space of candidate functions -- performing weak-form transformation, regression, and sparsification -- without suffering from the curse of dimensionality.

</details>
