Although its formulation has shown promising initial results, a systematic understanding of how its components interact across diverse SSS scenarios remains lacking. In this paper, we present a systematic cross-domain study of KLPCDA to characterize the interaction mechanisms among its core objectives. We analyze the behavior of its seven variants across multiple real-world SSS tasks, including hyperspectral image classification, mechanical fault diagnosis, medical diagnosis, and face recognition. Through extensive experiments and ablation studies, we investigate how different objective combinations influence performance under varying conditions such as noise, class imbalance, and high dimensionality. Our analysis reveals consistent patterns in the interaction of the three core objectives variance, between-class, and within-class terms, providing a unified and interpretable understanding of their roles in stabilizing representations and enhancing discrimination in SSS settings. Based on these findings, we further derive practical guidelines for selecting appropriate KLPCDA variants under different data characteristics. Experimental results demonstrate that KLPCDA achieves strong and robust performance across domains, while maintaining low computational complexity suitable for resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [198] [Development and Validation of a Physics-Guided Machine Learning Extrapolation Framework Using a Classical Transient Diffusion Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09912)
*Ashutosh Yadav,Alok Dubey,Prodyut Ranjan Chakraborty,Harshal Akolekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的外推框架，将其与成熟机器学习架构（双向长短期记忆网络BiLSTM和物理信息神经网络PINN）相结合，实现训练域之外的准确、物理一致预测。以经典一维瞬态扩散问题为基准（其精确解析解提供无限可靠数据），通过物理引导坐标变换、边界感知学习策略和稳定性增强的时间推进来提升外推可靠性，并采用“训练-预测-验证-扩展”策略递归扩展预测范围。结果表明该方法能在训练域外进行准确且物理一致的预测，适用于数据有限工程场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程中机器学习模型通常仅在有限运行范围内训练，但可靠预测常超出该范围，因此核心挑战是外推而非插值。而训练范围外数据稀缺使严格验证困难，亟需一种能保证物理一致性且可严格验证的外推框架。

Method: 采用经典一维瞬态扩散问题作为基准（利用其解析解提供无限可靠数据，可严格量化验证）。系统比较两种物理引导架构（BiLSTM与PINN），并融入物理引导坐标变换、边界感知学习策略与增强稳定性的时间推进。采用训练-预测-验证-扩展（train-predict-validate-extend）策略，将验证过的预测递归加入训练集以渐进扩展预测范围。该基准问题极具挑战性：解从初始奇异性经强非线性瞬态区演化到稳态线性剖面，且仅用中间段数据训练时向后外推至奇异性尤为困难。

Result: 实验表明两种物理引导架构能够对训练域之外进行准确且物理一致的预测，验证了框架在数据有限工程应用中的外推潜力。

Conclusion: 该外推框架结合物理引导策略，可有效克服训练范围外数据稀缺带来的验证困难，实现准确、物理一致的域外预测，为数据受限工程的可靠外推提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Machine learning models used in engineering are typically trained within limited operating ranges, yet reliable predictions are often required beyond these domains. Consequently, the primary challenge is extrapolation rather than interpolation. Rigorous validation is hindered by the scarcity of data outside the training range. To address this limitation, a novel extrapolation framework is integrated with established machine learning architectures to enable accurate and physically consistent predictions beyond the training domain. The framework is established by systematically evaluating two physics-guided architectures: a Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) network and a Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN). A classical one-dimensional transient diffusion problem is adopted as a benchmark because its exact analytical solution provides unlimited, reliable data across the spatio-temporal domain, enabling rigorous quantitative validation. The problem is particularly challenging because the solution evolves from an initial singularity through a strongly nonlinear transient regime before approaching a steady-state linear profile. When training data are confined to an intermediate portion of this evolution, backward extrapolation toward the singularity becomes especially demanding. To improve reliability, physics-guided coordinate transformations, boundary-aware learning strategies, and stability-enhancing temporal marching are incorporated. Extrapolation is evaluated using a train-predict-validate-extend strategy, in which validated predictions are recursively added to the training set to progressively extend the prediction horizon. The results demonstrate accurate and physically consistent predictions beyond the training domain, highlighting the framework's potential for engineering applications where data availability is limited.

</details>


### [199] [Multi-Pass, Multi-View Blended Learning for High-Fidelity Volumetric CT Synthesis from Chest X-Rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09920)
*Ozer Can Devecioglu,Serkan Kiranyaz,Rashid Mazhar,Tahir Hamid,Muhammad Chowdhury,Moncef Gabbouj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多通道多视角混合学习框架（Multi-Pass Multi-View Blended Learning），用于直接从真实胸部X光片（CXR）重建高保真体积CT。该方法将任务分解为两个互补阶段：第一阶段进行无监督的CXR到DRR的域适应，第二阶段包含三次传递（有监督DRR到CT转换、无监督多视角切片细化、渐进式迁移学习），有效缓解了合成图像与真实图像之间的域差距，在LIDC-IDRI数据集上PSNR提升最高达14%，SSIM提升7.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张二维胸部X光片重建三维体积CT本质上是一个病态逆问题，且面临配对CXR-CT训练数据稀缺的挑战。现有方法依赖数字化重建放射影像（DRR）进行训练，但DRR与真实CXR之间存在显著域差距，导致在临床应用时重建结果粗糙且解剖结构不一致。

Method: 提出混合学习框架（Blended Learning），将任务逐步分解为两个互补阶段。Stage 1为无监督CXR-to-DRR域适应（Domain Adaptation），消除合成与真实图像之间的域差距；Stage 2包含三次传递：(a) 有监督的DRR-to-CT变换（Transformation），(b) 无监督的多视角切片细化（Multi-View Slice Refinement），以及(c) 渐进式迁移学习（Progressive Transfer Learning, PTL）。通过这种混合学习范式，同时增强最终输出的结构完整性和解剖细节。

Result: 在LIDC-IDRI数据集上（提供配对DRR-CTground truth进行定量评估），该方法相比先前方法在PSNR上最高提升14%，在SSIM上提升7.6%。框架能够从真实CXR生成结构一致、解剖学上真实的高保真CT体积。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了从真实X光片重建结构一致且解剖学真实的高保真CT体积，标志着从标准放射影像重建CT向临床可行性迈出的重要一步。

Abstract: Reconstructing volumetric Computed Tomography (CT) from a single 2D chest radiograph (CXR) is an ill-posed inverse problem, further complicated by the scarcity of paired CXR-CT training data. Prior approaches address this by training on Digitally Reconstructed Radiographs (DRRs), which are synthetic projections derived from CT volumes. However, the domain gap between DRRs and real CXRs limits generalization, often resulting in coarse or anatomically inconsistent reconstructions when applied to clinical images. To address this challenging problem, this study introduces a Multi-Pass Multi-View Blended Learning framework for synthesizing high-fidelity volumetric CT directly from real chest X-ray (CXR) images. The proposed approach progressively decomposes the synthesis task into two distinct, complementary learning stages. Stage 1 is an unsupervised CXR-to-DRR Domain Adaptation, while Stage 2 includes three passes, namely, (a) supervised DRR-to-CT Transformation, (b) unsupervised Multi-View Slice Refinement, followed by (c) Progressive Transfer Learning (PTL). With such a blended learning paradigm, the proposed approach mitigates the synthetic-to-real domain gap while enhancing both the structural integrity and anatomical detail of the final output. On the LIDC-IDRI dataset, where paired DRR-CT ground truth is available for quantitative evaluation, the proposed method improves upon prior methods by up to 14% in PSNR and 7.6% in SSIM. The framework successfully generates structurally consistent and anatomically realistic high-fidelity CT volumes from real CXRs, marking a significant advancement toward clinical viability of CT reconstruction from standard radiographic images.

</details>


### [200] [Adversarial Training for Tabular Credit Scoring: A Multi-Attack Robustness Evaluation in P2P Lending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09945)
*Gijs A. F. Niewzwaag,Marijn G. S. Veth,Manuele Massei,Marcos R. Machado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对P2P借贷中基于机器学习的信用评分模型在面对对抗性操纵（申请人策略性篡改自报特征以获取有利决策）时的鲁棒性展开系统研究。作者在大型Lending Club数据集子集上构建了"训练-测试"鲁棒性基准，覆盖三类模型（逻辑回归、前馈神经网络、表格数据Transformer）和四种攻击（FGSM、PGD、Salt-and-Pepper噪声、DeepFool）及混合攻击场景。通过分层交叉验证的全网格评估发现：对抗训练能显著提升针对其训练攻击的鲁棒性，且在梯度类攻击族内迁移良好，但对非梯度型扰动迁移较弱；混合训练在保持干净测试性能的同时，能为异构攻击提供最均衡的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗鲁棒性研究大多集中于图像和文本领域，且通常只评估单一攻击与单一防御的匹配，缺乏对表格信用数据中防御如何跨攻击类型泛化的系统认识。申请人针对信用评分模型的可操控性（如篡改自报收入、职业等可变特征）尚未被充分理解，因此需要对表格信用评分模型进行系统性的多攻击、多模型的鲁棒性评估。

Method: 在Lending Club大型数据子集上构建系统性的"训练-测试"鲁棒性基准：采用三类模型（逻辑回归、前馈神经网络、表格数据Transformer），四种仅作用于申请人可变特征的攻击（FGSM、PGD、Salt-and-Pepper噪声、DeepFool）以及混合攻击机制；通过分层交叉验证在整个网格上进行评估，比较对抗训练与混合训练对不同攻击的鲁棒性迁移表现。

Result: 对抗训练能显著提升针对其训练的特定攻击的鲁棒性，并且在梯度类攻击（如FGSM、PGD、DeepFool）族内部具有良好的迁移性；但对非梯度型扰动（如Salt-and-Pepper噪声）的迁移能力较弱，说明单一攻击防御会高估现实世界的鲁棒性。混合训练在异构攻击间提供了最均衡的鲁棒性，同时保持了干净的测试性能。

Conclusion: 单一攻击的对抗训练不足以应对现实场景中的多样化攻击，会高估模型的实际鲁棒性。混合多攻击训练是表格信用评分中最稳健的防御策略，能在不牺牲干净测试性能的前提下提供跨异构攻击的均衡鲁棒性，因此建议在信用模型治理中采用多攻击压力测试框架。

Abstract: Machine learning-based credit scoring is increasingly central to Peer-to-Peer (P2P) lending, yet its resilience to adversarial manipulation, where applicants strategically alter self-reported inputs to secure favourable decisions, remains poorly understood. Most adversarial-robustness evidence comes from image and text domains and evaluates a single attack against a matching defence, offering little guidance on how defences generalise across attack types in tabular credit data. We address this with a systematic train-test robustness benchmark on a large Lending Club subset, spanning three model families (logistic regression, a feed-forward neural network, and a transformer for tabular data) and four attacks confined to applicant-mutable features: Fast Gradient Sign Method (FGSM), Projected Gradient Descent (PGD), Salt-and-Pepper (S&P) noise, and DeepFool, plus a mixed-attack regime. Across a full grid evaluated with stratified cross-validation, adversarial training sharply improves robustness against the attack it is trained on and transfers well within the gradient-based family, but transfers weakly to non-gradient corruption, so single-attack defences overstate real-world resilience. Mixed training delivers the most balanced robustness across heterogeneous attacks while preserving clean-test performance, supporting multi-attack stress testing in credit-model governance.

</details>


### [201] [Deep Neural Networks for Learning Intent from sEMG Signals to Support Hardware Devices for Post-Stroke Neurorehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09971)
*Zakariyya Brewster,Divy Wadhwani,Emily Yan,Aidan Wang,Karma Namgyal,Shuting Xie,Markiyan Konyk,Tala Abdelmaguid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于PhysioMio卒中患者双侧纵向sEMG数据集，研究了五指多标签运动意图解码。通过统一的信号处理协议与特征提取，比较了LSTM、CNN、GNN三种基线，并进一步通过架构搜索与跨通道知识蒸馏，得到了一个仅123K参数、可导出ONNX的紧凑CNN模型，在四通道硬件约束下实现了可靠的五指意图预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对卒中后康复，手指特异性运动意图是临床上重要的控制信号；即使患者运动微弱或不完整，残留肌肉活动仍可被sEMG测量。因此需要一种可复现的处理路径，从患侧sEMG中解码五指意图，并适配嵌入式/硬件受限场景。

Method: 采用20–450 Hz巴特沃斯滤波与Symlet-4小波去噪、200 ms重叠窗口分段，每通道提取12个时频域特征。先比较LSTM、CNN、GNN三种基线，再进行架构搜索确定CNN-Large与紧凑版CNN-Micro；最后在四通道硬件设计约束下（ECRB、ECRL、FDS、FDP，排除接地电极），使用跨通道知识蒸馏训练学生模型，并以五粒种子评估。

Result: 基线中LSTM子集准确率最高（0.545），GNN的macro F1（0.706）与macro AUPRC（0.776）最高；CNN-Large单折子集准确率0.593、macro F1 0.714。经知识蒸馏，四通道CNN-Micro达到子集准确率0.5219±0.0114、手指准确率0.7612±0.0038、macro F1 0.6095±0.0058；模型仅123K参数，接受9个窗口×48特征并已导出ONNX。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了从卒中后sEMG到紧凑五指意图预测的可复现软件路径，为后续硬件在环评估奠定基础，同时证明知识蒸馏能弥补通道数缩减带来的性能损失。

Abstract: Finger-specific motor intent is a clinically meaningful control signal for post-stroke neurorehabilitation, where residual muscle activity may remain measurable despite weak or incomplete movement. We study five-finger multilabel intent decoding from impaired-arm high-density surface electromyography (sEMG) in PhysioMio, a bilateral longitudinal dataset collected from stroke patients. A common processing protocol aligns movement labels, applies 20--450 Hz Butterworth filtering and Symlet-4 wavelet denoising, segments overlapping 200 ms windows, and extracts twelve time- and frequency-domain descriptors per channel. Direct LSTM, CNN, and GNN baselines reveal complementary behavior: the LSTM attains the highest subset accuracy (0.545), whereas the GNN attains the highest macro F1 (0.706) and macro AUPRC (0.776). Architecture search then identifies CNN-Large as the strongest single-split CNN, with 0.593 subset accuracy and 0.714 macro F1, while CNN-Micro provides a compact architecture for embedded inference. To match a four-sensor hardware design, we retrain CNN-Micro using channels associated with ECRB, ECRL, FDS, and FDP and exclude the ground electrode from model input. Across five seeds, cross-channel knowledge distillation improves the four-channel student over direct training, reaching $0.5219 \pm 0.0114$ subset accuracy, $0.7612 \pm 0.0038$ finger accuracy, and $0.6095 \pm 0.0058$ macro F1. The selected 123K-parameter model accepts nine windows of 48 features and has been exported to ONNX. These results establish a reproducible software path from post-stroke sEMG to compact five-finger intent prediction for subsequent hardware-in-the-loop evaluation.

</details>


### [202] [An Explainable Machine Learning Framework for Predicting Blood-Brain Barrier Permeability Using Molecular Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10012)
*Fatemeh Mahmoudi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个可解释的机器学习框架，利用RDKit从MoleculeNet BBBP数据集中2,039种化合物提取15种理化描述符，训练了逻辑回归、SVM、随机森林和XGBoost四种算法预测血脑屏障通透性。优化后的XGBoost分类器表现最佳，准确率88.97%、召回率97.76%、F1分数93.13%、ROC-AUC为0.9282；五折交叉验证平均ROC-AUC为0.8982。SHAP与特征重要性分析一致表明TPSA、HBD和LogP是最具影响力的分子描述符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血脑屏障通透性是中枢神经系统药物开发的关键决定因素，直接影响候选药物到达脑内靶点的能力。因此需要建立准确、可解释且高效的通透性预测模型，以支持CNS药物的早期筛选。

Method: 从MoleculeNet BBBP数据集提取2,039种化合物的15种理化描述符（基于RDKit工具包），训练四种监督学习算法（逻辑回归、支持向量机、随机森林、XGBoost）；采用GridSearchCV进行超参数优化，并利用SHAP进行模型可解释性分析，同时进行分层五折交叉验证评估鲁棒性。

Result: 优化后的XGBoost分类器取得最佳预测性能，准确率88.97%、精确率88.92%、召回率97.76%、F1分数93.13%、ROC-AUC 0.9282；分层五折交叉验证平均ROC-AUC为0.8982±0.0130。特征重要性与SHAP分析一致确认TPSA、HBD、LogP为最关键的分子描述符。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架提供了一种准确、可解释且计算高效的BBB通透性预测方法，可作为CNS候选药物早期筛选的实用工具。

Abstract: Blood-brain barrier (BBB) permeability is a critical determinant in the development of central nervous system therapeutics because it directly influences the ability of drug candidates to reach their target sites within the brain. In this study, an explainable machine learning framework was developed to predict BBB permeability using molecular descriptors generated from the MoleculeNet BBBP dataset with the RDKit cheminformatics toolkit. Fifteen physicochemical descriptors extracted from 2,039 compounds were used to train four supervised machine learning algorithms, including Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machine (SVM), Random Forest, and Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost). Hyperparameter optimization was performed using GridSearchCV, while model interpretability was investigated using SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP). Among the evaluated models, the optimized XGBoost classifier achieved the best predictive performance, with an accuracy of 88.97%, a precision of 88.92%, a recall of 97.76%, an F1-score of 93.13%, and a ROC-AUC of 0.9282. Stratified five-fold cross-validation further demonstrated the robustness of the proposed model, yielding a mean ROC-AUC of 0.8982 +/- 0.0130. Feature importance and SHAP analyses consistently identified TPSA, HBD, and LogP as the most influential molecular descriptors governing BBB permeability prediction. Overall, the proposed framework provides an accurate, interpretable, and computationally efficient approach for BBB permeability prediction and may serve as a valuable tool for the early-stage screening of CNS drug candidates.

</details>


### [203] [MetroLLM-Bench: Evaluating Language Models as Transit Kiosk Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10016)
*Remco Hendriks*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MetroLLM-Bench，一个包含 955 个案例的基准测试，用于检验语言模型作为地铁售票亭策略层的表现。基准覆盖 6 个真实地铁系统（37 至 414 个车站）和 11 个类别（含路线规划、票价计算、中断、无障碍与对抗性输入），要求模型调用结构化工具并提交可机器渲染的终端状态。评估包含 14 个确定性评分组件（Tier 1）与 8 个语义质量组件（Tier 2，其中 6 个使用语言模型裁判），按分层 75/25 划分训练与留出测试集。对 6 家厂商 26 个模型（23 个参与排名）进行评估：留出集上，经 PEFT 训练的 4B Qwen 3.5 学生模型在 Tier 1（91.3）超过 GPT-5.6 两档（90.6 和 90.0），并以 Q4_K_M 2.6GB 体积匹配 GPT-5.4 满推理（91.4）；更大规模（9B、27B）学生未见进一步提升。规则基线达 84.6，语言模型优势集中于策略自适应、复合场景、无障碍与时间推理。Muse Glimmer 30B 居综合榜首，服务配置本身可将 Qwen 3.5 与 3.8 的对比移动 2.7 个 Tier 1 分。基准、测试框架、复现指南与微调学生模型均已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏专门针对语言模型作为真实地铁售票亭策略层的结构化基准测试，现有评测往往忽视工具调用、可机器渲染状态输出、票价计算等运营约束，难以衡量模型在真实交通场景中的决策能力与对抗性输入应对能力。

Method: 构建 955 案例的两层基准：Tier 1 采用 14 个确定性评分组件，Tier 2 采用 8 个语义质量组件（其中 6 个由语言模型裁判评定）。覆盖 6 个真实地铁系统（37-414 站）与 11 个类别，按分层 75/25 划分 717 训练与 238 留出案例。评估 6 家厂商 26 个模型（23 个排名），包括基于 Qwen 系列的 PEFT 学生模型（2B 至 27B，多随机种子训练）与确定性规则基线，并考察服务配置对分数的影响。

Result: 留出集上，4B Qwen 3.5 PEFT 学生模型 Tier 1 得分 91.3，超过 GPT-5.6 两档（90.6、90.0），并以 2.6GB Q4_K_M 体积匹配 GPT-5.4 满推理（91.4）；9B、27B 学生无进一步提升。PEFT 相对基线的增益随规模增大从 2B 的 +7.03 降至 27B 的 -0.91，各种子方向一致。规则基线达 84.6，语言模型优势集中在策略自适应、复合场景、无障碍与时间推理。Muse Glimmer 30B 综合排名第一；服务配置变动可使 Qwen 3.5 与 3.8 对比相差 2.7 个 Tier 1 分。

Conclusion: MetroLLM-Bench 可有效区分模型作为地铁售货亭策略层的表现；小型 PEFT 学生模型在 Tier 1 上可匹敌乃至超越顶级商业模型，且成本与体积显著更低，表明该场景下小模型微调具有高性价比。同时揭示服务配置与推理预算对评分有可观影响，需在评测中加以控制。

Abstract: We introduce MetroLLM-Bench, a 955-case benchmark for testing language models as the policy layer of a transit kiosk. It covers six real metro systems, ranging from 37 to 414 stations, and eleven categories that include routing, fare calculation, disruptions, accessibility, and adversarial input. In each case, the model must call structured tools and submit a machine-renderable terminal state containing an outcome, a per-ticket fare quote when applicable, and a kiosk action. Fourteen deterministic scoring components form Tier 1; eight semantic-quality components form Tier 2, six of which use a language-model judge. We report Tier 1 and the combined score of both tiers. A stratified 75/25 split reserves 717 cases for training-data generation and 238 for held-out evaluation.
  We evaluate twenty-six models from six vendors, of which twenty-three are ranked. On the held-out partition, a 4B Qwen 3.5 student trained through parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) exceeds both GPT-5.6 tiers on Tier 1 (91.3 against 90.6 and 90.0) and matches GPT-5.4 full at maximum reasoning effort (91.4), with a 2.6 GB Q4_K_M footprint. Larger 9B and 27B students provide no further Tier 1 improvement over the 4B student at this training scale. Across the four Qwen sizes, the PEFT gain over the corresponding base model decreases from +7.03 points at 2B (three training seeds) to -0.91 at 27B; every seed shows the same direction at every size. A deterministic rule-based baseline reaches 84.6 on Tier 1, with the remaining language-model advantage concentrated in policy adaptation, compound scenarios, accessibility, and temporal reasoning. Muse Glimmer 30B leads the composite ranking, and serving configuration alone moves the Qwen 3.5-to-3.8 comparison by 2.7 Tier 1 points. The benchmark, harness, reproduction guide, and fine-tuned students are released at https://github.com/continker/metrollm-bench.

</details>


### [204] [Structure-Aware Unsupervised Anomaly Detection for Spacecraft Telemetry with Adaptive EVT Thresholding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10017)
*Óscar Alcarria,Rafael Sánchez,Javier Sempere,Pablo Torrijos,Juan C. Alfaro,Juan M. Auñón,José A. Gámez,José M. Puerta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需标签、无需故障先验知识、无需任务特定调参且部署即用的无监督航天器遥测异常检测框架，通过增量月度重训练、统计模型选择与自适应极值理论（EVT）阈值控制虚警，在ESA-AD数据集上以严格时序评估取得Mission 1 F0.5=0.700、Mission 2 F0.5=0.698。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际运行中的航天器遥测异常检测通常依赖标注的历史异常数据或较长的预热期（warm-up），而这些条件在实际部署中很少能满足，从而制约了现有方法的落地可行性。作者因此瞄准构建一种能快速上线、无需任何监督信息的实用检测框架。

Method: 采用无监督方案，核心包含三个组成部分：1) 增量式月度重训练，使模型随新数据滚动更新；2) 统计模型选择，自动挑选适配当前数据分布的检测模型；3) 自适应极值理论（EVT）阈值设定，用于动态控制误报（false alarm），从而在无标签情况下维持可接受的告警率。

Result: 在欧空局异常数据集（ESA-AD）上，于严格的时序（chronological）评估设置下，Mission 1 达到 F0.5=0.700，Mission 2 达到 F0.5=0.698，验证了该框架在真实条件下的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架证明：无需历史标签、故障知识或任务特定调参，仅依靠月度增量重训练与自适应EVT阈值即可在运营次月实现可靠的无人监督异常检测，具备实际部署价值，可显著降低遥测异常监测的部署门槛。

Abstract: Operational anomaly detection in spacecraft telemetry typically requires labeled historical anomalies or extended warm-up periods. These requirements are rarely met in practice. We propose an unsupervised, deployment-ready framework that produces predictions from the second month of operation without any labels, prior fault knowledge, or mission-specific tuning. The approach combines incremental monthly retraining, statistical model selection, and adaptive Extreme Value Theory (EVT) thresholding for false alarm control. On the ESA Anomalies Dataset (ESA-AD), it achieves $F_{0.5}=0.700$ on Mission~1 and $F_{0.5}=0.698$ on Mission~2 under strict chronological evaluation.

</details>


### [205] [Beyond Contact Sensors: Deep learning with Pseudo-Labeling for remote Photoplethysmography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10026)
*Bhargav Acharya,Barbara Hammer,Hanna Drimalla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了用无监督信号处理方法提取的伪标签能否替代接触式传感器标签来训练深度学习rPPG方法。结果表明，在同步质量不佳的数据集上，伪标签方法优于监督训练；在同步良好的数据集上两者表现相近，但跨数据集评估中伪标签方法效果较差，剔除单个异常参与者后其性能显著提升，证明信号处理方法可以生成有效的训练信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习rPPG方法在复杂场景下性能优于传统信号处理方法，但其训练依赖视频与接触式传感器采集的金标准信号之间的精确同步标注，这类数据集收集成本高昂且耗费人力。相比之下，信号处理方法不依赖标注数据。研究者希望探究在何种条件下，用无监督信号处理方法生成的伪标签可以替代接触式传感器标签来训练深度学习模型，从而降低对人工标注数据集的依赖。

Method: 研究者对信号处理方法生成的伪标签与接触式传感器标签这两种深度学习训练方案进行了系统性对比评估，考察不同数据集的同步质量对训练效果的影响。评估涵盖两类场景：同步不完美的数据集和同步良好的数据集，并在每种场景下分别进行数据集内（within-dataset）和跨数据集（cross-dataset）评估，同时还分析了剔除异常参与者对伪标签方法性能的影响。

Result: 在同步不完美的数据集上，伪标签方法优于在接触式传感器标签上的监督训练。在同步良好的数据集上结果呈混合状态：数据集内评估显示两种训练方法无显著差异，而跨数据集评估偏向于监督训练。然而，剔除单个异常参与者后，伪标签方法的跨数据集性能显著提升，表明标签质量对结果有重要影响。

Conclusion: 研究证明信号处理方法能够为深度学习模型生成有效的训练信号，在同步质量不佳的场景下甚至优于监督训练方法。这可以降低对劳动密集型数据集收集工作的依赖，同时保持具有竞争力的性能，为rPPG远程心率估计的实际应用提供了低成本、可扩展的训练方案。

Abstract: Heart rate is a critical biomarker of health, and remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) enables its contactless estimation from video data for telemedicine applications. Recent advancements in deep learning based rPPG methods achieve state-of-the-art results, outperforming classical signal-processing methods in complex scenarios. However, deep learning methods depend on datasets with precise synchronization between videos and ground truth signals collected via contact sensors, whereas signal-processing-based methods do not. To address this dependence on labeled datasets, which are labor-intensive to collect, we investigate under which circumstances pseudo-labels extracted using unsupervised signal-processing methods can replace contact sensors labels for training deep learning methods. Our systematic evaluations found that for datasets with imperfect synchronization, the pseudo-label approach outperforms supervised training on contact sensors. For datasets with good synchronization, results are mixed: within-dataset evaluation shows no significant difference between training methods, while cross-dataset evaluation favors supervised training. However, removing a single outlier participant significantly improves the pseudo-label approach's cross-dataset performance, highlighting the importance of label quality. These results demonstrate that signal-processing methods can generate valid training signals for deep learning models, reducing dependency on labor-intensive dataset collection while maintaining competitive performance.

</details>


### [206] [Field-level prediction of mid-plane stress tensor fields in concrete target penetration: a cross-velocity graph neural operator surrogate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10032)
*Wenpu Du,Peng Zhou,Yunlong Xia,Sinuo Xin,Congcong Zhang,Boyang Zhang,Yi Zhang,Wenzheng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个将混凝土中尺度非均质性（骨料随机分布）与全场景应力张量场预测联系起来的框架。利用LS-DYNA全尺度骨料解析模型生成400个案例（4种冲击速度×100个骨料种子）的六分量应力张量场数据集，并通过场级图神经算子（GNO）代理模型学习时变应力场演化，实现了跨速度的外推预测，并在验证基础上将该数据库确立为可复现资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管混凝土的抗冲击性能已被广泛研究，但缺乏一个将中尺度非均质性（骨料随机种子）与全场景应力张量预测联系起来、且能支撑跨速度外推的统一框架。现有的研究未能将骨料级随机异质性映射到完整的应力场演化预测，这一空白制约了对冲击响应机理的深入理解和可靠快捷的预测手段。

Method: 使用全尺度骨料解析的LS-DYNA模型生成数据（抛射体直径45mm、质量2.13kg，靶体直径500mm×厚200mm，网格10mm），并通过构型相似性对照已发表的穿深实验（Frew 2006、Hanchak 1992、Forrestal 1996）进行验证。数据集包含X-Z中平面上六分量应力张量场，共400个案例（4种冲击速度×100个骨料种子）。主要方法贡献包括：(1)逐案例验证终态穿深，界定静止态有效性与可靠观测锚点；(2)建立场级图神经算子（GNO）代理模型学习时变应力场演化并进行跨速度留一外推评估；(3)建成全尺度、骨料解析、跨速度、逐种子的可复现数据库。

Result: 100、135和200 m/s案例在观察窗结束时仍在运动（负速度即回弹），仅1个165 m/s案例被阻滞（穿深69.33 mm），因此穿深不作为静止态标量报告（除该阻滞案例外）。鼻尖节点深度差异被确认为侵蚀后位移积分的数值伪影。图神经算子单步相对L2误差为0.6977（如实报告）；从第11帧到第39帧的自回归滚动约需144 ms，相对单核LS-DYNA速度提升约3.6×10³至4.3×10³倍，作为应用价值报告。

Conclusion: 框架的验证受到构型相似性与场级自洽性的约束，该方法是经过仿真训练、在所研究参数空间内的决策支持方法，而非普适通用模型。研究确立了利用骨料解析的中尺度异质性驱动场级应力演化预测的可行路径，并提供了宝贵的数据资源。

Abstract: Although the impact resistance of concrete has been studied extensively, a framework linking mesoscale heterogeneity to full-field stress-tensor prediction has been lacking. Data were generated with a full-scale aggregate-resolved LS-DYNA model (projectile diameter 45 mm, mass 2.13 kg, target diameter 500 mm x thickness 200 mm, mesh 10 mm), verified against published penetration experiments (Frew 2006, Hanchak 1992, Forrestal 1996) by configuration similarity. The dataset contains six-component stress-tensor fields on the X-Z mid-plane for 400 cases (4 impact velocities x 100 aggregate seeds). Three contributions are reported. First, case-by-case verification of the terminal penetration state delimited the rest-state validity of penetration depth and anchored reliable observables to rigid-body motion and field-level stress evolution. Second, a field-level graph neural operator surrogate learned the time-varying stress-field evolution and evaluated cross-velocity leave-one-out extrapolation. Third, the full-scale, aggregate-resolved, cross-velocity, per-seed database was established as a reproducible resource. Cases at 100, 135 and 200 m/s still moved at window end (negative velocity, i.e. rebound), and only one 165 m/s case arrested. Penetration depth is therefore not reported as a rest-state scalar except for the single arrested case (69.33 mm); nose-node depth differences were confirmed as numerical artifacts of displacement integration after erosion. The single-step relative L2 error was 0.6977, reported honestly; autoregressive rollout from frame 11 to 39 took about 144 ms, a speedup of about 3.6x10^3 to 4.3x10^3 relative to single-core LS-DYNA, reported as application value. Validation is bounded by configuration similarity and field-level self-consistency; the framework is a simulation-trained decision-support method within the studied parameter space.

</details>


### [207] [Hybrid Quantum-Classical NLP Classification with Compact Semantic Representations: An Experimental Analysis of Representation Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10089)
*Ali Hassan,Zijia Zhao,Maha A. Metawei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究一种混合量子-经典NLP流水线：将高维句子嵌入经降维压缩后输入变分量子电路进行分类。系统对比了PCA（无监督）、NCA和LDA（有监督）三类降维方法，基于TREC问题分类数据集考察维度、信息保留、量子比特数与分类性能的关系。结果表明有监督降维（LDA达85.3%、NCA达83.1%，仅用5维）远比基于方差的压缩（PCA在8维时仍仅63.4%）高效，并接近384维全经典基线（85.1%），为实用化混合量子-经典NLP模型提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型和句嵌入模型能提供丰富的语义表征，但其高维特性对近期量子机器学习构成挑战，因为量子电路只能处理有限数量的输入特征。作者旨在探索如何将高维句嵌入压缩为紧凑表示，使其能够适配变分量子分类任务，从而推动混合量子-经典NLP的实际应用。

Method: 构建混合量子-经典流水线，包含预训练句嵌入模型、降维、角度编码（angle encoding）、变分量子电路（VQC）及经典决策层。系统比较三种降维方法：PCA（无监督）、NCA与LDA（有监督），在TREC问题分类数据集上研究表示维度、信息保留率、量子比特数与分类性能之间的关系，并采用无泄漏（leakage-free）的交叉验证协议。

Result: 初步PCA实验暴露明显信息瓶颈：将768维嵌入降至3/4/5/8维时仅保留约8.2%/10.2%/11.9%/16.4%的方差，对应分类准确率为50.3%/51.2%/57.9%/63.4%。相比之下有监督降维显著更高效：LDA达85.3%、NCA达83.1%（仅用5维），而384维全经典基线为85.1%。

Conclusion: 有监督降维能远比基于方差的压缩更有效地保留任务相关信息，使得紧凑表示成为通向实用混合量子-经典NLP模型的可行途径。

Abstract: Large language and sentence-embedding models provide rich semantic representations, but their high dimensionality poses a challenge for near-term quantum machine learning (QML), where quantum circuits can process only a limited number of input features. We investigate a hybrid quantum-classical pipeline that transforms high-dimensional sentence embeddings into compact representations for variational quantum classification. The workflow combines a pretrained sentence-embedding model, dimensionality reduction, angle encoding, a variational quantum circuit (VQC), and a classical decision layer. We systematically compare principal component analysis (PCA), neighborhood components analysis (NCA), and linear discriminant analysis (LDA), covering both unsupervised and supervised dimensionality reduction. Using the TREC question-classification dataset, we study the relationship between representation dimensionality, information retention, qubit count, and classification performance. Preliminary PCA experiments reveal a strong information bottleneck: reducing 768-dimensional embeddings to 3, 4, 5, and 8 dimensions retains about 8.2%, 10.2%, 11.9%, and 16.4% of the variance, with corresponding classification accuracies of 50.3%, 51.2%, 57.9%, and 63.4%. In contrast, supervised reduction is substantially more efficient. LDA reaches 85.3% accuracy and NCA reaches 83.1% using only 5 dimensions, under a leakage-free cross-validation protocol, compared with 85.1% for a full 384-dimensional classical baseline. These results indicate that supervised dimensionality reduction can preserve task-relevant information far more effectively than variance-based compression, making compact representations a promising route toward practical hybrid quantum-classical NLP models.

</details>


### [208] [A Systematic Evaluation of Molecule Generation Models for De Novo Drug Design: From Benchmarks to Practical Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10099)
*Xinrui Xu,Xueer Wang,Dan Luo,Sisi Yuan,Xuan Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本篇综述系统评估了用于全新药物设计的分子生成模型，涵盖5种深度生成框架下的82种方法（RNN/Transformer、VAE、GAN、流模型、扩散模型），总结了常用基准与分子表征、一般生成与口袋条件生成的方法论原理，并对基准与指标上的性能进行了系统性综合与比较分析，还归纳了代表性经实验验证的案例，最后展望了标准化3D数据、交互感知生成、受体灵活性与多目标分子设计等未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述通常只针对特定模型家族或应用场景进行孤立讨论，缺乏一个综合视角来阐明各组件（分子表征、生成架构、靶点感知策略）如何协同构成一条连贯的生成工作流。随着该领域因分子表征、生成架构和靶点感知建模的进步而快速发展，亟需一篇整体性的综述来填补这一空白。

Method: 该综述采用系统性综述方法：首先归纳广泛使用的基准数据集与分子表征方式；其次考察通用生成与口袋条件生成的底层方法论原理；然后跨常用基准和评估指标对报告性能进行系统性综合与比较分析；同时汇总代表性经实验验证的案例研究；所有基准资源、评估指标和模型参考文献均整理在公开仓库中。

Result: 本文对82种方法在5大深度生成框架（RNN/Transformer、VAE、GAN、流模型、扩散模型）下的方法进行了全面梳理，提供了跨基准与指标的系统性性能比较，并总结了已验证的实验案例；此外建立了公开可访问的资源仓库（GitHub）供社区使用。

Conclusion: 作者指出未来应关注标准化3D数据、交互感知生成、受体灵活性以及多目标分子设计等方向，旨在提升分子生成的可靠性与实验相关性，从而推动生成模型在全新药物设计中发挥更大作用。

Abstract: Molecule generation has emerged as a powerful computational tool for de novo drug design, enabling the exploration of chemical space beyond the limits of conventional virtual screening. The field has progressed rapidly, driven by advances in molecular representations, generative architectures, and target-aware modeling strategies. However, existing reviews typically address specific model families or application scenarios in isolation, rather than offering an integrated perspective on how these components collectively form a coherent generation workflow. In this review, we present a comprehensive evaluation of molecule generation models for de novo drug design, covering 82 methods across five deep generative frameworks, including recurrent neural network (RNN)- and Transformer-based models, variational autoencoders (VAEs), generative adversarial networks (GANs), flow-based models, and diffusion models. We first summarize widely used benchmarks and molecular representations, and then examine the methodological principles underlying both general and pocket-conditioned generation. A central contribution of this work is a systematic synthesis and comparative analysis of reported performance across commonly used benchmarks and evaluation metrics. We also summarize representative experimentally validated case studies. Looking ahead, we discuss future directions in standardized 3D data, interaction-aware generation, receptor flexibility, and multi-objective molecular design, with the aim of improving the reliability and experimental relevance of molecule generation. All collected benchmark resources, evaluation metrics, and model references are provided in a publicly accessible repository at https://github.com/JacklinGroup/molecule-generation-review.

</details>


### [209] [A Trust-Network-Based Federated Learning Framework for Multi-Center Aging Clock Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10108)
*Chunxu Zhang,Bo Li,Wenliang Wang,Yang Liu,Di Jiang,Yuan Huang,Yo-ichi Nabeshima,Akinori Yamamura,Bo Yang,Qiang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TNFL，一种基于信任网络的无中心化联邦学习框架，用于在分布式分子数据集上构建可解释的老化时钟。TNFL结合年龄感知的混合专家模型和生成性重放，应对本地样本量小、信任稀疏、模型漂移与遗忘等挑战，并通过分析所识别的蛋白质相互作用揭示老化的高阶生物组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老化时钟可量化生物学老化并刻画个体健康状况，但理解哪些蛋白质相互作用对准确的老化时钟重要（以及其为零阶还是高阶）需要学习跨医学中心的大型分子数据集，而隐私约束禁止集中式数据共享。联邦学习作为自然方案面临四大挑战：本地样本量有限、中心间信任稀疏且方向性、需在保持判别性年龄预测的同时支持可解释性、以及异质跨中心数据下的模型漂移与遗忘。

Method: 提出TNFL框架，沿有向成对信任关系渐进传播模型而无集中聚合；结合年龄感知的混合专家（mixture-of-experts）模型与生成性重放（generative replay）来保留已学信息并减少遗忘和漂移；随后通过功能与网络分析对TNFL识别的成对蛋白质相互作用及其高阶组织进行生物学探究。

Result: 在多个分子数据集上的实验表明，TNFL在有限本地数据下实现有效的老化时钟预测，提供可解释的年龄依赖预测模式，并在不同交互阶数下保持稳定性能。识别的蛋白质相互作用反复形成跨越多个老化相关生物系统的协同高阶子网络，多个蛋白质在子网络间反复出现。

Conclusion: TNFL捕获了超越孤立成对关联的分子关系，揭示了与老化相关的连贯高阶生物学组织，表明老化时钟所依赖的蛋白质相互作用具有高阶结构，而不仅是零阶/成对的关联。

Abstract: Aging clocks quantify biological aging and help characterize individual health status. What protein interactions are important for accurate aging clocks, and are they zeroth-order or higher-order? Addressing these questions requires learning from large molecular datasets distributed across medical centers, where privacy constraints prevent centralized data sharing. Federated learning offers a natural solution but faces four challenges in this setting: limited local sample sizes, sparse and directional inter-center trust, the need to retain discriminative age prediction while supporting interpretation, and model drift and forgetting under heterogeneous cross-center data.
  We propose TNFL, a trust-network-based federated learning framework that progressively propagates models along directed pairwise trust relations without centralized aggregation. TNFL combines an age-aware mixture-of-experts model with generative replay to preserve previously learned information and reduce forgetting and drift. Experiments across multiple molecular datasets show that TNFL enables effective aging-clock prediction with limited local data, provides interpretable age-dependent prediction patterns, and maintains stable performance across interaction orders.
  To investigate the biological questions, we analyze TNFL-identified pairwise protein interactions and their higher-order organization through functional and network analyses. The identified interactions repeatedly form coordinated higher-order subnetworks spanning multiple aging-related biological systems, with several proteins recurring across subnetworks. These findings suggest that TNFL captures molecular relationships beyond isolated pairwise associations and reveals coherent higher-order biological organization associated with aging.

</details>


### [210] [Storage-Scalable Progressive Semantic Communication via Knowledge-Base Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10112)
*Heng Zhu,Ye Liu,Kun Zhu,Feifei Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出存储可扩展的知识库复用量化（SSKBQ）方案，通过在一组紧凑的知识库上跨多个残差细化阶段复用，解耦传输阶段数与维护知识库数量，并引入阶段感知残差监督机制来规整中间量化表示、促进渐进细化，在解决存储可扩展性问题的同时保持有竞争力的渐进重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识库辅助语义通信方案分为单知识库量化（SKBQ）和多知识库残差量化（MKBQ）。SKBQ存储开销小但量化容量受限；MKBQ为每个阶段分配独立知识库以支持渐进细化，但知识库存储随传输深度线性增长，导致存储开销过大。本文旨在解决MKBQ存储随深度线性增长的可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出SSKBQ（存储可扩展的知识库复用量化）方案：在一组紧凑的知识库上跨多个残差细化阶段进行复用，从而将传输阶段数量与维护的知识库数量解耦；同时引入阶段感知残差监督机制，对中间量化表示进行正则化，鼓励渐进细化。

Result: 实验结果表明，知识库复用为存储可扩展性问题提供了有效解决方案，同时保持了具有竞争力的渐进重建性能。

Conclusion: SSKBQ通过知识库复用成功解决了知识库存储随传输深度线性增长的问题，在降低存储开销的同时维持了渐进细化重建性能，证明了知识库复用是解决存储可扩展性问题的有效途径。

Abstract: Existing knowledge-base-assisted semantic communication schemes commonly adopt either single knowledge-base quantization (SKBQ) or multi-knowledge-base residual quantization (MKBQ). SKBQ incurs limited storage overhead but has restricted quantization capacity, whereas MKBQ supports progressive refinement by assigning an independent knowledge base (KB) to each stage, causing the KB storage to grow linearly with the transmission depth. To address this problem, we propose storage-scalable knowledge-base reuse quantization (SSKBQ), which reuses a compact set of KBs across multiple residual refinement stages and thereby decouples the number of transmission stages from the number of maintained KBs. A stage-aware residual supervision mechanism is further introduced to regularize intermediate quantized representations and encourage progressive refinement. Experimental results demonstrate that KB reuse provides an effective solution to the storage scalability problem while maintaining competitive progressive reconstruction performance.

</details>


### [211] [CompassOPD: Cross-Family On-Policy Distillation via Within-Family Likelihood Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10154)
*Naibin Gu,Qingyi Si,Chenxu Yang,Chuanyu Qin,Junhao Zhou,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CompassOPD，针对跨模型家族知识蒸馏（OPD）中教师与学生家族不一致导致性能退化的现象。作者将跨家族OPD信号分解为“低能力教师家族基准与学生之间的偏移”和“该基准到强教师的家族内对数似然位移”两部分，发现标准OPD将两者一同传递，使得偏移主导更新方向并掩盖教师能力提升带来的变化。CompassOPD通过去除该偏移、仅传递家族内位移，并以冻结的学生参考锚定更新，使教师侧和学生侧的变化都基于各自家族度量。实验表明在三个学生家族和多个教师家族下优于标准跨家族OPD，平均推理准确率最高提升5.50点；对MoE教师可直接通过减少专家激活构造基准，无需独立基准检查点，仍保留3.43点增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现OPD在同族教学中表现优异，但在跨家族设置下即使完成分词器对齐，效果也会显著退化，甚至更强的外部教师也几乎无法带来额外提升。作者希望理解这一现象背后的机制，并设计方法恢复跨家族蒸馏的有效性。

Method: 作者将跨家族OPD信号分解为两个分量：低能力教师家族基准与学生之间的偏移，以及从该基准到强教师的家族内对数似然位移。标准OPD将两者一起传递导致偏移主导更新。所提CompassOPD将这两个分量分离：去除偏移分量、只传递家族内位移，同时用冻结的学生参考将更新锚定在学生初始策略上，从而教师侧和学生侧的变化都在各自模型家族内度量。对MoE教师，通过减少专家激活直接从教师检查点构造基准，省去独立基准检查点。

Result: 在三个学生家族和多个教师家族的实验中，CompassOPD一致优于标准跨家族OPD，平均推理准确率最高提升5.50点。对于MoE教师，直接构造基准的方式在无需独立检查点的情况下仍比OPD保留3.43点增益。

Conclusion: 跨家族OPD性能恶化的根源在于偏移分量主导更新方向、掩盖了教师能力提升带来的有效信号。通过将更新与信号解耦——去除偏移、仅传递家族内位移并用冻结的参照锚定——可以有效恢复跨家族蒸馏的性能增益，且该方法对MoE教师可通过模型内部操作无需额外基准检查点实现。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense token-level supervision on student-generated trajectories. Although OPD performs strongly when teacher and student belong to the same model family, we find that its effectiveness degrades in cross-family settings even after tokenizer alignment, with substantially stronger external teachers offering little additional improvement. To understand this disconnect, we decompose the cross-family OPD signal into two components: an offset between a low-capability teacher-family reference and the student, and the within-family log-likelihood shift from that reference to the strong teacher. Standard OPD transfers both components together, allowing the offset to dominate the update direction and obscure the changes associated with teacher capability improvements. We propose CompassOPD, which removes this offset and transfers the within-family shift, while a frozen student reference anchors updates to the student's initial policy. Thus, both teacher-side and student-side changes are measured within their respective model families. Experiments across three student families and multiple teacher families show that CompassOPD consistently outperforms standard cross-family OPD, improving average reasoning accuracy by up to 5.50 points. For an MoE teacher, we further construct the reference directly from the teacher checkpoint by reducing expert activation, eliminating the need for a separate reference checkpoint while retaining a 3.43-point gain over OPD.

</details>


### [212] [CoGe-GCD: Reframing Generalized Category Discovery with Compositional Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10158)
*Luyao Tang,Jiewei Zheng,Kunze Huang,Chaoqi Chen,Yue Huang,Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoGe-GCD 方法，将广义类别发现任务重新定义为组合泛化问题，通过"组合感知"和"泛化归纳"两个耦合阶段，将补丁标记映射到小规模原语词汇表并进行结构保持的校准，从而提升对未见类别组合的外推能力，可在不修改头或损失函数的情况下即插即用于多种 GCD 框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义类别发现要求模型像人类一样进行组合推理——从已知类别中复用原语，并判断何时新组合意味着新类别。现有 GCD 方法基于非结构化的标记特征，难以外推到未知的组合，因此需要一种结构与组合感知的方法。

Method: CoGe-GCD 包含两个耦合阶段：(i) 组合感知——通过竞争式标记-原语分配和信息传递，将补丁标记结构化地映射到小规模原语词汇表，并精炼标记嵌入，形成用于发现的连贯分组；(ii) 泛化归纳——利用诱导出的几何结构，对空间关系施加结构保持的校准，在保持概率语义的同时改善对未见原语组合的外推。该方法作为骨干网络与投影头之间的归纳偏置模块实现，不改动头或损失函数。

Result: 在标准基准上，CoGe-GCD 一致性地提升了所有类别的准确率、未知类别数量估计和几何质量，且仅带来可忽略的计算开销。

Conclusion: CoGe-GCD 验证了将 GCD 重新概念化为组合泛化问题的有效性，其作为即插即用的归纳偏置模块可无缝集成到多种 GCD 框架中，代码已开源。

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) assigns unlabeled instances, mixed with labeled data, to known or novel categories, requiring human-like compositional reasoning: reusing primitives learned from known classes and deciding when new combinations imply new categories. Existing GCD methods operate on unstructured token features and struggle to extrapolate to novel compositions. We propose CoGe-GCD, which rethinks GCD through compositional generalization with two coupled stages. (i) Compositional Perception structures patch tokens by mapping them to a small vocabulary of primitives and refining token embeddings via competitive token-primitive assignment and information passing, yielding coherent groups for discovery. (ii) Generalizing Induction exploits the induced geometric structure and applies a structure-preserving calibration over spatial relations, maintaining probabilistic semantics while improving extrapolation to unseen primitive combinations. CoGe-GCD is implemented as an inductive-bias module between backbone and projection head, without modifying heads or losses, and can be plugged into diverse GCD frameworks. On standard benchmarks, it consistently improves all-class accuracy, unknown-class number estimation, and geometric quality, with marginal computational overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/lytang63/CoGe-GCD.

</details>


### [213] [An Exponential Deterministic--Randomized Gap in ERM-Oracle Complexity for Thresholds on an Unknown Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10196)
*Xuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究可通过一致性ERM预言机访问的阈值转导在线学习问题，证明在固定自然预言机（最小前缀/最大前缀规则）下，确定性学习者的“失误数+查询数”总和至少为 T-ε（精确常数），从而 O(log T) 失误需要 T-ε-O(log T) 次查询；而随机化学习者在该预言机下实现了 O(log T) 期望查询与失误。作者还证明该随机化顺序最优：给出了期望失误的下界，并论证分离由预言机选择规则而非类别本身导致（可行中位数规则下确定性学习者可达到 O(log T)），同时探讨了对抗性冻结预言机、固定查询预算下的部分权衡以及弱一致性预言机下的 Θ(T) 下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Attias、Hanneke 和 Ramaswami (NeurIPS 2025) 提出一个开放问题：当类别只能通过预言机访问时，随机化是否能在保证学习性能的同时真正减少在线学习所需的预言机调用次数。本文聚焦于他们特别指出的实例——在未知全序的 T 个实例上、以一致性类型 ERM 预言机进行阈值转导在线学习，旨在确定随机化在该具体设置下是否带来查询复杂度的分离。

Method: 作者针对固定的自然预言机（最小前缀规则或最大前缀规则）分析确定性学习者的查询-失误总代价，推导出精确的线性下界 M+Q ≥ T-ε。随后考察该文提出的随机化学习者在同一规则下的复杂度，并针对显式困难分布证明随机化顺序的最优性（期望失误下界）。为说明分离源于预言机选取规则而非类别，作者比较了可行中位数ERG规则（允许确定性 O(log T) 代价）与全局中位数规则（再次强制线性总代价）。此外还处理了对抗性冻结预言机、固定查询预算下的部分权衡，以及仅返回可实现性比特的弱一致性预言机场景。

Result: 主要结果为：在最小前缀/最大前缀预言机下，确定性学习者在某些实例上满足 M+Q ≥ T-ε（常数精确，ε∈{0,1}取决于空前缀是否为概念），因此 O(log T) 失误至少需 T-ε-O(log T) 次调用；随机化学习者则达到 O(log T) 期望调用与失误。在显式困难分布下，所有学习者的期望失误至少为 ((T+1-ε)·128^{-E[Q]}-1)/2，故 polylog 失误需要 Ω(log T) 期望调用。可行中位数ERM规则下确定性学习者可实现 O(log T) 调用与失误，而全局中位数规则和对抗性冻结预言机迫使线性总代价。仅给出可实现性比特的弱一致性预言机下，确定性与随机化学习者均需 Θ(T) 次调用。

Conclusion: 结论是：随机化确实可以在固定自然预言机下显著减少在线阈值学习的查询开销，但这依赖于具体的预言机选择规则；不同的ERM选取规则（可行中位数 vs 全局中位数）会决定是否存在随机化-确定性分离。固定查询预算的中间区域仍开放，弱一致性预言机则彻底丧失随机化的优势。总体表明预言机的内部机制是查询复杂度分离的关键因素。

Abstract: Attias, Hanneke and Ramaswami (NeurIPS 2025) asked whether randomization provably reduces the oracle calls needed for online learning when the class is accessible only through an oracle. We study the instance they singled out: transductive online learning of thresholds on an unknown total order of T instances, with a consistency-type ERM oracle that returns a full concept consistent with a queried labeled set (or reports non-realizability). Our main result is a separation for a fixed natural oracle. When the oracle is the minimal-prefix rule (or the maximal-prefix rule), every deterministic learner makes M mistakes and Q calls with $M+Q\ge T-\varepsilon$ on some instance ($\varepsilon\in\{0,1\}$, according to whether the empty prefix is a concept), and the constant is exact; hence $O(\log T)$ mistakes cost $T-\varepsilon-O(\log T)$ calls, whereas that paper's randomized learner achieves $O(\log T)$ expected calls and mistakes under the same rule. The randomized order is optimal: on an explicit hard distribution under the minimal-prefix rule, every learner has expected mistakes at least $((T+1-\varepsilon)\,128^{-\mathbb{E}[Q]}-1)/2$, so $Ω(\log T)$ expected calls are necessary for polylogarithmic mistakes. The separation is governed by the oracle's selection rule, not by the class alone: for a legal feasible-median ERM rule a deterministic learner achieves $O(\log T)$ calls and mistakes, while a global-median rule again forces linear total cost. The same linear bound holds when the oracle's answers are chosen adversarially and then frozen into a memoryless oracle. We add partial tradeoff results for fixed query budgets (the middle regime is open) and an interface contrast: with only a weak consistency oracle, returning a realizability bit, both deterministic and randomized learners need $Θ(T)$ calls.

</details>


### [214] [Robust Beam Prediction for V2X Networks with Multi-Modal Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10200)
*Chen Shang,Dinh Thai Hoang,Diep N. Nguyen,Jiadong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对V2X网络中现有波束预测过度依赖射频感知、在复杂车载环境下可靠性不足的问题，提出了一种基于多模态融合的波束预测框架BeamTransFuser，采用层次化Transformer架构逐步融合摄像头、LiDAR、雷达和GPS观测；并引入生成式模块，在模态缺失时从可用观测中重构缺失模态特征，提升实际部署的鲁棒性。在真实多模态V2X数据集上的实验表明，该框架稳定优于代表性基线，生成模块进一步增强了不完备感知条件下的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有感知辅助波束成形方法严重依赖射频感知，在复杂车载环境中可能不可靠；而摄像头、LiDAR等异构传感器的日益普及提供了通过更丰富的环境感知改进波束预测的新机遇，因此亟需一种融合多模态感知的波束预测方案。

Method: 提出BeamTransFuser，一种层次化Transformer架构，通过渐进式融合摄像头、LiDAR、雷达和GPS观测实现鲁棒的波束预测；同时设计生成式模块，用于在实际部署中从现有观测重构缺失的模态特征。

Result: 在真实世界多模态V2X数据集上的实验表明，所提框架稳定优于多个代表性基线；在感知信息不完备（模态缺失）条件下，生成式模块进一步提升了波束预测的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 多模态融合（联合射频与非射频异构传感器）可为复杂V2X环境中的波束预测提供更可靠的基础，层次化Transformer融合架构结合生成式模态补偿机制是提升预测精度与鲁棒性的有效途径。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) provides a promising foundation for beam prediction in future vehicle-to-everything (V2X) networks. However, existing sensing-assisted beamforming methods still rely heavily on radio-frequency sensing, which may become unreliable in complex vehicular environments. Meanwhile, the growing availability of heterogeneous sensors, such as cameras and LiDAR, offers new opportunities to improve beam prediction through richer environmental perception. Motivated by this, this paper proposes a multi-modal beam prediction framework for V2X networks. Specifically, we develop BeamTransFuser, a hierarchical Transformer-based architecture that progressively fuses camera, LiDAR, radar, and GPS observations for robust beam prediction. In addition, to handle possible missing modalities in practical deployment, we introduce a generative module that reconstructs missing modality features from the available observations. Experimental results on a real-world multi-modal V2X dataset show that the proposed framework consistently outperforms representative baselines, while the generative module further improves robustness under incomplete sensing conditions.

</details>


### [215] [Hierarchical and Permutation-Invariant Feature Transformation Learning via Policy-Guided Embedding Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10225)
*Rui Liu,Tao Zhe,Yanyong Huang,Sankha Narayan Guria,Xiao Luo,Wei Fan,Yanjie Fu,Dongjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对表格数据的特征变换框架（PHER），包含两个互补组件：一是置换不变的分层模块，通过自注意力池化机制捕获底层特征、操作与高层抽象之间的层次关系，并将语义等价结构映射为一致嵌入；二是策略引导的多目标强化学习策略，从经验上强的种子出发初始化搜索，联合优化预测精度与变换效率。实验证明其在多个表格基准上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式方法将特征变换知识编码进连续嵌入空间以高效探索候选策略，但存在三个关键局限：忽略特征、操作与高层抽象间的层次关系；对本质上置换不变的变换序列施加顺序敏感嵌入，引入系统性偏差；依赖梯度搜索，不适用于非凸的变换空间。

Method: 框架包含两部分：(1) 置换不变分层模块，通过自注意力池化捕获特征、操作与抽象层次间的交互，并将语义等价结构映射为与下游性能对齐的一致嵌入；(2) 策略引导的多目标强化学习策略，从经验上强种子初始化搜索，联合优化预测精度与变换效率。

Result: 在多样化的表格基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明该框架相较强基线具有有效性与鲁棒性。代码与数据已公开。

Conclusion: 通过结合置换不变的分层嵌入建模与多目标强化学习搜索，所提出的框架有效克服了现有生成式特征变换方法的三大局限，在预测性能与变换效率之间取得平衡，并在多个表格基准上验证了优越性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Feature transformation improves predictive performance on tabular data by constructing informative abstractions from raw features. Recent generative approaches encode transformation knowledge into continuous embedding spaces for efficient exploration of candidate strategies, but face three key limitations: (1) overlooking hierarchical relationships between low-level features, operations, and high-level abstractions; (2) enforcing order-sensitive embeddings on inherently permutation-invariant transformation sequences, thereby introducing systematic bias; and (3) relying on gradient-based search, which is ill-suited to non-convex transformation spaces. We propose a framework with two complementary components. First, a permutation-invariant hierarchical module captures interactions across features, operations, and abstraction levels, with a self-attention pooling mechanism that maps semantically equivalent structures to consistent embeddings aligned with downstream performance. Second, a policy-guided multi-objective reinforcement learning strategy initializes the search from empirically strong seeds and jointly optimizes predictive accuracy and transformation efficiency. Extensive experiments on diverse tabular benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our framework against strong baselines. Our code and data are publicly available at: https://github.com/RayLiu1103/PHER.

</details>


### [216] [Training Trajectories Determine Circuit Removability in Annealable Soft-Prior Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10287)
*Zonglin Yang,Ziming Zhao,Wei Tang,Xunyu Jiang,Yihong Liu,Tailin Chen,Zifu Yu,Jiayu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了小型Transformer在去除软位置先验（soft positional prior）后，其检索电路（retrieval circuits）是否仍能正常工作。作者采用可退火（annealable）的软先验Transformer，其注意力偏置可在训练和评估过程中被学习、淡出或清零。研究发现：在关联回忆任务中，未强制的模型在先验激活时表现良好（0.772±0.020），但在零门控下崩溃（0.095±0.009）；采用平滑淡出至零（fade-to-zero）训练可保持高零门控准确率（0.734±0.028），而强制置零训练、硬切换和事后持续训练均无法恢复该效果。该模式在马尔可夫归纳任务中同样出现。线性回归的上下文学习（ICL）构成边界情况，因为零门控训练可直接学习该任务。机制追踪表明，电路整合（circuit consolidation）发生在门控达到零之后，尽管负责的注意力头在不同随机种子间有所变化。这些结果表明，小型离散检索任务中的电路可移除性（circuit removability）取决于训练轨迹，而非仅取决于最终架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究软位置先验（soft positional priors）对小型Transformer检索电路的影响，特别是当先验被移除后电路是否依然功能正常。这一问题的核心在于检验模型学到的检索能力是否已经固化为不依赖位置先验的电路，还是仅仅依附于先验提示。作者希望厘清电路可移除性（circuit removability）究竟由训练轨迹还是最终架构决定，从而深化对位置先验与注意力机制交互作用的理解。

Method: 作者使用一个可退火的软先验Transformer，其注意力偏置（attention biases）在训练与评估阶段可以被学习、淡出或清零。实验设计了多种训练/评估策略：未强制（unforced）模型（先验保持激活）、平滑淡出至零训练（smooth fade-to-zero）、强制置零训练（forced-zero）、硬切换（hard switching）以及事后持续训练（post hoc continuation）。在多种任务上系统评估，包括关联回忆（associative recall）和马尔可夫归纳（Markov induction），并以线性回归的上下文学习（ICL）作为边界情况。同时通过机制追踪（mechanistic traces）分析电路中负责的注意力头在不同随机种子下的变化。

Result: 核心发现为：未强制的模型在先验激活时表现良好（0.772±0.020），但在零门控下性能崩溃（0.095±0.009）。平滑淡出至零训练能保持高零门控准确率（0.734±0.028），而强制置零训练、硬切换和事后持续训练均无法恢复该效果。该模式在马尔可夫归纳任务中同样成立。线性回归ICL作为边界情况，因为零门控训练可以直接学会该任务。机制追踪显示电路整合发生在门控归零之后，且负责的注意力头在不同种子间存在差异。

Conclusion: 研究结论认为，在小型离散检索任务中，检索电路的可移除性（能否在去除位置先验后继续工作）并非由最终架构单独决定，而是很大程度上依赖于训练轨迹（training trajectory）。只有通过平滑淡出至零的训练方式，才能使模型形成不依赖先验的电路整合；其他训练策略（强制置零、硬切换、事后持续训练）无法实现同样效果。这一发现揭示了训练动态在电路形成与泛化能力中的关键作用。

Abstract: Soft positional priors can help small Transformers learn retrieval circuits, but it is unclear whether the resulting circuits remain functional once the prior is removed. We test this with an annealable soft-prior Transformer whose attention biases can be learned, faded, or zeroed during training and evaluation. On associative recall, unforced models perform well with the prior active ($0.772 \pm 0.020$) but collapse at zero gate ($0.095 \pm 0.009$). Smooth fade-to-zero training preserves high zero-gate accuracy ($0.734 \pm 0.028$), whereas forced-zero training, hard switching, and post hoc continuation fail to recover the same effect. The pattern also appears on Markov induction. Linear regression ICL provides a boundary case because zero-gate training can learn that task directly. Mechanistic traces show that circuit consolidation occurs after the gate reaches zero, even though the responsible heads vary across seeds. These results suggest that circuit removability in small discrete retrieval tasks depends on the training trajectory, not just the final architecture.

</details>


### [217] [A Dominant Diffuse Phase in the Sparse Autoencoder Phase Diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10299)
*Alexis D. Plascencia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过实施MAIS-O43开放问题所规定的受控实验，系统研究稀疏自编码器（SAE）在嵌套真实特征下的恢复/合并行为。在200次独立初始化的拟合及完整165格网格（3300次额外拟合）中，未观察到任何完整的字典恢复或特征合并，所有运行均收敛到可复现的“弥散相”（diffuse phase）：重构近乎完美，但学习到的原子与真实特征相距甚远（最佳余弦中位数仅0.5-0.7，远低于0.95恢复标准），且学习到的编码比真实数据稠密一个数量级。该结果与精确稀疏编码目标全局最优在双特征情形下会合并嵌套特征的已知结论形成对照，表明训练得到的SAE未必达到对应最小值，训练模型的相图可能与目标最小化器的相图存在根本差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE被广泛用于从神经网络激活中恢复可解释特征，但系统性特征共现会导致不同特征被吸收或合并。MAIS-O43开放问题提出一个受控实验，旨在刻画当嵌套分数γ、稀疏惩罚λ和字典大小M变化时，真实合成字典的恢复何时让位于特征合并。本研究旨在通过严格遵循该协议来填补这一空白。

Method: 研究严格实施MAIS-O43规定的实验协议，评估了165个网格单元中10个单元上的200次独立初始化拟合；随后使用标准小批量Adam在全部165单元网格上额外进行3300次拟合，并进行了稳健性检查。通过比较学习原子与真实特征的余弦相似度（恢复标准为0.95）、以及学习编码与真实编码的稠密度来评估恢复与合并行为。

Result: 实验观察到零次完整字典恢复和零次特征合并（与理论预期相反）。所有运行均收敛到可复现的弥散相：重构近乎完美，但学习原子典型地远离真实特征（最佳余弦中位数0.5-0.7），且学习编码比真实值稠密约一个数量级。该行为在稳健性检查及完整165格网格（3300次额外拟合）中持续存在。

Conclusion: 由于精确稀疏编码目标在双特征情形下已知会在全局最优处合并嵌套特征，而训练获得的SAE并未表现出此类合并，作者得出结论：训练模型的收敛解未必对应目标函数最小值，训练模型的相图可能从根本上不同于目标最小化器的相图。这提示SAE的实际训练动力学与理论最优之间存在显著分歧。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are increasingly used to recover interpretable features from neural-network activations, yet systematic feature co-occurrence can cause distinct features to be absorbed or merged. The MAIS-O43 open problem proposes a controlled experiment to characterize when recovery of a true synthetic dictionary gives way to feature merging as the nesting fraction $γ$, sparsity penalty $λ$, and dictionary size $M$ vary. We implement the specified protocol and evaluate 200 independently initialized fits across ten of the 165 grid cells. We observe zero full-dictionary recoveries and zero merges. Instead, every run converges to a reproducible diffuse phase: reconstruction is nearly perfect, but learned atoms typically remain far from the true features (median best cosine 0.5-0.7 against a 0.95 recovery criterion) and learned codes are an order of magnitude denser than the ground truth. This behavior persists under robustness checks and across the full 165-cell grid using standard minibatch Adam (3,300 additional fits). Since the global optimum of the exact sparse-coding objective is known to merge nested features in the two-feature case, these results suggest that trained SAEs need not reach the corresponding minima, and that the phase diagram of trained models may differ fundamentally from that of objective minimizers.

</details>


### [218] [View-Structured Conformal Prediction for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10307)
*Junzheng Chu,Bin Pan,Zhenwei Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 View-Structured 共形预测 (VSCP)，将预标定尺度拆分为来自渲染器的空间形状和可迁移的视图难度因子，用于 3D 高斯泼溅的新视图合成。VSCP 提供有限样本有效性的同时，在同等覆盖率下比常数尺度缩减 22.1% 宽度，匹配十模型集成的效果但只需单模型和更少的栅格化次数，并可迁移到无界 Mip-NeRF 360 场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 可实时渲染新视图，但其不确定性热图无法证明渲染视图满足一定的预测覆盖率。本文将新视图合成视为结构化回归，要求以至少 1-α 的概率使 RGB 预测框覆盖新视图中至少 1-β 比例的像素，从而将预测覆盖率要求显式化为可验证的统计保证。

Method: 将预标定尺度拆分为两部分：来自渲染器的空间形状(spatial shape)和一个可迁移的视图难度因子(view-difficulty factor)，该因子预测形状所需的最小视图乘数。随后在视图上使用留出分位数(View-CP)给出有限样本有效性，即使迁移到新场景也成立。该因子分解使分析是精确的：一致性分数为 oracle 与预测视图难度之比，超额宽度分为测试端与标定端两项。

Result: 在 13 个真实场景中，像素池化标定达到 89.9% 的边缘像素覆盖率，但在 90% 目标下视图事件覆盖率仅 61.4%；而 View-CP 达到 91.7-92.0%。在匹配覆盖率下，VSCP 相对常数尺度缩减宽度 22.1%，并用每场景单模型、四次而非十次栅格化匹配十模型集成的 21.0% 缩减。VSCP 比 3DGS-U 单模型基线提升 4.7 点(p=0.0225)。视图预测器从有界源族迁移到全部九个无界 Mip-NeRF 360 场景，满尺度相对常数尺度在所有九个场景上节省 20.7% 宽度；不同稠化骨干下仍保持 18.3% 节省，在 RTX 4090 上运行 216-280 FPS。

Conclusion: VSCP 通过将尺度分解为空间形状与可迁移的视图难度因子，实现了新视图合成的精确且可迁移的共形预测，在保持有限样本有效性的同时显著缩减预测宽度并提升视图事件覆盖率，且计算开销低于集成方法，展示了强迁移性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) renders novel views in real time, but an uncertainty heatmap does not certify that a rendered view meets a certain prediction coverage. We treat novel-view synthesis as structured regression and ask that, with probability at least $1-α$, RGB prediction boxes cover at least a $1-β$ fraction of pixels in a new view. We propose View-Structured Conformal Prediction (VSCP). It splits the pre-calibration scale into a spatial shape from the renderer and a transferable view-difficulty factor, which predicts the smallest view-wise multiplier that shape needs. A held-out quantile over views (View-CP) then gives finite-sample validity even when transferring to new scenes. The same factorization makes the analysis exact: a conformity score is the ratio of oracle to predicted view difficulty, and excess width separates into a test-side and a calibration-side term. Across 13 real scenes, pixel-pooled calibration reaches 89.9\% marginal pixel coverage but only 61.4\% view-event coverage at a 90\% target, while View-CP reaches 91.7--92.0\%. At matched coverage VSCP cuts width by 22.1\% against a constant scale, and matches a ten-model ensemble's 21.0\% reduction using only one model per scene and four rather than ten rasterization passes per query. VSCP also improves on the closest single-model baseline, the 3DGS-U field, by 4.7 points ($p=0.0225$). The view predictor transfers from bounded source families to all nine unbounded Mip-NeRF~360 scenes. There the full scale beats the constant scale with 20.7\% width saving on all nine scenes. It also keeps an 18.3\% saving under a different densification backbone and runs at 216--280 FPS on an RTX~4090.

</details>


### [219] [One Loop, Two Gains: Can Active Learning win the Lottery for Free?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10311)
*Benedikt Tscheschner,Eduardo Veas,Marc Masana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Improve & Prune (I&P)方法，将迭代幅度剪枝（IMP）无缝集成到基于池的深度主动学习每轮重训练循环中，几乎零额外成本地获得"中奖彩票"（稀疏子网络）。实验表明，在多种采集函数、架构和图像分类数据集（含主动微调场景）下，I&P能够在每轮主动学习迭代中产出稀疏且可部署的模型，在高达95%稀疏度下仍与稠密模型精度匹配，从而解决DAL在大架构和大未标注池上的两类计算瓶颈（逐轮重训练与采集评分）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 彩票假设与深度主动学习都依赖"从零开始迭代重训练"这一计算开销巨大的范式，但两者此前被分开研究。作者观察到基于池的主动学习固有的迭代训练循环恰好提供了迭代幅度剪枝所需的精确计算结构，因此提出将两者整合，在几乎零额外成本下同时获得稀疏中奖彩票与主动学习模型，并探究非平稳数据条件下IMP能否产生中奖彩票。

Method: 提出Improve & Prune (I&P)方法：在主动学习的每个重训练周期内集成幅度剪枝（IMP），利用主动学习已有的迭代重训练循环作为IMP的计算基础，无需额外训练成本。通过多种采集函数、架构家族和图像分类数据集（包括主动微调场景）进行系统实证评估。

Result: I&P方法在每轮主动学习迭代中产出稀疏、可部署的模型，在高达95%的稀疏度下仍能匹配稠密模型的精度，实质上将中奖彩票作为主动学习管线的副产品获得。这些逐轮稀疏模型能缓解DAL在大型架构和大规模未标注池上的per-round模型重训练和采集评分两大计算瓶颈。

Conclusion: 将彩票假设的迭代幅度剪枝与深度主动学习的迭代重训练范式相整合是可行且高效的，I&P能够在几乎零额外成本下获得中奖彩票，同时提升DAL的实用性与可扩展性，为两个此前独立的研究范式建立了有意义的桥梁。

Abstract: The lottery ticket hypothesis posits the existence of winning tickets: sparse subnetworks that, when trained in isolation from their original initialization, match the accuracy of the full dense network. The predominant method for discovering such tickets, iterative magnitude pruning, alternates pruning with full retraining from scratch until convergence over many cycles. Similarly, deep active learning also retrains a model from scratch after each acquisition round as new labels become available. Despite this shared reliance on iterative retraining with a substantial computational overhead, the two paradigms have been studied separately. We observe that the iterative training loop inherent to pool-based active learning already provides the exact computational structure that iterative magnitude pruning exploits, and propose Improve & Prune (I&P), a method that integrates magnitude pruning into each active learning retraining cycle at practically no additional cost. This raises a key empirical question: can iterative magnitude pruning produce winning tickets under the non-stationary data regime of active learning? We investigate this question across multiple acquisition functions, architecture families, and image classification datasets, including an active fine-tuning scenario. Our results demonstrate that I&P yields sparse, deployable models at each active learning iteration. Those match the accuracy of their dense counterparts at sparsities up to 95%, effectively obtaining winning tickets as a byproduct of the active learning pipeline. These per-iteration sparse models can address two computational bottlenecks - per-round model retraining and acquisition scoring over the unlabeled pool - that currently prevent the practical adoption of DAL on large architectures and large unlabeled pools.

</details>


### [220] [A Later Test Set Is Not a New Domain: Pretraining Familiarity Survives a Contamination-Free Hold-Out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10357)
*Mahdi Naser Moghadasi,Faezeh Ghaderi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个时间上严格后于所有模型发布日期的真实数据保持集（七个领域、五个域、十三种预测器），以此消除预训练记忆污染。结果显示预训练模型在5/7个领域获胜，但与经典基线差距有限；且模型优势无法用季节性强度和谱熵等内在属性解释，而是与该领域的语料熟悉度相关（尤其是Wikipedia页面浏览量，正是TimesFM预训练语料的主体）。作者据此提出时间保持集仅能消除窗口记忆、无法消除领域熟悉度的结论，呼吁基准需相对于公开语料声明领域保持，并指出实践者应关注自己的领域是否属于模型训练语料范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型的评估几乎全部依赖早于模型发布的历史公开档案，因此高分无法与"预训练已见过测试集"相区分。作者旨在构建一个严格后于模型发布时间的真实保持集，以纯净地检验模型泛化能力，并探究决定胜负的真正因素。

Method: 构建时间保持协议：选取13种预测器（4种经典方法、3种每个数据集单独训练的模型、6种预训练模型），覆盖7个领域、5个域；所有观测值均晚于最后发布模型的日期，且数据集无需API密钥即可重建。随后用季节性强度、谱熵（基于输入窗口测量）等内在属性，以及语料熟悉度（对比Wikipedia页面浏览量领域）来分析胜败模式；在预训练家族内部采用秩次比较与Mann-Whitney检验控制序列难度。

Result: 预训练模型赢得5/7个领域，在1个领域败给Theta基线，在日度汇率上与其他所有方法一样与季节性朴素预测无法区分。负面结果：季节性强度与谱熵无法解释胜负模式，且季节性强度与优势呈负相关。真正起作用的因素是语料熟悉度——最大的增益（周度Wikipedia页面浏览量较最优经典方法降低28% MASE）正落在TimesFM作者所称的预训练语料主体领域；在预训练家族内部（序列难度被抵消），TimesFM家族在Wikipedia上的平均秩比Chronos低0.53，而在其他领域仅低0.09（1,500 vs 754序列，Mann-Whitney p<1e-5）。

Conclusion: 时间保持集能消除对具体时间窗口的记忆，但无法消除对领域的熟悉效应；因此基准评价必须采用相对于已公开语料声明的领域保持集。对实践者而言，关键问题并非"哪个模型更好"，而是"自己的领域是否属于该模型被训练过的语料范围"。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models are evaluated almost exclusively on public archives that predate them, so a strong score cannot be separated from having seen the test set during pretraining. The obvious remedy is a hold-out that postdates the models. We build one: thirteen forecasters -- four classical, three trained per dataset, six pretrained -- on seven groups drawn from five domains, every observation published after the last model was released, and every dataset rebuildable without an API key. Under this protocol pretrained models win 5 of 7 groups, lose one to a Theta baseline, and on daily exchange rates are indistinguishable from a seasonal naive forecast, along with every other method tested.
  We then ask what separates the wins from the losses, and report a negative result: the two intrinsic properties one would reach for -- seasonal strength and spectral entropy, measured on the input window -- do not account for the pattern, and seasonal strength is if anything negatively associated with the advantage. What does track it is corpus familiarity. Our largest gain (28% lower MASE than the best classical method, on weekly Wikipedia pageviews) falls on Wikipedia pageviews, the domain TimesFM's authors describe as the bulk of its pretraining corpus, at the same granularities and differing only in time window. Within the pretrained family, where every model forecasts identical series so that series difficulty cancels, the TimesFM family outranks the Chronos family by -0.53 ranks on Wikipedia against -0.09 everywhere else (1,500 vs. 754 series, Mann-Whitney p < 1e-5). We conclude that a temporal hold-out removes memorisation of a window but not familiarity with a domain, that benchmarks therefore need domain hold-outs stated relative to disclosed corpora, and that the practitioner's question is less which model is better than whether their domain is one the model was raised on.

</details>


### [221] [OmniMed-FL: A Robust Multimodal Federated Learning Framework for Clinical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10364)
*Ayush Debnath,Ruelia Saha,Sudip Misra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniMed-FL，一项针对多模态联邦学习的受控系统研究，用于五类临床状况分类（正常、肺炎、COVID-19、胸腔积液、心脏肥大）。其使用3,000张公开胸部X光片与3,000条按类别而非按患者匹配的合成临床笔记构成代理语料，在非IID Dirichlet分区（3-20个医院客户端）下，系统对比了八种融合策略、三种初始化、四种缺失文本插补规则及联邦基线。关键发现：FedProx（0.737）在K=5、α=0.1场景下超越FedAvg（0.662）与本地训练（0.297）；标签偏斜对F1的代价（高达0.27）远超客户端数量增加（至多0.10）；多模态融合在两个语料上的表现均优于单模态。作者明确强调这是描述性代理比较，并非诊断性能或部署就绪性的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床诊断常需同时分析医学影像与患者病历，但标准机器学习算法无法联合处理异构数据；同时HIPAA与GDPR合规要求限制了对敏感患者数据的集中式聚合。这导致跨异地网络的视觉与文本上下文安全融合存在关键空白，亟需在不违反隐私法规的前提下实现多模态联邦学习。

Method: 构建OmniMed-FL框架：代理语料由3,000张公开胸部X光片与等量、按类别条件生成的合成临床笔记配对而成（类别匹配而非患者级匹配）。系统评测覆盖八种融合策略、三种模型初始化、四种缺失文本插补规则，并纳入了匹配的联邦基线（FedAvg、FedProx、FedMME风格一次性集成及SCAFFOLD-AdamW），在3至20个医院客户端间采用非IID Dirichlet分区（α=0.1）做受控实验。

Result: 客户端K=5、α=0.1时：本地训练macro-F1为0.297，FedAvg为0.662±0.074，FedProx为0.737±0.085，一次性集成为0.647±0.080，SCAFFOLD-AdamW仅0.070±0.015；FedProx-FedAvg差距0.075落在两随机种子标准差较宽者之内。在4×3网格上，标签偏斜代价高达0.27 F1，而客户端近7倍增长代价至多0.10；双向流量随K线性增至183.5 GiB（K=20）。多模态融合在两语料上均最优：合成语料0.956 vs 文本0.934、图像0.664；放射语料0.906 vs 0.880与0.737，代价为文本单独模型状态的2.3倍。

Conclusion: 由于所有临床笔记均为合成数据且非患者级配对，这些结果构成描述性代理比较，而非诊断性能或部署就绪性的估计。在该范围内，FedProx在极端偏斜下表现稳健，多模态融合一致优于单模态，揭示了标签偏斜比客户端规模对性能的影响更大，且通信成本随客户端数量线性增长。

Abstract: Simultaneous assessment of medical imaging and patient records is often required in clinical diagnosis. However, standard machine learning algorithms cannot analyze these data types together. Meanwhile, compliance with HIPAA and GDPR can constrain centralized aggregation of sensitive patient data. This leaves a crucial void of secure fusion of visual and textual context across distant networks. Thus, we present OmniMed-FL, a controlled systems study of multimodal federated learning for five-class clinical condition classification (Normal, Pneumonia, COVID-19, Pleural Effusion, Cardiomegaly). Our proxy corpus pairs 3,000 public chest radiographs with 3,000 class-conditioned synthetic notes, matched by class, not by patient. The framework benchmarks eight fusion strategies, three initializations, four missing-text imputation rules, and matched federated baselines under non-IID Dirichlet partitioning across 3 to 20 hospital clients. As all notes are synthetic and pairing is not patient-level, these are descriptive proxy comparisons, not estimates of diagnostic performance or deployment readiness. Within those limits with clients ($K=5$) and severe skew ($α=0.1$), local-only training achieves a macro-F1 score of 0.297, FedAvg achieves $0.662\pm0.074$, FedProx $0.737\pm0.085$, a matched FedMME-style one-shot ensemble $0.647\pm0.080$, and our SCAFFOLD-AdamW adaptation $0.070\pm0.015$, the 0.075 FedProx-FedAvg gap falling inside the wider of the two two-seed standard deviations. Over a $4\times3$ grid, label skew costs up to 0.27 F1 whereas a near-sevenfold client increase costs at most 0.10, while bidirectional volume grows linearly to 183.5 GiB at $K=20$. Multimodal fusion leads on both corpora, scoring 0.956 against 0.934 for text and 0.664 for images on the synthetic corpus and 0.906 against 0.880 and 0.737 on the radiograph corpus, for $2.3\times$ the model state of text alone.

</details>


### [222] [Forgetting Only What Matters: Layer-Selective Unlearning toward Robust LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10439)
*Ravi Ranjan,Olivera Kotevska,Agoritsa Polyzou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FOM-UL，通过基于遗忘/保留显著性评分选择Transformer层进行定向更新，在保持保留集效用和量化鲁棒性的同时提高机器遗忘效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可能记忆并重现敏感或有版权的内容，机器遗忘是重训练的替代方案，但现有方法通常使用宽泛或固定的参数更新，导致效用下降，且在后训练量化等部署变化下被遗忘知识可能部分重现。

Method: FOM-UL是一种层级遗忘框架，利用遗忘-保留显著性评分（对遗忘集影响高、对保留集敏感度低的层）来选择Transformer层，仅在这些层进行更新，其余模型保持不变，以减少更新范围并提高遗忘与效用的权衡，同时降低量化对更新的破坏。

Result: 在TOFU、KnowUnDo和MUSE风格评估中，FOM-UL相比GA、NPO、KLD、SURE、ReLearn和LUNAR等基线，能更大程度降低残余记忆，同时保留集效用接近原始模型；在8位和4位量化下保持更强的记忆抑制和效用保持，对抗性提示下遗忘内容恢复更少。

Conclusion: FOM-UL提供了一种高效的定向遗忘策略，兼顾针对性遗忘、效用保持和部署鲁棒性，但作者不声称提供正式的擦除保证。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can memorize and reproduce sensitive, copyrighted, or otherwise undesirable training content, creating privacy, safety, and regulatory concerns. Machine unlearning offers a practical alternative to full retraining, but many existing methods apply broad or fixed parameter updates that can degrade utility and remain brittle under deployment changes such as post-training quantization, where forgotten knowledge may partially re-emerge. We propose Forgetting Only What Matters via Unlearning Layers (FOM-UL), a layer-level unlearning framework that selects transformer layers using a forget-to-retain significance score. This score identifies layers with high influence on the forget set and low sensitivity to the retain set, allowing FOM-UL to concentrate updates where they are most effective while leaving most of the model unchanged. This targeted update strategy improves the forgetting-utility trade-off and provides an empirical path toward quantization-resilient unlearning by reducing the chance that small, diffuse updates are erased by low-bit rounding. Across TOFU, KnowUnDo, and MUSE-style evaluations, FOM-UL reduces residual memorization compared with strong GA, NPO, KLD, SURE, ReLearn, and LUNAR-based baselines while preserving retain-set utility close to the vanilla model. Under 8-bit and 4-bit post-training quantization, FOM-UL maintains stronger memorization suppression and utility preservation than competing methods, and adversarial prompt evaluations show lower recovery of forgotten content. Overall, FOM-UL provides an efficient unlearning strategy that improves targeted forgetting, utility preservation, and deployment robustness without claiming formal guarantees of erasure.

</details>


### [223] [Semigroup-JEPA: Latent Dynamics Consistency for Zero-Shot Physics Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10464)
*Andy Zeyi Liu,Haoran Sun,Lucas Baker,Randall Balestriero,John Sous*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-JEPA（半群JEPA），通过动作条件化将控制物理规律的参数注入时序模型，并联合训练编码器与预测器进行自回归隐空间滚动，从而使JEPA世界模型能够学习物理并生成物理上真实的动力学。实验表明SG-JEPA在二维数据集上将开环预测误差降低至DINO-WM的1/2，在三维机器人数据集上将控制成功率提升至2.5倍。作者通过线性特征模型拆解误差来源，发现大部分增益源于编码器学习了预测器可携带的动力学相关特征，而非预测器本身学到了更好的动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA类世界模型虽能在隐空间学习紧凑的世界表征以支持预测与规划，但其学习物理规律、生成物理真实动力学的潜力此前从未被验证。作者希望评估并改进世界模型在物理规律变化下的分布外泛化能力（如不同重力场下的动力学），从而推动世界模型在物理驱动任务上的应用。

Method: SG-JEPA基于LeWorldModel框架，通过动作条件化将控制物理规律的参数（如重力）提供给时序模型，并采用自回归隐空间滚动（autoregressive latent rollout）联合训练编码器与预测器。实验设计在不同重力场下的动力学任务，这些任务遵循同一物理定律但表现出定性不同的动力学。此外，作者构建了一个线性特征模型，将局部"定律条件化误差"与滚动过程产生的递归放大误差分离，以剖析误差来源。

Result: 在二维数据集上，SG-JEPA相比DINO-WM将开环预测误差降低至原来的1/2；在三维机器人数据集上（配合独立训练的去噪扩散策略），控制成功率提升至2.5倍。线性特征模型分析表明，将多步滚动损失反向传播到表征中，会训练编码器保留预测器能向前携带的特征，而这些正是动力学所依赖的特征，因此大部分增益来自编码器学到了更好的特征，而非预测器学到了更好的动力学。

Conclusion: SG-JEPA成功赋予JEPA世界模型学习物理、生成物理真实动力学的泛化能力，并揭示了在联合训练中编码器学习优质特征（而非预测器自身动力学建模）是性能提升的关键机制。这一发现为显式物理规律的隐空间世界建模提供了可行路径，也为世界模型在机器人和物理仿真中的落地提供了指导。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) world models learn a compact latent representation of the world that supports prediction and planning, but their capability to learn physics and generate physically realistic dynamics remains hitherto untested. In this work, we introduce SemiGroup-JEPA (SG-JEPA), which extends the LeWorldModel framework by supplying the parameter governing the physics to the temporal model via action-conditioning and jointly training an encoder and predictor through an autoregressive latent rollout. To evaluate the model's ability to generalize out of distribution, we design dynamical tasks under different gravitational fields that, despite obeying the same physical law, exhibit qualitatively different dynamics, ranging from floating motion in weak gravitational fields to rapid bouncing in strong ones. In contrast to DINO-WM, SG-JEPA reduces open-loop prediction error by up to 2 times on two-dimensional datasets, and increases control success rate up to 2.5 times for three-dimensional robotic datasets, for which we train independent diffusion policies. To explain this advantage, we develop a linear feature model that separates local law-conditioned error from its recursive amplification under rollout. Guided by this model, we find that back-propagating the multi-step rollout loss into the representation trains the encoder to keep the features that the predictor can carry forward, and that those are the features the dynamics depend on, so most of the gain comes from the encoder learning better features rather than from the predictor learning better dynamics. See project page at https://sg-jepa.github.io.

</details>


### [224] [Nonmaximal sums of maximally monotone operators under Rockafellar's constraint qualification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10487)
*Weifeng Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构造了Rockafellar和猜想（sum conjecture）的反例：两个极大单调算子满足内域条件（interior-domain condition）但其和并非极大单调。作者在c0空间上给出一个反例，在ℓ1空间（配通常范数）上给出另一个反例，并建立了通用构造定理，计算一类图的完整单调极（monotone polar），给出其极大单调性的充要条件，并证明在该条件下正秩一扰动会产生非极大的和。作者在c0上验证了定理假设与极大性判据，从而获得反例；还构造了一个从ℓ1到c0的有界线性满射，并借此获得ℓ1上的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rockafellar和猜想是极大单调算子理论中的著名开放问题：若两个极大单调算子满足内域条件（其定义域交集的内部非空），则它们的和是否仍为极大单调。长期以来该猜想缺乏明确答案，本文旨在通过构造具体反例来否定该猜想，填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者采用构造性方法：首先建立通用构造定理，对一类算子图计算其完整的单调极，推导出极大单调性的充要条件，并证明在此条件成立时正秩一扰动会导致和的非极大性。随后在c0空间上验证定理的假设与极大性判据，得到反例；再构造从ℓ1到c0的有界线性满射，利用其将c0上的反例转移/提升到ℓ1空间，得到第二个反例。

Result: 成功构造了满足内域条件而和并非极大单调的两个极大单调算子：一个在c0上，另一个在ℓ1（通常范数）上，从而给出Rockafellar和猜想的反例。同时建立了可用于判定一类算子极大单调性并预测正秩一扰动效应的一般性构造定理。

Conclusion: 工作表明Rockafellar和猜想即使在内域条件满足时也不成立，即在某些Banach空间中极大单调性质在加法下并不封闭。通用构造定理为该类反例的构造提供了系统方法论，并通过范数空间之间的有界线性满射实现反例在不同空间之间的移植。

Abstract: We construct counterexamples to Rockafellar's sum conjecture in which two maximally monotone operators satisfy the interior-domain condition but their sum is not maximally monotone. We give one counterexample on $c_0$ and another on $\ell^1$ with its usual norm. We establish a general construction theorem that computes the entire monotone polar of a class of graphs, gives a necessary and sufficient condition for their maximal monotonicity, and shows how a positive rank-one perturbation yields a nonmaximal sum under this condition. We verify the theorem's hypotheses and its maximality criterion on $c_0$, thereby obtaining a counterexample to the conjecture. Furthermore, we construct a bounded linear surjection from $\ell^1$ onto $c_0$ and use it to obtain the counterexample on $\ell^1$.

</details>


### [225] [Learning with Covariance Matrices: Principal Component Analysis Meets Learning with Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10490)
*Saurabh Sihag,Andrea Cavallo,Elvin Isufi,Gonzalo Mateos,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文作为专题文章，系统综述了协方差神经网络（VNNs）的理论基础——即作用于协方差矩阵（作为图）的图神经网络（GNNs）。文章通过数学分析揭示了VNNs的若干新理论洞见：与主成分分析（PCA）信息处理的概念等价性、有限样本诱发协方差矩阵扰动下的预测稳定性界、以及跨多尺度数据集的可迁移性刻画。这些理论为在协方差矩阵作为数据结构描述符的领域中以VNN替代传统PCA学习管道提供了原理依据，并展示了其在神经退行性疾病脑年龄差距刻画等实际任务中的概念性见解与应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协方差矩阵在众多领域普遍存在，因此GNNs常被部署在基于成对统计依赖的图上。然而，现有GNNs的理论贡献仅考虑抽象图表示，无法适应协方差矩阵带来的数据驱动细微差别。文章旨在通过数学分析弥补这一理论空白，为VNNs提供理论基础并阐明其相较于PCA学习管道的优势。

Method: 文章采用数学分析与理论推导方法，聚焦VNNs的三大核心理论洞见：（i）建立VNNs与PCA信息处理之间的概念等价性；（ii）推导有限样本诱发协方差矩阵扰动下预测结果的精化稳定性界；（iii）刻画VNNs跨多尺度数据集的可迁移性。此外，通过神经影像数据集上脑年龄差距刻画任务作为具体实例，阐述理论见解如何渗透到实际设计。

Result: 文章获得三项理论成果：VNNs与PCA信息处理的概念等价性、针对协方差矩阵扰动的精化稳定性界、以及VNNs跨多尺度数据集的精化可迁移性表征。这些理论为在协方差矩阵作为数据结构描述符的应用中采用VNNs替代传统PCA学习管道提供了原理依据，并阐明其对脑年龄差距刻画等计算神经科学问题的概念性贡献。

Conclusion: VNNs的理论洞见为在协方差矩阵普遍出现的广泛领域中取代主流PCA学习管道提供了底层原理与合理性论证。这些理论基础不仅渗透到学习方法的原理性设计与应用中，还对神经退行性疾病脑年龄差距刻画等具体任务及其他应用领域产生广泛影响。

Abstract: This feature article provides an overview of the theoretical foundations for coVariance neural networks (VNNs), i.e., graph neural networks (GNNs) operating on covariance matrices as graphs. Covariance matrices are ubiquitous across domains, and hence, the deployment of GNNs often leverages graphs of pairwise statistical dependencies. Existing theoretical contributions on GNNs consider abstract graph representations and cannot accommodate the data-driven nuances associated with covariance matrices. This tutorial brings into focus various novel theoretical insights via mathematical analyses of VNNs that have broad signal processing implications, including: (i) a conceptual equivalence between VNNs and principal component analysis (PCA)-based information processing; (ii) refined stability bounds on predictive outcomes in the presence of finite sample-induced covariance matrix perturbations; and (iii) refined characterization of transferability of VNNs across multiscale datasets. The theoretical insights discussed herein provide the underlying principles and justification towards adopting VNNs over workhorse PCA-based learning pipelines, in applications where covariance matrices are useful descriptors of data structure. We also convey how impact of these foundational advances permeates to \textit{principled} designs and applications of learning methods across broad domains where covariance matrices emerge. Notably, we elucidate the conceptual insights facilitated by VNNs to the specific task of characterizing brain age gap for neurodegenerative conditions using neuroimaging datasets, a timely problem in computational neuroscience. Broader impacts to other application domains are discussed as well.

</details>


### [226] [Quantum Feature Engineering for Credit Default Prediction: When and Why IQP Circuits Help Linear Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10505)
*Menachem Finkelstein,Diana Legziel Levy,Zohar Yakhini,Sarel Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究使用瞬时量子多项式（IQP）电路为信贷违约预测（表格分类）生成特征。在UCI信用卡违约数据集上，将16个IQP特征（8比特）附加到逻辑回归模型后，F1从0.462提升至0.517（+0.055，p<0.0001），显著优于Kernel PCA的0.493，且该优势通过Benjamini-Hochberg校正。但其他分类器（随机森林、SVM、XGBoost、k-NN）均未见提升，提示其机制为线性可表达性而非通用增益。此外，特征选择方式至关重要：基于随机森林重要性的选择达到F1=0.523，而最大不相关编码仅0.496，说明电路放大而非凭空创造信息结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信贷违约预测是表格分类问题，F1的小幅提升可直接转化为金融风险敞口的减少。作者的核心动机是利用IQP电路的恒定深度和指数维希尔伯特空间编码特征相关性，验证其能否在同等特征预算下超越原始经典基线与最强的无监督非线性经典替代方案——Kernel PCA，从而证明量子特征生成在真实金融任务中的实用价值。

Method: 采用UCI信用卡违约数据集，包含每客户23项财务属性。对n比特量子电路选取n个特征，每个编码为旋转角度，并读回2n个期望值作为新特征。n比特IQP电路以恒定深度运行，在2^n维希尔伯特空间中编码特征相关性，而其精确输出统计的经典模拟计算代价随n指数增长。采用五折交叉验证，比较附加IQP特征后的逻辑回归与经典基线及Kernel PCA，并测试随机森林、SVM、XGBoost、k-NN等分类器，同时考察不同输入特征选择策略（随机森林重要性引导与最大不相关）的影响。

Result: 附加16个IQP特征（n=8）到逻辑回归将F1从0.462提升至0.517（+0.055，p<0.0001）。Kernel PCA在相同特征数下仅达0.493，差距在12项检验的Benjamini-Hochberg校正后依然显著（p=0.00007）。其他分类器均无增益，表明效果来源于线性可表达性机制。特征选择影响显著：随机森林重要性选择达到F1=0.523，最大不相关编码降至0.496。

Conclusion: IQP电路在信贷违约预测中能够生成有效特征，在同等特征预算下优于经典非线性替代方案，且仅对线性分类器产生增益，说明其机制是通过指数维特征空间实现线性可表达性增强。同时，电路的增益依赖于输入特征的信息量，它放大而非创造结构，这表明量子特征生成应结合经典特征选择策略才能发挥最佳效果。

Abstract: Credit default prediction is a tabular classification problem in which modest gains in F1 translate directly into reduced financial exposure. We ask whether Instantaneous Quantum Polynomial-time (IQP) circuits can produce features that improve a classifier over both its raw classical baseline and Kernel PCA - the strongest unsupervised classical non-linear alternative - at an equal feature budget. The dataset provides 23 financial attributes per client; for an n-qubit circuit we select n of them, encode each as a rotation angle, and read 2n expectation values back out as new features. The motivation for using a quantum circuit is computational: an n-qubit IQP circuit runs in constant depth and encodes feature correlations in a 2^n-dimensional Hilbert space, whereas classical simulation of its exact output statistics scales exponentially in n. Using the UCI Default of Credit Card Clients dataset and five-fold cross-validation, we find that appending 16 IQP features (n = 8 qubits) to a Logistic Regression model raises F1 from 0.462 to 0.517 (+0.055, p < 0.0001). Kernel PCA, the next-best method, reaches only 0.493 at the same feature count; the gap survives Benjamini-Hochberg correction across 12 tests (p = 0.00007). No other classifier - Random Forest, SVM, XGBoost, or k-NN - benefits, which points to a linear-expressivity mechanism rather than a generic improvement. We also show that how the 8 input features are chosen matters: Random Forest importance-guided selection reaches F1 = 0.523, while encoding maximally uncorrelated features drops it to 0.496, demonstrating that the circuit amplifies informative structure rather than creating it from scratch.

</details>


### [227] [A positive resolution of the gap-entropy conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10529)
