*P. M. Aronow,Nathan Kallus,Patrick Lopatto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了固定置信度最佳臂识别问题中的间隙-熵猜想：对具有单位方差高斯臂、均值在[0,1]内且存在唯一最优臂的情形，任何以至少1-δ概率识别最优臂的算法，其在某个实例上的期望样本数（对所有臂标签排列取平均）与H(log(1/δ)+Ent(I))仅相差绝对常数因子。此外还构造了一个与实例无关的算法，其期望样本数以该量的常数倍加g^{-2}log log(e^e/g)为上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最佳臂识别（BBAI）的最优样本复杂度长期以来缺乏精确刻画。间隙-熵猜想最初是针对伯努利分布提出的，本文目标是在高斯分布这一更经典的设定下证明该猜想，从而完善对该问题信息论下界的理解，并给出近乎匹配的算法。

Method: 作者将间隙-熵猜想的证明归结为：在所有可识别最优臂的算法中，某实例上期望样本数（对臂标签排列取平均）的下界与H(log(1/δ)+Ent(I))成正比。同时构造了一个实例无关的自适应算法，其样本复杂度被该量常数倍加上一个与最小间隙g相关的近似对数项所控制，从而与下界匹配。

Result: 证明了对高斯臂设定下固定置信度最佳臂识别的间隙-熵猜想成立：最优样本数（对标签排列平均意义上）在常数因子内等于H(log(1/δ)+Ent(I))；并给出了一个依赖实例无关的算法，其复杂度达到该量常数倍加g^{-2}log log(e^e/g)。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了一个普适的、在常数因子内紧的样本复杂度刻画，表明间隙-熵公式在高斯模型下是正确的，为最佳臂识别问题的理论提供了确定性的上界与下界匹配证明。

Abstract: We prove the gap-entropy conjecture for fixed-confidence best-arm identification with independent unit-variance Gaussian arms, means in $[0,1]$, and a unique optimal arm. For each suboptimal arm $i$, let $Δ_i=μ_*-μ_i$ be its gap from the optimal mean, and write $H=\sum_{i\ne *}Δ_i^{-2}$. Let $p_r$ be the fraction of $H$ contributed by arms with $2^{-(r+1)}<Δ_i\le2^{-r}$, and let $\mathrm{Ent}(I)=\sum_{r:p_r>0} p_r\log(1/p_r)$. Among all algorithms that identify the optimal arm with probability at least $1-δ$ on every Gaussian instance, the optimal expected number of samples on a given instance, averaged over all permutations of the arm labels, is within absolute constant factors of $H(\log(1/δ)+\mathrm{Ent}(I))$. Moreover, there is an algorithm, independent of the instance, whose expected number of samples is bounded by a constant multiple of this quantity plus $g^{-2}\log\log(e^e/g)$, where $g=\min_{i\ne *}Δ_i$ is the gap to the closest competitor.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [MotionCanvas: Learning Implicit Motion Planning from Composable Kinematic Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10457)
*Zeyu Ling,Di Kang,Qing Shuai,Yuxin Wen,Jing Li,Zhanke Wang,Heng Li,Changqing Zou,Chunchao Guo,Linchao Bao*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: MotionCanvas是一个基于流匹配的动作生成模型，支持在共享“运动画布”上指定多种运动学线索（位置、旋转、时间、根轨迹、身体部位等），实现线索条件化的隐式运动规划，并能同时满足多个兼容条件，生成全局连贯的动作序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业角色动画需要自然运动与精确、灵活的控制，例如指定动作时间、控制手臂摆动幅度和行走路线等。现有方法难以灵活组合多种稀疏或稠密、完整或部分的运动学线索。

Method: 将异构运动学线索统一表示在共享的运动画布上（在身体关节和时间上指定位置和旋转值），使用共享的流匹配模型，以该画布为条件生成动作（可选语言和输入动作）；通过线索填补在训练和采样时保持指定值不变，并采用组合式线索采样器，在训练时变化线索的时间、位置、旋转和组合方式，从而学习对不同线索集的连贯补全。

Result: 在时间、根轨迹、身体部位线索（单独或组合）以及语言引导编辑、顺序生成和动作修复等任务上，MotionCanvas均达到最先进的受控动作质量、混合线索遵循度、顺序生成、指令编辑和动作修复效果，同时保留文本到动作的生成能力。

Conclusion: 通过统一线索表示和组合训练，单一生成器即可联合满足多种兼容的异构运动学线索，生成全局连贯的动作，并自然扩展到顺序生成与动作修复。

Abstract: Professional character animation requires both natural motion and precise, versatile control. For example, it is common for the creators to define the timing of a specified action, to control the motion range of the character's arm swing, and the route the character walks through, like specifying various kinematic motion cues on a ``motion canvas''. This motivates us to propose MotionCanvas, a model that supports \emph{cue-conditioned implicit motion planning} to faithfully and coherently connect all cues, dense or sparse, full or partial, into one full-body motion sequence. Specifically, MotionCanvas represents heterogeneous kinematic cues on a shared motion canvas, where position and rotation values are specified across body joints and time. A shared flow-matching model generates motion conditioned on this canvas, with optional language and input motion; cue imputation keeps the specified canvas values fixed in both training and sampling. To learn coherent completion across different cue sets, we train with a compositional cue sampler that varies when cues are applied, which positions or rotations are specified, and how they are combined.
  Together, these designs enable a single generator to synthesize globally coherent actions that jointly satisfy compatible heterogeneous cues. We test this planning ability with temporal, root, and body-part cues---alone and in combination---and language-guided editing. We naturally extend this evaluation to sequential generation and motion repair, since both require the same ability to organize coherent motion from kinematic cues. Across these evaluations, MotionCanvas establishes state-of-the-art results in controlled-motion quality, mixed-cue adherence, sequential generation, instruction editing, and motion repair while preserving its text-to-motion capability.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [OpenDiscoveryTrace: Process Traces for Evaluating AI Scientist Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09203)
*Aayam Bansal,Keertan Balaji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenDiscoveryTrace数据集，包含558条完整AI科学智能体轨迹，以9字段/步的结构化追踪记录模型的思考、工具调用、观测、错误、修正触发与自信度等过程信息，而非仅评估最终输出。数据集涵盖7个模型执行124项科学任务（药物发现、材料科学、基因组学、文献分析），并定义5个基准任务。初步分析显示过程轨迹能暴露输出级评估无法发现的差异：三个前沿模型成功率接近（84-89%），但Claude Opus 4.6每轨迹错误数是GPT-5.4的30倍，且错误类型显著不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主AI科学家的基准只评估最终输出（代码、假设、论文），丢弃了获得这些输出的推理过程，导致无法审计科学方法论、诊断失败模式，也无法区分系统性推理与运气性猜测。作者旨在通过公开过程级轨迹数据集弥补这一空白。

Method: 构建558条完整AI科学智能体轨迹数据集，采用9字段/步的追踪模式（含思考、工具调用、观测、错误、修正触发、置信度）。覆盖7个模型（3个前沿模型各124条、4个开源模型各30条）执行124项科学任务，横跨药物发现、材料科学、基因组学、文献分析，另有60条实时检索变体轨迹。用LLM评审363条轨迹进行试点分析，并用逻辑回归、随机森林、LSTM和Transformer建立5个基准任务基线。

Result: 试点分析显示过程轨迹暴露了输出级评估不可见的差异：三个前沿模型成功率相近（84-89%），但Claude Opus 4.6每轨迹2.5次错误，远高于GPT-5.4的0.08次（p<0.0001，Cliff's δ=0.613），约30倍；且错误画像定性不同——Claude以66.7%工具误用为主，GPT-5.4以83.6%推理错误为主。数据集、轨迹模式、智能体框架与基准定义以CC BY 4.0公开。

Conclusion: 过程级轨迹评估能揭示输出级评估掩盖的关键行为差异，为AI科学家的方法论审计、失败模式诊断和AI治理提供支持。公开的数据集和基准为过程级评估、科学智能体审计与AI治理研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for autonomous AI scientists evaluate only final outputs---generated code, hypotheses, or papers---yet discard the reasoning process by which those outputs were obtained. This makes it impossible to audit scientific methodology, diagnose failure modes, or distinguish systematic reasoning from fortunate guessing. We present \textbf{OpenDiscoveryTrace}, a public dataset of 558 complete AI scientific agent trajectories that captures how models reason, not just what they produce. Each trajectory records a structured 9-field-per-step trace---including thoughts, tool calls, observations, errors, revision triggers, and self-reported confidence---as models execute 124 scientific tasks spanning drug discovery, materials science, genomics, and scientific literature analysis. The dataset covers seven models: three frontier models (GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.6, and Gemini 3.1 Pro; 124 trajectories each, fully balanced across domains and difficulty levels) and four open-weight models (Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B-v0.3, Phi-3.5-mini, and Qwen2.5-1.5B; 30 each), plus 60 live-retrieval variant trajectories. Pilot analysis on 363 LLM-judged trajectories reveals that process traces expose behavioral differences invisible to output-only evaluation: all three frontier models achieve comparable success rates (84--89%), yet Claude Opus 4.6 produces 30$\times$ more errors than GPT-5.4 (2.5 vs. 0.08 per trajectory, $p < 0.0001$, Cliff's $δ= 0.613$), with qualitatively different error profiles---66.7% tool misuse for Claude versus 83.6% reasoning errors for GPT-5.4. We define five benchmark tasks with baselines from logistic regression, random forests, LSTMs, and Transformer models. The dataset, trace schema, agent harness, and benchmark definitions are publicly available under CC BY 4.0 to support research on process-level evaluation, scientific agent auditing, and AI governance.

</details>


### [230] [Adaptive Entangled Game Modules in Artificial General Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09226)
*Haochen Li,Xinshuai Guo,Jingdong Ouyang,Wei Zhang,Leilei Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个概率波框架（广义行为智能GBI非局域概率波方程）来建模适应性智能体的集体行为。通过对中国A股日内数据的实证分析，发现自适应纠缠博弈模式解释了89%的决策模式，2-12%的行为表现出对日内新闻/环境的适应，而纯独立模式少于5%。这些结果支持LCA假说（脑中非局域纠缠神经纤维），并论证了将概率波纠缠脑模拟与ANN结合以构建更紧凑、高效、稳健的AGI（HPU）的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望突破新古典金融学基于独立理性主体的假设以及传统ANN型AI依赖海量不透明参数的局限。通过引入概率波框架，既可以统一刻画多种人类智能行为，又能间接检验LCA关于脑内非局域纠缠神经纤维的假说，并最终为AGI/HPU架构提供脑启发机制。

Method: 方法包含三层：1) 建立GBI非局域概率波方程以导出可检验的本征模；2) 利用该框架解析地刻画多种智能行为；3) 通过中国股票日内数据进行实证，识别自适应纠缠博弈、环境适应（双平衡态与参考点突变）与纯独立模式三类决策模式的比例。

Result: 实证显示自适应纠缠博弈模式解释了82-94%（总体89%）的决策模式；2-12%的行为表现出对新闻/事件/环境的适应（具双平衡态与参考点突变）；而纯独立模式占比<5%。

Conclusion: 观测到的交易行为反映了底层脑机制与内部智能决策，经验上支持LCA假说；作者主张将ANN与概率波纠缠脑模拟相融合以丰富AGI基础模型，发展脑启发的HPU，从而构建更紧凑、高效、稳健的AGI，尤其适用于具身智能与机器人。

Abstract: We introduce a probability-wave framework for modeling the collective behavior of interacting adaptive agents, deriving testable eigenmodes through a generalized behavioral intelligence (GBI) nonlocal probability-wave equation. This framework captures a broad range of human intelligence behaviors with analytical mechanisms and offers an indirect method to examine the Liu-Chen-Ao (LCA) hypothesis of nonlocal entangled nerve fibers in the brain through collective trader behaviors. Our empirical analysis of Chinese intraday stock market data demonstrates that adaptive entangled game modes explain 82-94% (89% overall) of observed decision patterns, a sharp contrast to the predictions of neoclassical finance based on independent rational agents. Moreover, 2-12% of behaviors show adaption to intraday news, events, and environments, characterized by dual equilibrium states and abrupt reference point shifts, while purely independent modes occur in less than 5% of cases. These findings empirically support the LCA hypothesis, as observable trading behaviors reflect underlying brain mechanisms and internal intelligence decision-making in behavioral psychology. Our results highlight the necessity of incorporating adaptive entangled game modules into artificial general intelligence (AGI) architectures, addressing the limitations of conventional artificial neural network (ANN)-based AI, which relies on trillions of opaque parameters. By integrating ANN-based AI with probability-wave-based entangled-brain simulations, machine learning can enrich AGI foundation models (FMs) and facilitate the development of human-like processing units (HPUs) that leverage brain-inspired mechanisms. Such HPUs may ultimately create more compact, efficient, and robust AGI systems, particularly for embodied intelligence and robotics.

</details>


### [231] [Subagents vs Agent Skills: Executing Reusable Knowledge for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09233)
*Wasu Top Piriyakulkij,Rachel Lawrence,Alicia Curth,Sushrut Karmalkar,Niranjani Prasad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型智能体如何有效利用可复用知识库解决长时程任务，对比了两种技能包调用方式：将技能指令加载进主上下文（agent-skill执行），与以子智能体方式调用技能包（在独立的新上下文中执行）。结果表明，当技能包具备清晰的输入-输出契约且指令编码了满足契约的程序性知识时，子智能体执行优于主上下文加载，但代价是增加了智能体间协调的额外通信开销。结论是技能包的可复用性收益不仅取决于内容，也取决于其组织与调用方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务中，将越来越多的技能指令加载进上下文会导致推理质量下降，因此需要探索替代方案来更有效地利用可复用知识库。

Method: 对比两种技能包调用方式：agent-skill执行（将技能指令加载进主上下文）与subagent执行（为每个子任务开辟全新上下文窗口来执行技能包）；分析子智能体方式在技能包具备清晰输入-输出契约与程序性知识时的表现，以及相应的通信开销代价。

Result: 子智能体执行在技能包具备清晰输入-输出契约且指令编码满足契约的程序性知识时，优于agent-skill执行；但引入了额外令牌通信开销，用于主智能体与子智能体间的协调。

Conclusion: 可复用知识的收益不仅取决于其内容，还取决于其组织与调用方式；应该根据任务性质和技能包契约的清晰程度来选择加载或子智能体的执行策略。

Abstract: How can language model agents effectively leverage libraries of reusable knowledge to solve long-horizon tasks? Recent work has increasingly focused on agent skills: reusable capabilities represented as skill packages, i.e., multi-file bundles containing instructions, scripts, and other resources that help agents perform specific tasks. Agent skills are typically executed by loading their skill instructions into an agent's context and relying on the agent to follow them. As task horizons grow, however, this approach becomes increasingly brittle, because reasoning quality degrades as more information accumulates in the context window. We investigate an alternative approach in which skill packages are instead invoked as subagents. Rather than loading skill instructions into the main context, subagent execution spawns fresh context windows dedicated to solving individual subtasks. We show that subagent execution outperforms agent-skill execution when skill packages expose clear input-output contracts and their instructions encode the procedural knowledge needed to fulfill those contracts. The tradeoff is additional communication overhead, as extra tokens are required to coordinate between the main agent and its subagents. Our results show that the benefit of reusable knowledge depends not only on its content, but also on how it is organized and invoked.

</details>


### [232] [Gradland: On Phenomenal Experience, Differentiated Across Many Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09306)
*David Balduzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在一个假想的“梯度之地”(Gradland)——由神经网络构成、物理规律已知且函数(大部分)可微的理想化世界——中，检验“一阶物理交互结构（梯度或雅可比矩阵）刻画现象体验结构”这一假说。论文引入有效秩与基于基尔霍夫复杂度的“凝聚力”两个雅可比结构度量，并通过一系列工作示例证明该假说可以解释七类现象：体验时长（可延续数百毫秒）、体验的鲜明与模糊之别、质感体验、新生儿的“轰轰然杂乱困惑”、头脑中清晰区分与混淆的想法之别、学习的主观感受，以及丰富密集体验的功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在从物理交互的一阶结构（梯度/雅可比矩阵）出发，为意识体验（现象学）提供一个可计算、可量化的形式化解释框架，试图弥合物理结构（第三视角）与主观体验（第一视角）之间的解释鸿沟，并统一解释多种看似异质的经验现象。

Method: 论文构建了一个理想化世界Gradland，其中物理规律已知、所涉函数（多为）可微。在此基础上定义两个雅可比结构度量：有效秩（effective rank）和基于基尔霍夫复杂度的凝聚力（cohesion），并通过一系列工作示例（worked examples）将这些度量应用于各类经验现象的计算分析。

Result: 所提出的雅可比结构度量成功地解释并统一了七类现象：1) 体验可在数百毫秒内持续延展；2) 鲜明体验与朦胧体验之间的差异；3) 质感（纹理）体验的形成；4) 新生儿可能经历的“轰轰然杂乱困惑”；5) 头脑中清晰持有的观念与混淆混淆的观念的差别；6) 学习的主观体验（“学习像什么”）；7) 丰富、密集体验所具有的功能。

Conclusion: 论文得出的结论是：物理交互的一阶结构（梯度/雅可比矩阵）确实足以刻画现象体验的结构，说明体验的结构特征可以追溯到神经系统与环境交互的微分几何特性，为意识体验的计算理解提供了一个统一的理论支点。

Abstract: This paper investigates the hypothesis that the first-order structure of physical interactions, i.e. gradients or Jacobians, characterizes the structure of phenomenal experience. It does so in an idealized world inhabited by neural networks, Gradland, where the physics are known and the functions are (mostly) differentiable. The paper introduces two measures of Jacobian structure: effective rank and cohesion, based on Kirchhoff complexity. Applying the measures to a series of worked examples shows the hypothesis accounts for: (1) the duration of experience, that it can prolong over hundreds of milliseconds; (2) the difference between what is experienced vividly and obscurely; (3) the experience of texture; (4) the blooming buzzing confusion presumably experienced by newborns; (5) the difference between ideas that are held distinctly in mind and ideas that are confused; (6) what learning is like; and finally (7) the paper explains the function of rich, dense experience.

</details>


### [233] [An Autonomous GeoAI Agent for Arctic Eco-Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09374)
*Samira Alkaee Taleghan,Younghyun Koo,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向北极生态导航的人机协同多智能体GeoAI系统，将运营、物理、生态和社区相关标准统一整合到路由框架中。系统集成多个专业智能体，负责地理空间数据采集与准备、多目标路线生成和海天线（skyline）决策支持，并将生态影响（如Essential Fish Habitat和海豹关键栖息地暴露）及社区负担纳入考量，同时将价值判断保留在人类控制之下，以支持更安全、透明和社会负责任的北极导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 北极海上导航因海冰条件变化而日益重要，但现有路由方法仅优先考虑航行时间、燃料使用和航行风险，往往忽视生态和社区影响。北极路线规划本质上是多准则问题——改善船舶安全或效率的路线可能增加对海冰、敏感生态系统或邻近社区的暴露，因此需要统一的框架来平衡这些相互冲突的目标。

Method: 构建一个人机协同（human-in-the-loop）的多智能体GeoAI系统，整合运营、物理、生态和社区相关四类准则于统一路由框架中。系统包含多个专业智能体协同工作：地理空间数据获取与准备、多目标路线生成、以及基于skyline（海天线/天际线）的决策支持。生态准则显式考虑敏感区域暴露，包括Essential Fish Habitat和海洋哺乳动物（海豹）关键栖息地。

Result: 该框架通过将生态系统影响和潜在社区负担纳入路线规划，同时将具有后果性的价值判断保留在人类控制之下，支持更安全、更透明和社会负责任的北极导航。项目页面和代码已公开提供。

Conclusion: 该多智能体GeoAI系统提供了一种兼顾运营效率、生态保护和社区影响的北极导航路线规划方案，通过人机协同确保关键价值决策由人类掌控，从而在北极日益可通航但风险上升的背景下实现更负责任的海上导航。

Abstract: Arctic maritime navigation is becoming increasingly important as changing sea-ice conditions expand seasonal accessibility while simultaneously introducing substantial operational, environmental, and community risks. Arctic route planning is inherently a multi-criteria problem: routes that improve vessel safety or efficiency may increase exposure to sea ice, sensitive ecosystems, or nearby communities. Existing routing methods prioritize travel time, fuel use, and navigational risk, often overlooking ecological and community impacts. We introduce a human-in-the-loop, multi-agent GeoAI system for Arctic eco-navigation that integrates operational, physical, ecological, and community-related criteria within a unified routing framework. Multiple specialized agents coordinate geospatial data acquisition and preparation, multi-objective route generation, and skyline-based decision support. The ecological criteria explicitly account for exposure to sensitive areas, including Essential Fish Habitat and seal critical habitat. By considering these ecosystem impacts and potential community burdens while keeping consequential value judgments under human control, the framework supports safer, more transparent, and socially responsible Arctic navigation. Project page and code are publicly available. https://samiraat.github.io/Arctic-Eco-Navigation-Agent/, https://github.com/samiraat/Arctic-Eco-Navigation-Agent

</details>


### [234] [The Menu Is an Execution Prior: State-Path Tool Menus for Online Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09395)
*Bo Yan,Weikai Lin,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出状态路径工具菜单（State-Path Tool Menu），将工具菜单视为从请求状态到目标结果的执行先验。相比按相关性排序的传统方法，该框架通过编码器学习工具间输入输出依赖和递归顺序，检索器覆盖可执行工具、缺失输入生产者和最终动作，重排序器确保生产者位于消费者之前。在ToolBench上，在线成功率从0.737提升至0.898，且仅用32个工具即覆盖官方128个工具的更完整链路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实用智能体面临包含数千接口的工具库，而多步任务需要最终动作及其输入生产工具以可用顺序排列。现有构造器按请求相关性对工具排序，可能暴露最终动作却遗漏或延迟看似不相关的生产者工具，导致链式任务失败。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 编码器——学习状态路径特征，表示当前状态可运行的工具、输出如何满足后续输入及训练路径中递归出现的顺序；(2) 检索器——覆盖可执行的入口工具、缺失输入的生产者及最终动作；(3) 重排序器——将生产者置于消费者之前。菜单作为这些路径上的执行先验。

Result: 在ToolBench上，在线成功率从0.737提升至0.898，优于检索、重排序、生成和路由基线。状态路径菜单用32个工具覆盖了比官方128个工具更完整的链路，且性能提升在不同模型容量的执行器家族间保持稳定。

Conclusion: 状态路径视角为工具菜单构造提供了优于相关性排序的范式，无需改变智能体本体即可显著提升多步工具调用的成功率，体现了显式建模工具间依赖顺序的价值。

Abstract: Language models act through tools, yet practical agents face libraries containing thousands of interfaces. We introduce the tool menu as the short, ordered subset of available tools shown to an agent before execution. The agent can call only tools in this menu. Multi-step tasks require the final action and the prerequisite tools that create its inputs in a usable order. Current constructors rank tools by request relevance, which can surface the final action while omitting or delaying less obvious producers. We introduce the state path, a pre-execution route from the observable request state to the desired outcome, and propose State-Path Tool Menu to learn it. Our framework treats the menu as an execution prior over these routes. Its encoder represents which tools can run from the current state, how their outputs satisfy later inputs, and which orders recur in training paths. A retriever covers an executable entry, the missing-input producers, and the final action. A reranker then places producers before consumers. On ToolBench, our menu raises online success from 0.737 to 0.898 and outperforms retrieval, reranking, generation, and routing baselines without changing the agent. The State-Path menu also covers more complete chains with 32 tools than the official list covers with 128, and its success gain persists across executor families with different model capacities. Our code is at https://github.com/Met2348/State-Path.

</details>


### [235] [Decision-Focused Active Learning for Scale-Aware Critical-Materials Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09413)
*Niranjan Srinivas,Debajyoti Ray,Elias Nakouzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于太平洋西北国家实验室CICERO自主选择性沉淀工作流的记录，用条件回顾性基准评估主动学习策略：在回收钕铁硼磁体（NdFeB）中，主动学习以远少于非自适应空间填充的实验次数（16至24口井 vs 48口）达到记录的最大富集度，并进一步提出以"下游贝叶斯风险期望减少"为准则的批量选择方法，同时规划了预先注册的前瞻性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将实验室结果放大到规模化回收工艺时，需同时考虑产品要求、过程成本与尺度效应；传统实验设计效率低。作者希望通过主动学习驱动自主选择性沉淀，以更少的实验高效筛选最优工艺条件，并建立连接实验结果与下游决策风险的选批准则。

Method: 采用条件回顾性基准：用拟合模型与回收NdFeB磁体记录进行回溯评估，比较主动学习与非自适应空间填充；定义富集度（选定的稀土-铁比相对进料的比值）为指标；考察两阶段重构、条件分析（SmCo磁体的纯度与标称产率权衡、NdFeB第一轮路径）以及产出水排序的相与稀释假设敏感性；提出按候选批次对下游贝叶斯风险（当前信念下可用过程决策的最小期望损失）的期望减少来选择批量，并比较混合过滤策略与已实施的联合搜索，最后设计预先注册的前瞻性检验。

Result: 主动学习策略在16至24口井内达到记录的最大富集度，而非自适应空间填充需48口井；两阶段重构使两种自适应替代方案在16口井处持平；SmCo条件分析显示第二轮存在纯度与标称产率权衡、NdFeB第一轮路径在富集度上有差异；产出水排序依赖相与稀释假设。探索性模拟中，混合过滤候选策略的估计损失低于已实施的联合搜索，但两阶段策略的差异在估计不确定性范围内不显著。

Conclusion: 主动学习可显著减少达到最优记录结果所需的实验数，为规模化工艺放大的高效决策提供支持；建议采用以"下游贝叶斯风险期望减少"为准则的批量选择。为保证可靠推广，需在共享损失与记录标准下开展预先注册的前瞻性检验，明确测量与记录、定义过程决策与相关输出、使用可信经济输入，并在目标尺度上进行验证。

Abstract: Choosing a recovery process for scale-up requires connecting laboratory results with product requirements, process costs, and scale effects. We analyze records from Pacific Northwest National Laboratory's Computer Intelligence for Critical Element Recovery and Optimization (CICERO) workflow for autonomous selective precipitation. Active learning uses prior results to choose experiments. In a conditional retrospective benchmark with fitted models and recycled neodymium-iron-boron (NdFeB) magnet records, active learning finds the best recorded result with fewer experiments than nonadaptive space filling. Enrichment is the selected rare-earth-to-iron ratio relative to that in the feed. Adaptive policies reach the recorded enrichment maximum by 16 to 24 wells (individual experiments), versus 48. Our two-stage reconstruction ties two adaptive alternatives at 16 wells. Conditional analyses of recycled samarium-cobalt (SmCo) magnets show a Round 2 tradeoff between purity and nominal yield, the recovery fraction calculated from an assumed starting amount - NdFeB Round 1 routes differ in enrichment. Rankings for produced water from oil and gas extraction depend on phase and dilution assumptions requiring confirmation.
  We propose choosing batches by their expected reduction in downstream Bayes risk: the minimum expected loss among available process decisions under current beliefs. In exploratory simulations, a hybrid that filters candidates has lower estimated loss than the implemented joint search across routes and conditions. Differences involving the synthetic two-stage policy are small relative to estimation uncertainty. We outline a pre-registered prospective test under a shared loss and logging standard, requiring clarified measurements and records, a defined process decision and relevant outputs, credible economic inputs, and validation at the intended scale.

</details>


### [236] [Valerant: An Automatic Navigable Game Map Generator via Action-Conditioned World Model Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09418)
*Yiran Qiao,Feng Wang,Jing Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Valerant框架，将预训练的动作条件世界模型转化为可探索并构建3D游戏地图的世界动作模型，无需训练，通过结合预测性视觉滚动、SLAM空间重建和探索驱动动作选择，将单张图像逐步变为持久3D地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏领域的世界动作模型（WAM）主要在2D视觉观测空间操作，缺乏持久3D几何；而自动驾驶和机器人有外部物理环境提供3D世界，游戏没有外部基板，虚拟世界本身必须被实例化。3D游戏需要可导航空间和显式几何，动作条件视频滚动只能提供视觉观测而无法提供空间表示。

Method: Valerant采用训练自由框架，将预训练的动作条件世界模型与SLAM空间重建结合，并通过探索驱动的动作选择来指导预测性视觉滚动，逐步将单张图像扩展为持久、可导航的3D游戏地图。

Result: 该框架成功实现从单张图像出发构建3D游戏地图，将WAM交互扩展到2D视觉仿真之外，为3D游戏地图创作提供了减少手动工作量的新途径。

Conclusion: Valerant有效填补了通用WAM在3D游戏领域的空白，展示了结合预测世界模型与SLAM重建的可行性，为自动构建3D游戏世界提供了新的范式。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) couple predictive world modeling with action generation, allowing anticipated future states to guide agent behavior. Although WAMs are rapidly advancing embodied AI, general-purpose counterparts remain largely unexplored in games. Existing game-oriented approaches often combine action-conditioned world models with external policies and reward functions to realize WAM-like decision-making, yet they operate mainly in 2D visual observation space and do not instantiate persistent 3D geometry. Extending this paradigm to 3D games introduces a distinct challenge. In autonomous driving and robotics, the physical environment exists independently of the model, providing a persistent 3D world in which selected actions can be executed. Games have no such external substrate; the virtual world itself must be instantiated. Most playable games require a persistent and navigable space, while 3D games additionally require explicit geometry that supports movement and interaction. Action-conditioned video rollouts provide visual observations but not this spatial representation. We present \textsc{Valerant}, a training-free framework that transforms a pretrained action-conditioned world model into a WAM for exploring and constructing 3D game maps. By coupling predictive visual rollouts with SLAM-based spatial reconstruction and exploration-driven action selection, \textsc{Valerant} progressively transforms a single image into a persistent 3D game map. This framework extends WAM-based interaction beyond 2D visual simulation and offers a new approach to reducing manual effort in 3D game-map creation.

</details>


### [237] [XAI-Arena: Can LLMs Assess the Quality of XAI Explanations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09428)
*Yanfei Hu Fleischhauer,Alona Zharova,Nadja Klein,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出XAI-Arena框架，利用LLM作为“法官”来对可解释人工智能（XAI）解释质量进行可扩展、可复现、多维度和利益相关者敏感的评估。通过人类验证发现LLM评分与人类评分存在强正相关（Spearman rho=.693, p<.001），证明LLM能够捕捉XAI解释质量的系统性差异，为XAI解释的比较评估提供了可扩展且可复现的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI解释质量评估方法依赖主观人类判断，导致可复现性差、难以规模化、研究间缺乏可比性。作者旨在解决这一评估困境，探索是否能用LLM作为可复现且可扩展的评估器来对XAI解释进行质量比较评估。

Method: 提出XAI-Arena框架，这是一种LLM-as-a-judge（LLM作为评委）方法。该框架对XAI解释进行多维评估，涵盖八个维度：感知简单性（perceived simplicity）、清晰度（clarity）、任务充分性（task adequacy）、信任校准（trust calibration）、可操作性（actionability）、透明度（transparency）、忠实度（faithfulness）和整体可解释性（overall interpretability）。作者在多种数据集、机器学习模型和利益相关者角色（stakeholder personas）上对XAI解释方法进行基准测试，并通过人类验证评估LLM评分与人类评分的一致性。

Result: 人类验证显示LLM生成评分与人类评分之间存在强正相关（Spearman's rho=.693, p<.001）。结果表明LLM能够捕捉XAI解释质量的系统性差异，为XAI解释的比较评估提供了可扩展且可复现的框架。

Conclusion: LLM-based评估能够有效替代或补充人类主观判断，实现XAI解释质量的可规模化和可复现比较评估，克服了传统评估方法在可复现性、可扩展性和可比性方面的局限。

Abstract: Evaluating the quality of explanations produced by explainable AI (XAI) methods remains challenging because existing approaches often rely on subjective human judgment, limiting reproducibility, scalability, and comparability between studies. We examine whether LLMs can serve as a reproducible and scalable mechanism to make comparative assessments of the quality of XAI explanations. We introduce XAI-Arena, an LLM-as-a-judge framework for scalable, reproducible, multidimensional, and stakeholder-sensitive evaluation of XAI explanation quality. XAI-Arena then allows us to compare XAI explanations along various dimensions, namely, perceived simplicity, clarity, task adequacy, trust calibration, actionability, transparency, faithfulness, and overall interpretability. We then benchmark XAI explanation methods across various datasets, machine learning models, and stakeholder personas. Human validation shows a strong positive association between LLM-generated and human ratings (Spearman's rho=.693, p<.001). Together, LLM-based evaluations can capture systematic differences in XAI explanation quality and provide a scalable and reproducible framework for comparative assessment of XAI explanations.

</details>


### [238] [Do Agents Know When They Succeed? Calibrating Agent Confidence from Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09448)
*Priyanka Mary Mammen,Emil Joswin,Srujananjali Medicherla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多轮智能体（agentic）系统中衡量任务成功置信度的问题，提出两种基于模型内部表示的方法——Latent Trajectory Dynamics（LTD，通过残差流表示在交互轨迹上的变化来总结动态）和Action Representation Probe（ARP，从动作决策时形成的表示预测成功）。在Bash、SQL、Python三个交互基准和Qwen14B/Qwen7B/DeepSeek6.7B三个模型族上，这两种方法一致优于表面生成层面和基于序列的校准基线，且是零开销的可靠性监控器——无需修改提示词或多采样rollout。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统正在被快速应用于安全关键场景，因此度量智能体动作的置信度至关重要。与传统机器学习系统不同，智能体工作流具有复杂的失效模式，涉及规划、工具调用和动态环境交互，这使得可靠性度量更具挑战性。本文动机在于探究模型内部表示是否能比表面层输出提供更强的任务最终成功信号，从而为智能体系统提供更可靠的置信度估计。

Method: 本文提出两个互补的方法：(1) Latent Trajectory Dynamics (LTD)——通过汇总交互轨迹上残差流表示的变化来捕捉轨迹层面的动态特征；(2) Action Representation Probe (ARP)——从模型做出动作决策时形成的表示中预测任务成功。两种方法均基于模型内部表示而非表面层生成文本或序列，实现零开销的可靠性监控。

Result: 在三个交互基准（Bash、SQL、Python）和三个模型族（Qwen14B、Qwen7B、DeepSeek6.7B）上，所提出的LTD和ARP方法在预测任务成功方面一致性地超越了表面层生成基线以及基于序列的校准基线，证明了内部表示能提供更强的最终成功信号。

Conclusion: 模型内部表示在多轮智能体设置中可提供比表面输出更可靠的成功信号。LTD和ARP构成了零开销的可靠性监控方案，无需提示修改或多次采样rollout，适合安全关键应用中智能体置信度的实时度量。

Abstract: As agentic systems getting adopted rapidly in safety critical applications, it is vital to measure the confidence associated with the agentic actions. In comparison to the traditional machine learning systems, agentic workflows have complex failure modes with planning, tool invocation and dynamic environment interactions. In this paper, we investigate whether model's internal representations provide stronger signals of eventual task success in multi-turn agentic setups. We introduce two complementary methods: Latent Trajectory Dynamics (LTD), which summarizes changes in residual-stream representations across an an interaction trajectory, and the Action Representation Probe (ARP), which predicts success from representations formed at action decisions. Across three interactive benchmarks (Bash, SQL, Python) and three model families (Qwen14B, Qwen7B, DeepSeek6.7B), our methods consistently outperform surface level generation and sequence-based calibration baselines providing a zero-overhead reliability monitor that requires neither prompt alterations nor multi-sample rollouts.

</details>


### [239] [ContractEval: Query-Conditioned Execution Matching for Procedural Instruction Conformance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09458)
*Praphul Singh,Shanu Kumar,Akshat Agarwal,Ganesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CONTRACTEVAL，一个将程序性指令表示为“查询活跃义务”并与响应或轨迹证据匹配的诊断框架，能把遗漏检查、错误分支、顺序错误、多余动作、不变量违反和输出契约违规转化为不同的合规失败类型，从而实现过程合规的可审计性，而非仅依赖最终答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体从回答问题转向执行程序时，其失败往往表现为“无理但看似正常”的结果——最终响应看起来可接受，但系统跳过了使答案合理的检查、分支、依赖或不变量。仅看输出的评估只能看到答案，仅看轨迹的裁判能看到活动，但两者都无法识别哪些义务对该查询是活跃的、应当被满足的。

Method: CONTRACTEVAL 将程序性指令表示为查询活跃义务（query-active obligations），并将其与响应或轨迹证据进行匹配，从而将遗漏、错误分支、顺序错误、多余动作、不变量违反和输出契约违规分类为不同的合规失败。采用两种证据获取方式：基于标注的金标准期望图与观测图，以及基于 LLM 的义务提取。

Result: 在受控的程序契约审计基准上，仅看输出和仅看轨迹的 LLM 裁判遗漏了许多注入的结构性失败；而在金标准期望图与观测图下，CONTRACTEVAL 检测并定位了所有失败。基于 LLM 的义务提取保留了大部分信号，但对校准敏感。

Conclusion: CONTRACTEVAL 并非合规保证机制，其价值在于将程序性合规从隐含在最终答案质量中转变为可审计的、可定位的诊断过程，为过程性智能体评估提供了新的诊断范式。

Abstract: As LLM agents move from answering questions to carrying out procedures, failures can be unwarranted rather than visibly wrong: the final response looks acceptable even though the system skipped the check, branch, dependency, or invariant that made the answer justified. Output-only evaluation sees the answer, and trace-aware judging sees activity, but neither identifies which obligations were active for the query. We introduce CONTRACTEVAL, a diagnostic framework for making those active obligations explicit. It represents procedural instructions as query-active obligations and matches them against response or trace evidence, turning omissions, wrong branches, ordering errors, extra actions, invariant breaches, and output-contract violations into distinct conformance failures. On a controlled suite of audited procedural contracts, output-only and trace-aware LLM judges miss many injected structural failures; under gold expected and observed graphs, ContractEval detects and localizes all of them. LLM-backed extraction preserves much of this signal but remains calibration-sensitive. ContractEval is therefore not a compliance guarantee; it makes procedural conformance auditable rather than implicit in final-answer quality.

</details>


### [240] [Multi-Agent Agentic Graph Learning via Structural Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09565)
*Liang Qu,Jianxin Li,Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MAAGL（多智能体智能体图学习）框架，将图划分为社区并为每个社区分配独立智能体，分别表示结构证据（置换不变、大小固定的动态结构签名）与语义证据（按相关性过滤的 top-k 节点），基于历史轨迹中的相似签名估计置信度并触发辩论式协作，在四个基准数据集上超越现有最先进的 AGL 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AGL 方法（单智能体或多角色智能体）在整个图上共享推理策略，对结构/语义模式异构的图而言是次优的。直接将多智能体协作范式应用于图面临两个挑战：一是将图结构口头化为自然语言描述会破坏图的置换不变性；二是采样邻域增大会导致上下文迅速膨胀。

Method: MAAGL 将图按社区划分，为每个社区分配独立智能体以进行区域专属的专门化；结构证据由动态更新、置换不变且大小固定的结构签名总结，语义证据过滤为按相关性排序的 top-k 节点；智能体依据具有相似签名历史轨迹估计置信度，并在必要时触发辩论式协作。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，MAAGL 优于最先进的 AGL 方法。

Conclusion: 通过社区划分加智能体专门化、结构/语义证据分离表示（置换不变的结构签名与 top-k 语义过滤）以及基于置信度的辩论式协作，MAAGL 有效解决了异构图上共享策略次优、口头化破坏置换不变性及上下文膨胀等问题，取得 SOTA 性能。

Abstract: Agentic graph learning (AGL) has recently achieved promising results on graph reasoning tasks, where an agent powered by a large language model (LLM) sequentially samples the graph as evidence to support its final prediction. Existing methods either employ a single agent or orchestrate multiple role-based agents to reason and learn over the entire graph, but both essentially rely on a shared reasoning policy across different graph regions, which can be suboptimal for graphs with heterogeneous structural and semantic patterns. Inspired by the progress of multi-agent collaboration on complex reasoning tasks, a natural remedy is to let multiple agents own different memory and collaborate; however, applying this paradigm to graphs directly faces two challenges. First, existing AGL methods typically verbalize graph structures into natural-language descriptions for LLM agents, making the reasoning process sensitive to the ordering of structural information and thereby breaking the permutation-invariant nature of graphs. Second, incorporating increasingly large sampled neighborhoods leads to rapidly growing contexts. To address these challenges, this paper introduces a multi-agent agentic graph learning (i.e., MAAGL) framework. MAAGL partitions the graph into communities and assigns an independent agent to each community for region-specific specialization. MAAGL represents structural and semantic evidence separately. Structural evidence is summarized by a dynamically updated structural signature that is permutation-invariant and fixed in size, while semantic evidence is filtered to the top-k nodes ranked by relevance. Based on historical trajectories with similar signatures, agents estimate their confidence and trigger debate-style collaboration when needed. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets show that MAAGL outperforms SOTA AGL methods.

</details>


### [241] [CityPlanner: A Sandbox Agent for Executable Urban Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09578)
*Wentao Zhang,Jingyuan Wang,Zetong Zhou,Yifan Yang,Wenrui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CityPlanner是一个用于可执行城市规划的沙盒-智能体框架，提出统一的基于文件的UrbanSandbox环境，并引入原子任务强化学习（将长轨迹分解为BuildPlan初始构建和ImprovePlan反馈优化），在真实世界基准上优于启发式、任务特定RL和通用LLM智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市规划是现实世界中的空间优化问题，需要在大规模候选空间中根据成本和服务质量等实际目标选择可行动作。现有优化和强化学习方法对固定公式化问题有效，但通常依赖任务特定的表示和约束处理，缺乏通用性与可扩展性。

Method: 提出CityPlanner沙盒-智能体框架，包含：(1) UrbanSandbox，一种统一的基于文件的环境，智能体可检查任务文件、生成计划、运行评估器，并基于可执行反馈修订决策；(2) 原子任务强化学习，将长沙盒轨迹分解为用于初始构建的BuildPlan和用于反馈式改进的ImprovePlan，使学习更易处理。

Result: 在真实世界基准上的实验表明，CityPlanner在性能上持续优于启发式方法、任务特定RL和通用LLM智能体基线。消融实验验证了UrbanSandbox、原子任务RL和迭代部署各组成部分的贡献。

Conclusion: CityPlanner证明了基于可执行反馈的沙盒环境和原子任务分解能有效提升城市规划中智能体学习的泛化能力与性能，代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Urban planning is a real-world spatial optimization problem that requires selecting feasible actions from large candidate spaces under practical objectives such as cost and service quality. Existing optimization and reinforcement learning methods are effective for fixed formulations, but often depend on task-specific representations and constraint handling. We propose \emph{CityPlanner}, a sandbox-agent framework for executable urban planning. CityPlanner introduces \emph{UrbanSandbox}, a unified file-based environment where agents inspect task files, generate plans, run evaluators, and revise decisions based on executable feedback. To make learning tractable, we further propose atomic-task reinforcement learning, which decomposes long sandbox trajectories into \emph{BuildPlan} for initial construction and \emph{ImprovePlan} for feedback-based refinement. Experiments on a real-world benchmark show that CityPlanner consistently outperforms heuristic, task-specific RL, and general LLM-agent baselines. Ablations verify the contributions of UrbanSandbox, atomic-task RL, and iterative deployment. We release the code and dataset at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/co-agent-C1C8

</details>


### [242] [A Function-Space Approach to the Statistical Mechanics of Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09589)
*Yizhou Zhang,Weichen Wu,Lun Du,Zhengjie Miao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从统计力学视角在函数空间（而非参数空间）描述深度神经网络的宏观学习行为。将参数配置视为微观实现、函数及其动力学算子视为宏观变量；对均方误差损失，误差动力学由学习算子 M=JJ* 控制。结合条件随机动力学的动力学Boltzmann权重与参数空间态密度（其局部曲率定义统计算子 B），并积分掉局部涨落，得到涨落自由能 Φ_fluc(M;B)=σ_ξ²/2 log det(M⁻¹+B)+const。该自由能在谱固定时具旋转平稳性（当 [M,B]=0），通过将 M 的大特征值与 B 的小特征值配对而最小化，并对旋转失配产生局部恢复项。对 ReLU 型函数空间在温和稳定统计条件下有 B=σ_ξ² L* K L，其中 L 度量粗粒化二阶结构，故低 B 扇区对应低结构曲率，表明更倾向于沿平滑、数据自适应方向的更快弛豫。结论：函数空间是研究学习稳定集体组织的自然宏观层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在巨大参数空间中具有高度非线性动力学，却在宏观层面呈现规则行为。作者希望从统计力学视角建立学习过程的宏观描述，突破参数空间低维表示的局限，直接在函数空间分析集体组织的稳定性与弛豫规律。

Method: 采用统计力学框架：将参数配置类比为微观态，函数及其动力学算符类比为宏观变量。针对均方误差损失，推导误差动力学由学习算子 M=JJ* 支配；将条件随机动力学的动力学Boltzmann权重与参数空间态密度（其局部曲率定义统计算子 B）相结合，并通过对局部涨落积分，得出涨落自由能解析式 Φ_fluc(M;B)=σ_ξ²/2 log det(M⁻¹+B)+const。随后分析该量的旋转对称性、特征值配对最小化以及旋转失配恢复项，并在 ReLU 型函数空间下推导 B 的具体形式。

Result: 得到完整的涨落自由能表达式；证明在固定谱下其旋转平稳条件为 [M,B]=0，最小化由 M 的大特征值与 B 的小特征值配对实现，且局部存在恢复项对抗旋转失配。对 ReLU 型空间，在稳定统计条件下 B=σ_ξ² L* K L，L 表征粗粒化二阶结构；由此低 B 扇区等价于低结构曲率，对应沿平滑、数据自适应方向的更快弛豫。

Conclusion: 函数空间可作为学习过程中稳定集体组织的自然宏观层次；该框架揭示学习弛豫偏好与函数结构曲率之间的内在联系，为深度学习的宏观统计力学理论提供基础。

Abstract: Deep neural networks exhibit regular macroscopic behavior despite highly nonlinear dynamics in vast parameter spaces. We develop a statistical-mechanical description of learning directly in function space, treating parameter configurations as microscopic realizations and functions with their dynamical operators as macroscopic variables. For mean-squared loss, the exact error dynamics are governed by the learning operator \(M=JJ^\ast\). Combining the dynamical Boltzmann weight of the conditional stochastic dynamics with the parameter-space density of states, whose local curvature defines a statistical operator \(B\), and integrating over local fluctuations yields
  $$ Φ_{\mathrm{fluc}}(M;B)=\frac{σ_ξ^2}{2}\log\det(M^{-1}+B)+\mathrm{const}. $$
  At fixed spectrum, this term is rotationally stationary when \([M,B]=0\), is minimized by pairing large eigenvalues of \(M\) with small eigenvalues of \(B\), and generates a local restoring contribution against rotational mismatch. For ReLU-type function spaces under mild stable statistical conditions, \(B=σ_ξ^2L^\ast\mathcal K L\), where \(L\) measures coarse-grained second-order structure. Thus the low-\(B\) sector corresponds, up to bounded anisotropy of \(\mathcal K\), to low structural curvature, implying a preference for faster relaxation along smooth, data-adaptive directions. These results identify function space as a natural macroscopic level for studying stable collective organization in learning.

</details>


### [243] [From State Synchronization to Cognitive Self-Evolution: An Operational Architecture for Cognitive Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09625)
*Haoran Gao,An Li,Zhen Li,Jun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对认知数字孪生（CDT）研究中缺乏系统认知集成的问题，提出一种四层CDT架构（物理层、数字孪生层、认知层、任务层），建立自进化闭环运行回路，支持用户请求驱动和自驱动两种运行模式，并通过轻量仿真验证了在有限语义信息下闭环任务可行性和任务经验带来的运行效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CDT研究多聚焦于单一使能技术（如学习模块、知识图谱、大语言模型），缺乏对认知能力如何系统性集成到数字孪生架构中的深入洞察。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供一个结构化的CDT架构框架。

Method: 提出四层CDT架构：物理层、数字孪生层、认知层和任务层。架构建立四层间的自进化闭环运行回路：物理状态同步至数字表示，认知层通过知识、记忆和注意力构建任务特定认知模型，在现实约束下生成任务级决策；运行反馈进一步优化认知经验并更新数字表示中的关系和注解。基于该框架表征两种运行模式（用户请求驱动认知和自驱动认知），并讨论语义通信、知识查询、任务编排和闭环同步等关键使能机制与部署挑战，最后通过轻量仿真验证方案。

Result: 轻量仿真研究表明：在有限语义信息条件下，闭环任务可行性得到有效保障；通过累积任务经验，系统运行效率得到显著提升。

Conclusion: 该四层CDT架构为未来CDT系统的设计与开发提供了结构化基础，能够支持任务解释、发起和推理随系统运行持续进化，为认知能力系统化集成到数字孪生架构中提供了可行的参考框架。

Abstract: As Digital Twin (DT) systems evolve beyond state synchronization toward task-oriented and knowledge-driven operation, Cognitive Digital Twins (CDTs) have emerged as an extension that incorporates cognitive capabilities into twin operation. Existing CDT studies often focus on specific enabling techniques, such as learning modules, knowledge graphs, and large language models, while providing limited insight into how cognition can be systematically integrated into DT architectures. To address this issue, this paper proposes a four-layer CDT architecture consisting of the physical layer, digital-twin layer, cognitive layer, and task layer. The proposed architecture establishes a self-evolving closed operational loop spanning these four layers, in which physical states are synchronized into digital representations, cognition constructs task-specific cognitive models through knowledge, memory, and attention, and task-level decisions are generated under practical constraints. Operational feedback further refines cognitive experience and updates relationships and annotations in the digital representation, enabling subsequent task interpretation, initiation, and reasoning to evolve with system operation. Based on this framework, two representative operation modes are characterized: user-request-driven cognition and self-driven cognition. We further discuss key enabling mechanisms and deployment challenges associated with semantic communication, knowledge querying, task orchestration, and closed-loop synchronization. A lightweight simulation study illustrates reliable closed-loop task feasibility under limited semantic information and improved operational efficiency through accumulated task experience. The proposed framework provides a structured foundation for the design and development of future CDT systems.

</details>


### [244] [Seven Sources of Physical AI Capability Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09627)
*Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的能力形成来源分类框架，识别出七种非排他性来源（记录经验、预测建模、评估交互、代理环境、机制锚定、具身耦合、进化驱动），通过重构归纳和理论饱和方法验证了该框架能解释所有49条证据记录，并主张在限定范围内达到理论饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于形态、架构、学习算法、任务或领域的分类法无法直接回答是什么因素产生了某种能力。作者希望区分“能力表现相似”与“能力形成方式相似”，从而更好地支持解释、迁移、复制、依赖关系分析等。

Method: 采用重构归纳（reconstructive induction）与理论饱和（theoretical saturation）方法。先建立研究矩阵并追溯至原始研究，去重文献，制定编码规则，然后进行三轮最大差异抽样和反面案例抽样。挑战对象包括课程学习、自监督学习、主动推理、开放式发展学习、规划与搜索、神经符号架构、数字孪生、生成式物理世界模型、形态-控制协同设计等。截止2026年9月4日，共分析49条证据记录。

Result: 所有49条证据记录均可由七种来源单独或组合解释，三轮挑战未产生不可约的第八种来源，第三轮也未要求新的核心定义或实质性边界规则。因此作者主张在所设定范围内达到理论饱和，但不声称逻辑完备性或对未来覆盖的穷尽。

Conclusion: 该框架能区分观察到的能力相似性与形成过程相似性，可用于分析解释、迁移、复制、依赖关系、治理证据以及地缘经济基础。理论饱和仅在特定范围内成立，并非绝对完备。

Abstract: Capabilities relevant to Physical AI can arise from materially different formation histories, yet existing taxonomies organized by morphology, architecture, learning algorithm, task, or domain do not directly answer what gives rise to a capability. We define a capability-formation source as a factor materially contributing to capability formation, distinct from components or construction steps. We identify seven non-exclusive sources: Recorded-Experience (RE), Predictive-Modeling (PM), Evaluative-Interaction (EI), Surrogate-Environment (SE), Mechanism-Grounded (MG), Embodied-Coupling (EC), and Evolution-Driven (ED) Formation. Using reconstructive induction with theoretical saturation, we traced a research matrix to primary studies, deduplicated the literature, set coding rules, and conducted three rounds of maximum-difference and negative-case sampling. Challenges included curriculum and self-supervised learning, active inference, open-ended and developmental learning, planning and search, neuro-symbolic architectures, digital twins, generative physical world models, and morphology-control co-design. Within the scope and criteria fixed as of September 4, 2026, all 49 evidence records were explainable by the seven sources individually or in combination. No R1-R3 challenge produced an irreducible eighth source, and R3 required no new core definition or substantive boundary rule. We therefore claim theoretical saturation within the stated scope, not logical completeness or exhaustive future coverage. The framework distinguishes similarity in observed capability from similarity in how it was formed, supporting analysis of explanation, transfer, replication, dependencies, governance evidence, and geoeconomic foundations.

</details>


### [245] [RobustSGPO: Search-Space Control for Agent Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09646)
*Zibo Zhao,Jijun Shi,Mo Zhou,Zhongyuan Wang,Shifu Bie,Yunfei Zhang,Xuanting Zhou,Xiangyu Wu,Bin Liu,Ruiming Tang,Wenwu Ou,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RobustSGPO，一种基于语义梯度的提示优化方法，通过明确指定编辑请求、构造并校验补丁，以及从当前最优解或保留快照继续搜索，解决了传统 SGPO 在编辑范围与操作选择上的局部更新缺陷。实验在 AgentX 头脑风暴工作流中，以 120 个任务、95 次运行和 7,350 次候选尝试为基准，验证了权限调度、累积控制与任务族迁移的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础 SGPO 虽然利用执行反馈改进智能体流程，但其局部更新规则无法有效解决编辑范围（edit scope）和编辑操作（operation）的选择问题，导致优化在搜索空间中受限，难以稳定收敛到高质量解。

Method: RobustSGPO 的核心机制包括三点：1) 显式指定所请求的编辑内容；2) 构造补丁并进行校验检查；3) 在搜索过程中既可继续从当前最优解迭代，也可从保留的候选快照恢复搜索，从而拓宽搜索空间并避免陷入局部最优。

Result: 实验结果显示：周期性的 1→2→3 权限调度比固定最大权限高出 0.28 个测试分数点；在 2000 万 token 预算下，RobustSGPO 将 30 个留出任务的完成率从 60.0% 提升至 80.0%，测试质量从 3.77 提升至 4.14。类别保留机制降低了源任务退化，而随机保留则能达到更高的目标端点；搜索空间控制通过可执行的编辑与备选起点提升了质量，但引入了可测量的保留开销。

Conclusion: RobustSGPO 通过在请求规格、补丁校验与快照回溯三方面增强 SGPO，显著提升了智能体工作流的完成率与输出质量。权限的周期性调度优于静态最大权限设定；保留策略的选择需在源任务稳定性与目标任务性能之间权衡，搜索空间控制则提升了编辑的可执行性与解的多样性。

Abstract: Semantic-gradient-based prompt optimization (SGPO) improves agent harnesses using execution feedback, but its local update rule leaves the choice of edit scope and operation unresolved. We introduce RobustSGPO, which specifies the requested edit, constructs and checks the patch, and continues search from either the incumbent or retained snapshots. We evaluate permission scheduling, cumulative controls, and task-family transfer in the AgentX brainstorming workflow using 120 tasks, 95 runs, and 7,350 candidate attempts. Periodic $1\to2\to3$ scheduling exceeds fixed maximum permission by 0.28 test-score points. RobustSGPO increases completion on 30 held-out tasks from 60.0% to 80.0% and improves test quality from 3.77 to 4.14 under a 20-million-token budget. Category retention reduces source-task degradation after a shift, whereas random retention reaches a higher destination endpoint. Search-space control benefits quality through executable edits and alternative starting points, with measurable retention overhead.

</details>


### [246] [Black-Box Red Teaming of Agentic AI: A Taxonomy-Driven Framework for Automated Risk Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09647)
*Divyanshu Kumar,Nitin Aravind Birur,Tanay Baswa,Sahil Agarwal,Prashanth Harshangi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个系统性的黑盒框架SAGE-RT用于风险感知的智能体（Agent）评估，该框架包含七领域风险分类、每领域120个自动化对抗场景以及人工验证的LLM评估。在CrewAI和AutoGen两种架构、四种基座模型上验证发现：治理风险平均56.25%，多智能体配置下隐私风险达65%，行为漏洞高达85%，仅凭基础系统描述即可识别关键架构漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统正快速走向生产环境，会读取不可信输入、以真实权限调用工具并自主行动，其安全攻击面远超聊天式模型。然而现有标准评估仍停留在单轮对话层面，无法捕捉多步智能体漏洞，因此需要新的系统性评估框架。

Method: 提出黑盒评估框架，包含三大组件：(1) 七领域风险分类法，将可观测行为映射到风险类别；(2) 全自动SAGE-RT红队测试，每个领域生成120个对抗场景；(3) 基于LLM裁判的人工验证评估。仅需基础系统描述即可运行，无需特权访问。

Result: 在CrewAI和AutoGen两种智能体架构与四种基座模型的实证验证中，发现警示性模式：治理风险平均56.25%，多智能体配置下隐私风险达65%，智能体行为漏洞高达85%。黑盒方法无需特权访问即可有效识别关键架构漏洞。

Conclusion: 该黑盒评估框架提供了一种可扩展的路径，能够在无需特权访问的情况下识别关键架构漏洞，为更安全的智能体部署提供支撑。研究凸显了多智能体配置中治理与隐私风险的严重性，呼吁业界重视智能体安全评估的多步、多域与自动化特征。

Abstract: Agentic systems are rapidly moving to production, where they read untrusted inputs, call tools with real permissions, and act autonomously, expanding the security surface beyond chat-only models. Yet standard evaluations remain single-turn and fail to capture multi-step agent vulnerabilities. We present a systematic black-box framework for risk-aware agent evaluation requiring only basic system descriptions. Our approach introduces: (1) a seven-domain taxonomy mapping observable behaviors to risk categories, (2) fully automated SAGE-RT red teaming producing 120 adversarial scenarios per domain, and (3) human-validated evaluation using LLM judges. Empirical validation across two agent architectures (CrewAI and AutoGen) with four base models reveals alarming patterns: 56.25\% average governance risk, 65\% privacy risk in multi-agent configurations, and agent behavior vulnerabilities reaching 85\%. Our black-box approach effectively identifies critical architectural vulnerabilities without privileged access, providing a scalable path toward safer agent deployments.

</details>


### [247] [RESCUE-BENCH: Towards Relation-Aware Multi-Party Emotional Support Conversation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09657)
*Haichuan Hu,Yang Xiao,Mingni Tang,Jiawen Duan,Quanjun Zhang,Congqing He,Hao Zhang,Jiashuo Wang,Johan F. Hoorn,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出关系感知的情感支持对话任务，并构建了RESCUE基准（来自真实夫妻/家庭访谈视频，含191个样本、7,079个标注轮次、1,064.8分钟视频），定义六项任务评估关系理解与关系敏感支持两大核心能力。实验表明10个LLM在依赖局部情感/干预线索的任务上表现较好，但在关系模式预测、观点预测和支持策略预测等关系密集型任务上表现欠佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感支持对话系统大多聚焦一对一的求助者-支持者交互及个体情感状态，忽略了多人场景中的人际关系动态。作者认为能否捕捉并利用关系演变对提供有效情感支持至关重要，但该方向尚未被充分探索。

Method: 从真实夫妻及家庭访谈视频构建RESCUE基准，包含191个样本、7,079个标注轮次和1,064.8分钟视频；基于社会情感与支持相关动态的丰富标注，定义六项任务，划分为关系理解与关系敏感支持两大核心能力；用10个LLM进行系统实验评估。

Result: 现有多数LLM在依赖局部情感或干预线索的任务上表现相对较好，但在关系模式预测、观点预测、支持策略预测等关系密集型任务上表现困难，暴露出LLM在人际关系统建模及关系敏感决策方面的局限。

Conclusion: 当前LLM难以充分建模多人交互中的人际关系动态，也难以为关系敏感的情感支持做出有效决策；RESCUE可作为评估与推动该能力发展的新基准。

Abstract: Existing emotional support conversation systems mainly focus on one-on-one seeker-supporter interactions and individual emotional states, leaving interpersonal relations in multi-party scenarios underexplored. In this work, we introduce relation-aware emotional support conversation, a new task that evaluates whether LLMs can capture and utilize the evolving dynamics of relationships to offer more effective emotional support. We construct RESCUE (Relation-aware Emotional Support Conversation Understanding and Evaluation Benchmark) from real couple and family interview conversations, containing 191 samples, 7,079 annotated turns, and 1,064.8 minutes of video. Based on rich annotations of socio-emotional and support-related dynamics, RESCUE defines six tasks that evaluate two core capabilities required for relation-aware emotional support: Relational Understanding and Relation-Sensitive Support. Experiments with ten LLMs show that current models perform relatively well on tasks relying on local emotional or intervention cues, but struggle with relation-intensive tasks such as relation pattern prediction, viewpoint prediction, and support strategy prediction. These findings reveal the limitations of current LLMs in modeling interpersonal relations and making relation-sensitive support decisions.

</details>


### [248] [PRAGMA: Evaluating Personalized Guidance with Memory Alignment in Lifelong Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09664)
*Hyojeong Yu,Hyukhun Koh,Minsung Kim,Yunah Jang,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PRAGMA基准，用于评估大语言模型在长期对话中的个性化指导能力。该基准包含纵向对话历史、证据标注和基于不断演变的用户情境及错误假设的指导场景。实验发现现有检索系统、记忆系统和长上下文模型在恢复对话证据及运用证据进行个性化推理方面均存在困难，凸显了超越简单证据回忆的记忆架构需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益被部署为长期交互的个性化助手，但长上下文带来计算开销并难以持续定位最相关信息。现有对话记忆评估主要聚焦于检索与事实回忆，忽视了在实际交互中用户更常寻求推荐、规划、决策支持等需要跨多轮对话整合信息并推理用户动态偏好与经验的实用指导。因此需要一个专门评估个性化指导能力的基准。

Method: 引入PRAGMA基准，包含精心策划的纵向对话历史、证据标注，以及基于不断演变的用户情境和错误用户假设的指导场景。通过检索系统、记忆系统与长上下文模型的对比实验进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，当前系统既难以恢复合适的对话证据，也难以有效利用证据进行个性化指导，即在会话检索与基于记忆的推理两个环节均表现不佳。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明需要设计支持稳健对话检索和记忆接地推理的记忆架构，而不仅仅是证据回忆；个性化指导应是长期对话记忆评估的重要维度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as personalized assistants that interact with users over extended periods of time. As conversations grow longer, relying on full interaction histories becomes increasingly inefficient and unreliable: long contexts introduce substantial computational overhead, making it difficult for models to consistently identify and utilize the most relevant information for the current request. These challenges have motivated memory systems that structure and retrieve user-specific information. In realistic interactions, users often seek practical guidance such as recommendations, planning, and decision support. Unlike factual recall tasks, personalized guidance requires models to integrate information across multiple past conversations and reason about changing user preferences and experiences. However, existing conversational memory evaluations mainly focus on retrieval and factual recall. To study this challenge, we introduce PRAGMA, a benchmark for evaluating personalized guidance in long-term conversations. PRGAMA contains curated longitudinal conversation histories, evidence annotations, and guidance scenarios grounded in evolving user contexts and incorrect user assumptions. Experiments across retrieval systems, memory systems, and long-context models reveal that current systems struggle both to recover the appropriate conversational evidence and to effectively use it for personalized guidance. Our results highlight the need for memory architectures that support robust conversational retrieval and memory-grounded reasoning beyond evidence recall.

</details>


### [249] [Safe to Stop? Risk-Constrained Stopping for Sequential Clinical Diagnosis Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09678)
*Yuexin Wu,Vasile Rus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Cros，一个风险约束的停止决策层，用于临床诊断智能体在面对"何时检测、何时诊断、何时推迟"时提供有限样本担保的自主停止方案。通过对选择性诊断错误和最小自主覆盖率进行 LTT 风格的精确检验，在 MIMIC 腹部疼痛基准上验证了其探索性可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床诊断智能体不仅要决定下一步请求哪项检测，还要决定何时该做出诊断或推迟决策。现有智能体基准大多只评估固定或不受约束交互后的准确性，将自主停止的可靠性视为隐含假设，缺乏对停止时机的系统性担保。

Method: Cros 是一个风险约束的停止层，包含三个组成部分：按状态排序的错误排名（state-wise error ranking）、在不重叠的开发数据划分上设计策略、以及采用 LTT 风格的精确检验对完整顺序策略的选择性诊断错误和最小自主覆盖率进行检验。其有限样本保证要求候选策略族、检验规则及任何随机化在访问校准标签之前全部冻结。

Result: 在 1,834 个 episode 的 MIMIC 腹部疼痛基准上，完整排序器的探索性状态错误 AUROC 为 0.853，优于最大类概率的 0.715 和骨干网络原生停止分数的 0.552。在已查看的 367-episode 评估划分上，对冻结权重解析平均后得到 16.9% 选择性错误、78.8% 覆盖率、成本 5.57、0.68 次检测，对比原生停止的 30.8% 错误、100% 覆盖率、成本 8.14、1.53 次检测。强制继续为非单调的（HPI 单独时 28.3% 错误，完整检查后 34.3%）。然而均匀权重混合作消融在该划分上更便宜，且 Cros 仅在 20 次开发重划分中的 6 次名义上满足联合标准。

Conclusion: 由于评估标签在早期开发中已被查看，上述结果属于探索性可行性和审计证据，而非确证性安全证书。这项工作为临床诊断智能体的自主停止可靠性提供了风险约束的框架性方案，并揭示了其局限性与开发过程中的审慎考量。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis agents must decide not only what test to request next, but also when to diagnose or defer. Existing agent benchmarks largely evaluate accuracy after fixed or unconstrained interaction, leaving autonomous stopping reliability implicit. We present Cros, a risk-constrained stopping layer combining state-wise error ranking, policy design on disjoint development splits, and LTT-style exact tests of selective diagnostic error and minimum autonomous coverage for complete sequential policies. Its finite-sample guarantee requires the candidate family, testing rule, and any randomization to be frozen before calibration labels are accessed. On a 1,834-episode MIMIC-derived abdominal-pain benchmark, the full ranker achieves exploratory state-error AUROC 0.853, compared with 0.715 for maximum class probability and 0.552 for the backbone's native stop score. On the previously viewed 367-episode evaluation split, analytically averaging over the frozen Cros weights yields 16.9% selective error at 78.8% coverage, cost 5.57, and 0.68 tests, versus 30.8% error at 100% coverage, cost 8.14, and 1.53 tests under native stopping. Forced continuation is non-monotone: error is 28.3% with HPI alone and 34.3% after full workup. However, the uniform-weight mixture ablation is cheaper on this viewed split despite missing the locked development margins, and Cros nominally satisfies the joint criterion in only 6 of 20 development resplits. Because evaluation labels were inspected during earlier development, these findings provide exploratory feasibility and audit evidence, not a confirmatory safety certificate.

</details>


### [250] [Decision Shifts, Lost Label Functionality, and an Inconclusive Grounding Audit in Correctness-Gated Multi-Teacher Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09702)
*Xiaofei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在固定实验矩阵中检验“正确性加权多教师蒸馏”与“理由(证据)基础”的分离：加权蒸馏相比未过滤蒸馏在准确率、macro-F1上提升，条件不安全率下降，但代价是标签功能丧失（零Refuted召回、多数样本被归为NotEnoughInfo）；可用的人类审计因非配对、源重叠未序列化等原因无法证明共同源下的理由基础增益，因此加权臂未表现出对硬过滤的增量决策收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出决策正确性与理由基础（rationale grounding）是不同的优化目标，作者通过固定实验系统评估“正确性门控多教师蒸馏”的效果，希望厘清这类方法是否真正改善推理的证据基础，而不仅是提高决策指标。

Method: 固定实验：八组共享4,330个来源、63.9M参数学生、12,990优化行、406次更新、证据输入和解码器；七组教师臂使用固定三响应池。比较未过滤蒸馏与正确性加权蒸馏（以及源标签SFT、硬过滤等）在267个留出样本上的三种子表现，统计指标包括准确率、macro-F1、条件不安全率；另进行一次“可用性修正”的人类审计。

Result: 正确性加权臂相对未过滤蒸馏：准确率+0.1660（95%CI [0.0670,0.2455]）、macro-F1+0.1323（[0.0916,0.1731]）、条件不安全率-0.4979（[-0.5926,-0.3686]）。但加权臂在所有种子中Refuted召回率为零，两个种子将所有167个声称示例标为NotEnoughInfo。源标签SFT取得最高macro-F1（0.586）。可用性审计（参考种子）显示加权臂0/20证据支持正例，未过滤1/20；加权臂20/20含未支持材料，未过滤19/20。审计因样本非配对、源重叠未序列化、修正顺序问题而无法估计共同源下的理由基础效应。

Conclusion: 正确性加权蒸馏虽然改善了决策指标，但以损失标签功能为代价；现有审计无法证明理由基础提升，因此该加权臂相对硬过滤（0.660准确率、0.530 macro-F1、0.135条件不安全率）没有展示出增量决策收益，整体属于失败分析——决策再分配而非真正的理由基础改进。

Abstract: Candidate decision correctness and rationale grounding are different objectives. We examine correctness-gated multi-teacher distillation in a fixed experiment. Eight arms share 4,330 sources, a 63.9M-parameter student, 12,990 optimization rows, 406 updates, evidence inputs, and a decoder; seven teacher-based arms use one fixed three-response pool. Three seeds are evaluated on 267 held-out examples. Relative to unfiltered distillation, the correctness-weighted arm differed in accuracy by +0.1660 (95% observed-matrix interval [0.0670, 0.2455]), five-label macro-F1 by +0.1323 ([0.0916, 0.1731]), and task-defined conditional unsafe-action rate by -0.4979 ([-0.5926, -0.3686]). These shifts do not imply uniformly better behavior. Source-label SFT had the highest mean macro-F1 (0.586). The weighted arm had zero Refuted recall in every seed, and two seeds assigned NotEnoughInfo to all 167 claim examples. In an availability-amended audit at one reference seed, weighted and unfiltered outputs had 0/20 versus 1/20 evidence-supported positives and 20/20 versus 19/20 positives containing unsupported material. Samples were non-paired, source overlap was not serialized, and the amendment followed automatic summarization but preceded annotation. The audit therefore cannot estimate a common-source grounding effect and is inconclusive about system-level improvement or harm. Hard filtering already achieved 0.660 accuracy, 0.530 macro-F1, and 0.135 conditional unsafe rate. The implemented weighted arm showed no demonstrated incremental decision benefit over hard filtering. This fixed-matrix failure analysis shows decision redistribution with lost label functionality; the available human audit does not establish a grounding gain.

</details>


### [251] [Which Tokens Should SFT Actually Learn? A Token-Trimming Perspective on Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09707)
*Yaning Jia,Chunhui Zhang,Wenxuan Xu,Xingjian Diao,Xiaoyuan Wang,Soroush Vosoughi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TrimSFT（Trimmed Logit-Gap SFT），一种基于 logit 间隙（金标 token 与其最强竞争者之间的输出概率差）的简单 token 级重加权方法，从两侧修剪监督信号：既去除已掌握 token（logit 间隙大）的过度训练，也去除当前模型弱支持 token（logit 间隙小或负）的学习压力，将学习集中在一个中间 logit 间隙区间。在 Llama、Qwen、DeepMath 三个系列的六个基础模型及五个数学推理基准上，TrimSFT 一致优于标准 SFT，在五个模型中取得最佳平均性能，MATH500 上相对 SFT 最高提升 +26.9 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 SFT 对所有目标 token 施加统一的交叉熵损失，但不同 token 对数学推理提供的学习信号并不相等。这种统一处理会对已掌握 token 造成过度锐化（over-sharpening），同时对不确定、低置信度的 token 放大学习压力，导致次优的训练动力学。作者旨在通过按 token 学习状态差异化分配损失权重来改善推理 SFT 的训练效果。

Method: 提出 TrimSFT，一种 token 级损失重加权方法：以金标 token 与其最强竞争者之间的 logit 间隙为度量，从两个极端修剪监督——已掌握（间隙大）与弱支持（间隙小或负）的 token，将学习集中于两者之间的中间 logit 间隙区域。权重由以间隙 m 为中心、带宽 τ 的高斯函数实例化，无需参考模型或额外前向传播。

Result: 在 Llama、Qwen、DeepMath 系列的六个基础模型与五个数学推理基准上，TrimSFT 一致优于标准 SFT，在六分之五的模型上取得最佳平均性能，MATH500 上相对 SFT 提升最高达 +26.9 分。进一步分析表明带宽 τ 比精确的中心位置 m 更重要；仅修剪一侧的半修剪变体产生较差的权衡；token 级 logit 间隙分布分析显示 TrimSFT 比统一 SFT 或单调重加权方法更均衡地重塑了模型置信度。

Conclusion: 推理 SFT 可以受益于对两个极端（已掌握与弱支持的 token）进行修剪，而非对所有 token 统一施加损失。TrimSFT 通过无需参考模型的简单高斯加权机制，实现了更均衡的置信度重塑和一致的性能提升，为推理任务的有监督微调提供了新的训练动态学视角。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) applies a uniform cross-entropy loss to all target tokens, even though different tokens provide unequal learning signals for mathematical reasoning. This uniform treatment can over-sharpen already mastered tokens while amplifying learning pressure on uncertain, low-confidence tokens, leading to suboptimal training dynamics. We propose Trimmed Logit-Gap SFT (TrimSFT), a simple token-level reweighting method that scales the SFT loss according to the logit gap between the gold token and its strongest competitor. TrimSFT trims supervision away from both extremes: tokens already mastered (large logit gap) and tokens weakly supported by the current model (small or negative logit gap), concentrating learning within an intermediate logit-gap region between them. We instantiate this principle with a Gaussian weight centered at margin m with bandwidth τ, requiring no reference model or additional forward pass. We evaluate TrimSFT on six base models from the Llama, Qwen, and DeepMath families across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks. TrimSFT consistently improves over standard SFT, achieving the best average performance on five out of six models, with gains of up to +26.9 points over SFT on MATH500. Further analyses show that the bandwidth τ matters more than the exact margin location, and that half-trim variants that remove supervision pressure from only one side yield inferior trade-offs. A token-level logit-gap distribution analysis suggests that TrimSFT reshapes model confidence in a more balanced way than uniform SFT or monotonic reweighting methods. These results suggest that reasoning SFT can benefit from trimming both extremes rather than treating all tokens uniformly.

</details>


### [252] [Can Artificial Intelligence Support Healthcare and Mental Health Through Early Cyberbullying Detection ? The Impact of Emotion-Aware AI on Proactive Online Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09735)
*Hamed Jelodar,Amir Firouzi,Yen-Wu Lo,Maryam Tanha,Sajjad Dadkhah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CareGuard，一个面向医疗健康场景的网络欺凌早期预警框架，结合零样本语义标注与微调Transformer模型（BERT、DistilBERT、RoBERTa）实现分类，并引入情感感知过滤与余弦相似度语义筛选机制，在保持检测精度的同时提升计算效率，适用于医疗系统与心理健康监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络欺凌和不良在线互动日益影响医疗系统、心理健康与公众福祉，亟需在医疗驱动的心理健康保护场景中实现主动在线安全防护。现有模型在敏感分类中常面临效率和精度难以兼顾的问题，需要一种既能保证上下文感知分类能力又能在医疗监控场景中降低不必要计算开销的框架。

Method: CareGuard采用多阶段框架：(1) 零样本语义标签与微调Transformer模型（BERT、DistilBERT、RoBERTa）集成，实现敏感网络欺凌类别的鲁棒、上下文感知分类；(2) 引入情感感知过滤机制，优先处理情绪显著内容；(3) 结合余弦相似度语义筛选，聚焦语义相关内容，从而减少医疗监控场景中的无效计算。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验结果表明，CareGuard有效平衡了检测准确性与计算效率，证明了其在医疗系统、心理健康监测和在线安全应用中的可扩展部署潜力。

Conclusion: CareGuard为医疗健康场景下的网络欺凌检测提供了一种兼顾精度与效率的可行方案，通过语义与情感双维度筛选优化计算资源分配，有望在规模化部署中支持心理健康保护与主动在线安全保障。

Abstract: Healthcare systems, mental health, and public well-being are increasingly affected by cyberbullying and harmful online interactions. This paper presents CareGuard, an early-warning framework designed to support healthcare-driven mental health protection and proactive online safety through the detection of cyberbullying-related content using advanced natural language processing techniques. CareGuard integrates zero-shot semantic labeling with fine-tuned transformer-based models, including BERT, DistilBERT, and RoBERTa, to enable robust and context-aware classification across sensitive cyberbullying categories. To improve efficiency and reduce unnecessary computation in healthcare-oriented monitoring settings, the framework incorporates an emotion-aware filtering mechanism alongside cosine similarity-based semantic screening, allowing the system to focus on semantically relevant and emotionally salient content. Experimental results on benchmark datasets demonstrate that CareGuard effectively balances detection accuracy and computational efficiency, highlighting its potential for scalable deployment in healthcare systems, mental health monitoring, and online safety applications.

</details>


### [253] [LexAgentHallu: A Hierarchical Benchmark for Profiling Hallucinations in Legal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09754)
*Yujin Zhou,Mingxuan Zheng,Chuxue Cao,Huang Yidan,Jiale Chen,Yike Guo,Sirui Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LexAgentHallu，一个面向法律智能体的幻觉诊断基准，包含 3414 个实例、17 个法律类别和 6 种任务类型，采用 7 个高层级和 27 个细粒度的双层幻觉分类体系进行标注，并设计细粒度指标以量化失败程度并定位执行轨迹中的失败位置。对 18 个开源与专有智能体的评估揭示了"答案正确但理由错误"效应，并发现幻觉子类呈聚集而非分散分布，形成了不同的智能体框架、法律任务和类别特征画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的法律基准仅评估单轮问答且使用结果级指标，而智能体幻觉基准缺乏法律特有的诊断能力，均无法回答法律智能体在其多步执行轨迹中以何种程度和何种方式产生幻觉（如工具调用与推理错误级联导致虚构结论和错误引证），因此需要专门的法律智能体幻觉诊断基准。

Method: 通过四阶段专家参与（expert-in-the-loop）流水线构建基准，包含 3414 个实例、17 个法律类别和 6 种任务类型；采用双层幻觉分类体系（7 个高层级类别和 27 个细粒度子类），覆盖实体性错误和智能体程序性失败；设计能量化失败程度并沿执行路径定位失败位置的细粒度指标。

Result: 对 18 个专有和开源智能体的评估揭示了"Right-Answer-Wrong-Reason"（答案正确但理由错误）效应，并发现幻觉子类呈聚类而非分散分布，形成不同的智能体框架、法律任务和类别画像；这些发现无法通过结果级评估观察到。

Conclusion: 实验中发现的规律验证了 LexAgentHallu 在评估法律领域中智能体幻觉的诊断能力，说明该基准能捕捉到传统结果级评估无法揭示的失败模式，为法律智能体的幻觉诊断提供了有效的评估工具。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed as tool-augmented legal agents, they introduce agentic hallucinations where tool-call and reasoning errors cascade into fabricated holdings and miscited authority. However, existing legal benchmarks evaluate only single-turn QA with outcome-level metrics, while agentic hallucination benchmarks lack legal-specific diagnostic capability. Neither answers to what extent and how a legal agent hallucinates along its trajectory. To address these limitations, we introduce LexAgentHallu, a legal agentic hallucination benchmark designed to evaluate to what extent and how legal agents fail along multi-step trajectories. Built through a four-stage expert-in-the-loop pipeline, LexAgentHallu contains 3414 instances across 17 legal categories and 6 task types. Each instance is annotated under a dual-layer hallucination taxonomy of 7 high-level categories and 27 fine-grained subclasses, covering both substantive errors and agent-procedural failures. We further design fine-grained metrics that quantify to what extent and localize how each failure occurs along an agent's execution path. Our evaluation across 18 proprietary and open-source agents uncovers a Right-Answer-Wrong-Reason effect and reveals that hallucination subclasses cluster rather than scatter, forming distinct agentic framework, legal task, and category profiles. These findings, invisible to outcome-level evaluation, validate the diagnostic power of LexAgentHallu for evaluating agentic hallucination in law.

</details>


### [254] [Procedural Memory Under Change: Reuse and Interference in Controlled Web Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09774)
*Yanze Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究程序性记忆（procedural memory）在语言智能体中被"故意违背适用性假设"时的行为影响。通过回顾性分析（BrowserGym TimeWarp的人机协作接口适配案例）与受控冻结记忆比较（32个正式实验单元，使用本地qwen3:8b模型、温度0生成、无自适应重试），考察了四种失配形式（数量改变、证据表示不同、局部与全局优化冲突、分布式促销证据）。结果显示，在当前任务证据明确且充分时，预定义的干扰特征未在对应任务上出现，界定了一个"非干扰区域"——记忆失配但不产生行为破坏。研究未声称普遍安全性或机制解释，仅指出了待探索的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体依赖程序性记忆复用成功的例程，但复用隐含"存储的例程仍然适用"这一假设。作者想研究当该假设被故意违反时会发生什么，即探查"适用性失配"（applicability mismatch）是否会导致行为错误，从而评估程序性记忆复用的稳健性边界。

Method: 研究采用双轨设计：（1）回顾性、人机协作的界面适配案例（BrowserGym TimeWarp WebShop V1-V6开发路径），其中接口特定代码被适配而高层程序未报告改变，该阶段未被归为自主记忆智能体评估；（2）受控冻结记忆比较，基于合成购物决策任务。正式实验含32个细胞单元（4种失配形式×多任务），每个单元使用同一本地qwen3:8b配置、温度0单次生成、无自适应重试。预定义了诊断性干扰特征签名，检查其是否出现在对应任务上。

Result: 早期试点中有一个任务上两种记忆条件选择了更贵商品而无记忆条件选择参考最低价；后续探测未建立规律性的行序或身份绑定模式。正式32个单元中，当当前任务证据明确且充分时，任何一种预定义的诊断性干扰签名都未在其对应任务上出现。由此界定了一个经受测试的非干扰区域：记忆失配在既有条件下不产生行为层面的破坏。

Conclusion: 结论是：存在一个被实证测试的非干扰区域——程序性记忆可以在失配情况下不产生行为干扰，但该结果既不能确立普遍安全性，也不能给出机制解释。关键开放问题是：哪些额外条件会把适用性失配转变成可观测的、由记忆引起的错误。

Abstract: Procedural memory lets language agents reuse successful routines, but reuse presumes that a stored routine remains applicable. We study what happens when that presumption is deliberately violated. The study combines a retrospective, human-assisted interface-adaptation case from BrowserGym TimeWarp with controlled frozen-memory comparisons on synthetic shopping decisions. During the documented WebShop V1-V6 development path, interface-specific code was adapted while the separately stored high-level procedure was not reported to change; this phase does not constitute an autonomous memory-agent evaluation. In the controlled phase, an early pilot produced one task on which two memory conditions selected a more expensive item while the no-memory condition selected the reference minimum. Follow-up probes did not establish a recurring row-order or identity-binding pattern. We then tested four forms of mismatch: changed quantities, a different evidence representation, a conflict between local and global optimization, and distributed promotion evidence, across 32 formal cells. Each cell used one temperature-0 generation with the same local qwen3:8b configuration and no adaptive retry. Across these pairs, none of the predefined diagnostic interference signatures appeared on the tasks for which they were defined when current-task evidence was explicit and sufficient. The result identifies a tested region of non-interference: a procedural memory can be mismatched without becoming behaviorally disruptive. It does not establish general safety or a mechanism. The remaining question is which additional conditions turn applicability mismatch into observable, memory-caused error.

</details>


### [255] [Proof-Carrying Cognition: Closing the Verification Gap with Reality-Settled Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09776)
*Eshwar Reddy M,Sourav Karmakar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出语言模型推理能力的前沿提升依赖于对推理轨迹的强化学习，且集中于具有廉价、可靠验证器的领域。作者提出该领域的关键约束是"验证鸿沟"：在正式领域之外缺乏可扩展且不可腐败的推理奖励。文章做出四项贡献：建立了验证器-金标相关性与测试时计算/能力之间精确兑换率的理论模型；在多组实验中证明不可靠验证器在优化压力下丧失可靠性，而可靠验证器单调提升；提出"证明携带认知"的新范式；并设定了"压力下可靠性"基准指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型推理能力的前沿进展依赖强化学习，其收益集中在拥有廉价且可靠验证器的领域。但作者认为该领域的根本瓶颈在于"验证鸿沟"——在形式化领域之外，不存在可扩展、不可腐败（不会被对抗性剥削）的推理奖励信号。这一缺口制约了推理强化学习的可扩展应用，因此需要系统性理论化、实证化和方法论上的突破。

Method: 研究包含四项核心方法：(1) 理论层面，在联合高斯best-of-N选择模型中，证明验证器-金标相关性ρ是测试时计算与能力之间的精确兑换率，不可靠验证器付出的代价为多项式惩罚N^(1/ρ²)，并用无边际copula形式预测真实LLM裁判的实际可靠性（中位误差4%）；(2) 实证层面，在具有可执行真实标签的程序综合测试平台进行预注册的规模化复现，比较不可靠与可靠验证器在优化压力下的表现，并测试现实锚定结算与冻结验证器在i.i.d.与对抗压力下的差异；(3) 范式层面，提出"证明携带认知"，将推理步骤视为带类型的概率主张，由仅基于留出现实数据训练的自建世界模型定价，并用适当评分规则结算；(4) 基准层面，将"压力下可靠性"设定为现实结算推理基准的核心指标。

Result: 在程序综合测试中，不可靠验证器在优化增长时可靠性从0.94降至0.32（N=4096），而可靠验证器单调提升；现实锚定结算比冻结验证器在i.i.d.和对抗压力下更优，将漏洞鸿沟从约0.27压至约0；可靠性随结算标签对数线性扩展，在策略结算比随机标注约高效10倍。用真实LLM裁判配合单元测试执行作为金标时，弱裁判在best-of-N下丧失可靠性（p<0.001），强裁判更稳健，仅选择即可从诚实样本制造+0.53的漏洞鸿沟。在真实GRPO训练中，冻结奖励模型完整呈现过优化曲线（执行奖励崩塌90%），而对10%结算流重拟合的同一模型保留了6倍的执行奖励。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：解决"验证鸿沟"的关键在于用现实锚定的、基于适当评分规则结算的奖励机制替代不可靠的冻结验证器。"证明携带认知"范式与"压力下可靠性"基准为该问题提供了统一的方法论框架与评估标准，使推理强化学习的奖励信号可扩展且难以腐败，从而突破形式化领域的限制。

Abstract: Frontier gains in language-model reasoning come from reinforcement learning on reasoning traces and are concentrated in domains with a cheap, sound verifier. We argue the field's binding constraint is the verification gap: no scalable, incorruptible reward for reasoning outside formal domains. We make four contributions. (1) Theory: in a joint-Gaussian model of best-of-N selection, verifier-gold correlation rho is the exact exchange rate between test-time compute and capability, and an unsound verifier pays a polynomial penalty N^(1/rho^2); a margin-free copula form predicts realized soundness of real LLM judges to 4% median error. (2) Demonstration: in program-synthesis testbeds with executable ground truth, including a pre-registered scaled replication, unsound verifiers lose Soundness-under-Pressure as optimization grows (0.94 to 0.32 at N=4096) while a sound verifier improves monotonically; reality-anchored settlement beats a frozen verifier under i.i.d. and adversarial pressure, driving the hacking gap from ~0.27 to ~0; soundness scales log-linearly with settled labels, with on-policy settlement ~10x more label-efficient than random labeling. With real LLM judges and unit-test execution as gold, a weak judge loses soundness under best-of-N (p<0.001), a stronger judge is more robust, and selection alone manufactures +0.53 hacking gaps from honest samples. Under real GRPO training, a frozen reward model traces the full overoptimization curve (executed reward collapses 90%) while the same model refit on a 10% settlement stream preserves 6x the executed reward. (3) Paradigm: proof-carrying cognition, where reasoning steps are typed probabilistic claims priced by a self-built world model trained only on held-out reality and settled by proper scoring rules. (4) Benchmark: we specify Soundness-under-Pressure as the headline metric for a reality-settled reasoning benchmark.

</details>


### [256] [UnitBoost: Managing Compound LLM Systems with a Merge Operator, Not a Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09815)
*Xing Zhang,Guanghui Wang,Yanwei Cui,Mengdie Flora Wang,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UnitBoost，挑战复合LLM系统中高层生成式管理者（meta-agent）的必要性，主张用一个确定性的元级算子替代生成模型：任务给定的单元映射将工作者输出转化为槽-值提案，受约束的argmax组装输出，未填充或不支持的槽位作为显式残余进入下一轮。该算子具有顺序无关性、记录单元来源，并提供无耦合约束下单元级最大化支配完整候选选择的简单保证。在三个保留基准上超过黄金标签选出的最佳单候选0.060-0.195绝对任务分，并超过输入匹配的生成式管理者0.048-0.076。仅替换管理层即可提升六个复合系统配置0.013-0.182。残余引导轮次将FanOutQA细胞F1从0.4778提升至0.5524；对照实验证明真实残余优于随机目标和普通重读，标签无关的供给信号可在一次无产出轮后标记"消耗殆尽"。同时量化了三种无法获益的条件（不可分单元、单元身份不可得、每发射单元计费的端点）及跨单元耦合的修复代价。管理者以放弃语义自由换取顺序不变性、单元来源记录和可测试的失败条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合LLM系统通常通过添加高层LLM来解决协调问题，由此产生的元智能体读取工作者输出、撰写最终答案、分配后续调用并决定何时停止。虽然这种生成式设计表达力强，但它将三种控制决策集中在一次不透明、顺序敏感的模型调用中。作者质疑管理者是否根本不需要具备生成能力，希望通过定义性的元级算子消除这种不透明性。

Method: UnitBoost用确定性的元级操作符替代生成式manager模型：1) 任务给定的单元映射将工作者输出转化为槽-值提案；2) 受约束的argmax组装输出；3) 未填充或不受支持的槽构成显式残余，作为下一轮输入。该操作符是顺序无关（order-free）的，记录单元来源，并给出理论保证——在无耦合约束下，基于同一准入分数的单元级最大化支配任何完整候选的选择。文中还通过对标实验（对照真实残余、随机目标、普通重读）来验证残余引导的有效性，并用标签无关的供给信号检测资源耗尽。

Result: 在三个保留基准上：超过最优单个候选（黄金标签选择）0.060-0.195绝对任务分；超过输入匹配的生成式管理者0.048-0.076。仅替换管理层即提升六个复合系统配置0.013-0.182。残余引导的轮次将FanOutQA细胞F1从0.4778提升到0.5524。对照显示真实残余优于随机目标和普通重读；标签无关的供给信号在一次无产出轮后即可标记消耗殆尽。同时量测到三种无法获益的条件（不可分单元、单元身份不可得、每单元计费的端点）以及跨单元耦合的修复代价。

Conclusion: 生成式管理者并非复合LLM协调的必要条件。通过放弃语义自由，UnitBoost获得了顺序不变性、单元来源的可追溯性和可测试的失败条件。这项工作表明复杂协调本质上可被定义性算子与残余驱动机制取代，并且残余方向、供给信号和耦合成本都可以被量化，为可审计、可测试的复合LLM系统提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: Compound LLM systems often solve a coordination problem by adding a higher-level LLM. The resulting meta-agent reads workers' outputs, writes the final answer, allocates later calls, and decides when to stop. It is expressive, but it also concentrates three control decisions in an opaque, order-sensitive model call. We ask whether the manager needs to be generative at all. UnitBoost replaces that model with a defined meta-level operator: a task-given unit map turns worker outputs into slot-value proposals, a constrained argmax assembles the output, and the slots left unfilled or unsupported become an explicit residual for the next round. The operator is order-free, records unit provenance, and gives a simple guarantee: without coupling constraints, unit-wise maximization under the same admission score dominates selection of any complete candidate. On three held-out benchmarks, it exceeds the best single candidate chosen with gold labels by 0.060-0.195 absolute task-score points and input-matched generative managers by 0.048-0.076. Replacing only the management step improves six compound-system configurations by 0.013-0.182. Residual-directed rounds raise FanOutQA cell F1 from 0.4778 to 0.5524; matched controls show that the true residual outperforms random targets and ordinary rereading, while a label-free supply signal flags exhaustion after one unproductive round. The same analysis measures three conditions in which no such gain is available (one indivisible unit, unavailable unit identity, and an endpoint that charges for every emitted unit) and quantifies cross-unit coupling as a repair cost. The manager gives up semantic freedom and gains order invariance, unit provenance, and testable failure conditions.

</details>


### [257] [The Era by Eon Benchmark: A Generated Enterprise Estate with Exact Ground Truth for Benchmarking LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09853)
*Benjamin Gruenbaum,Doron Porat,Assaf Natanzon,Roy Zavida,Chen Dinachi,Or Itzahary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Era by Eon基准，用于评估使用企业工具的LLM代理，通过虚构公司、模拟器和内部数据库生成带真实答案的测试集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业系统记录上的LLM代理无法用客户生产数据评估，现有替代方案缺乏真实答案（ground truth）。

Method: 构建完整虚构公司，包括产品模拟器、公司内部数据库、基准问题和计算答案键。每个公司由行业、规模、业务模式、应用组合和种子定义。一个种子化实体图为Salesforce、Zendesk、Slack、Gong等模拟器提供共享数据。问题条件化生成器创建内部数据库的模式和记录，从同一图提取共享实体、键和值，从而保证一致性。答案从最终记录计算，实现精确评分，并通过设计和答案键检查验证内部数据库，真实度记分卡和对抗检测器验证实体图。

Result: 在23个生成公司中，平均真实度评分从61.8提升到97.0，零记录被标记为合成。模拟器跟踪对比中，九个模型对相同33个问题各回答三次，准确率估计范围42.4%–76.8%，36对两两差异中仅3对在校正后仍显著。

Conclusion: 该基准能够生成高度真实且一致的企业场景，提供精确答案键，可用于可靠评估LLM代理在企业工具上的表现，但模型间差异多数不显著，基准区分度仍需提升。

Abstract: LLM agents for enterprise systems of record cannot be evaluated on customer production data, and no existing substitute provides ground truth. We present the Era by Eon Benchmark for evaluating LLM agents that use enterprise tools. The benchmark is built around a complete fictional company. It includes product simulators, company-specific internal databases, benchmark questions, and computed answer keys. Industry, company size, business model, application portfolio, and a seed define each company. One seeded entity graph supplies shared company data to simulators of Salesforce, Zendesk, Slack, Gong, and other products. A questionconditioned generator creates the schemas and records for internal databases. It takes shared entities, keys, and values from the same graph before generating database-specific facts. Both mechanisms therefore describe one consistent enterprise estate. Every expected answer is computed from the final records, so grading is exact. Design and answer-key checks validate the internal databases. A realism scorecard and adversarial detector validate the entity graph. Across 23 generated companies, the mean realism score rose from 61.8 to 97.0, with zero records flagged as synthetic. In the reported simulator-track comparison, nine models answered the same 33 questions three times each. Accuracy estimates ranged from 42.4% to 76.8%, and three of 36 pairwise differences remained supported after correction.

</details>


### [258] [Shifting Relational Paradigms for Affective Computing: Affective Resonance, Vitality Affects, and Vocal Interaction Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09864)
*Cy Gorman,Yihang Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将情感计算的研究范式从"个体状态"转向"关系性框架"，以多方位对话中的声音动态构成的"互动场"作为情感分析的基本单元。通过连续自监督语音表征检测多方差对话中的方向性表达耦合，并以初步实验证明该耦合具有体制特异性、集中于亚秒级时间尺度且在单一说话人负对照中消失。论文还提出了基于情感共振动态本体论的"人工情感共振智能"设计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统情感计算沿袭个体状态范式，从孤立说话人中提取离散情感标签或唤醒度/效价。作者认为这一框架对互动场景而言是不完整的，因此主张借鉴情感共振（affective resonance）和活力轮廓（vitality-contour）理论，将情感分析的基本单位重新定义为由声音动态构成的互动场。

Method: 提出关系性情感计算框架，将互动场作为基本分析单元。作为概念验证，采用连续自监督语音表征（self-supervised speech representations）检测多方位对话中的方向性表达耦合（directional expressive coupling），并设置单一说话人负对照（exclusive-speech negative controls）进行验证。同时引入基于情感共振动态本体论（Affective Resonance Dynamic Ontologies）的设计框架，配合跨互动体制的零校准方向性耦合分析。

Result: 初步实证研究表明，方向性表达耦合具有体制特异性（regime-specific），集中在亚秒级时间尺度上，且在单一说话人负对照条件下消失，该结果与关系性情感动力学的理论预期一致。

Conclusion: 互动中的情感不应仅作为个体状态来处理，而应从关系性、动态的互动场角度理解。基于自监督表征的方向性耦合分析可为情感共振研究提供工具，并支撑人工情感共振智能（Artificial Affective Resonance Intelligence）的设计实现。

Abstract: Affective computing has largely followed an individual-state paradigm, extracting discrete emotion labels or arousal/valence from isolated speakers. We argue this framing is incomplete for interaction.
